[1] Psychophysics of Duration Perception in Multimodal Large Language Models cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.SD | eess.ASPDF

Auss Abbood, Zihao Fu, Nigel Collier

TL;DR: 论文从心理物理学角度评估多模态大语言模型的时长感知能力：将旋律识别作为任务，通过逐渐放慢旋律测试模型何时无法识别熟悉旋律，并与人类基线对比。研究发现多数模型在无标注上下文示例时无法识别旋律，有示例时部分模型可靠识别且峰值接近人类识别区间，但泛化受提示示例速度、旋律标题偏置和音高记谱示例影响，模型的时间感知窗口更多由提示决定而非模型自身属性。

Details

Motivation: 现有时序感知研究多集中于纯文本语言模型，而多模态模型越来越多用于需要感知时间流逝的任务，因此需要理解其时长感知的极限，并与人脑基线比较。

Result: 在21个测试模型中，多数无标注上下文示例时不能识别熟悉旋律；提供上下文示例后，7个模型能可靠识别，且多数识别峰值落在人类识别中位数区间内。泛化有限：识别峰值随示例速度偏移，旋律标题会引入预测偏置，科学音高记谱示例也不能阻止多数模型在长音符时长下准确率下降。

Insight: 创新点在于将心理物理学的时长感知范式转化为旋律减速识别任务，系统评测多模态LLM的时长感知极限，并揭示其识别窗口主要由提示/上下文决定，而非像人类那样是感知者自身的稳定属性。客观看，该工作提供了可复用的音频时长感知评测思路，并强调上下文示例对多模态时间感知评测的强影响。

Abstract: Studies of time perception in language models focus mainly on text-based settings. Yet multimodal models are increasingly used for tasks that require perceiving the passage of time, making the limits of their temporal perception important to understand. We measure the limits of duration perception from audio against a human baseline to address this gap. Drawing from psychophysics, we operationalise duration perception as a melody recognition task, testing when a melody becomes unrecognisable as it is slowed down. Of 21 tested models, most cannot recognise well-known melodies without labelled in-context examples. With in-context examples, seven recognise melodies reliably, most peaking within the median human recognition band. Generalisation is limited: (i) recognition peaks shift with in-context example speed, (ii) melody titles induce prediction bias, and (iii) providing examples with scientific pitch notation does not prevent accuracy declining at long note durations for most models. In humans, the recognition window is a property of the perceiver; in current models, it is largely a property of the prompt.

[2] Extraneous Cognitive Load in Large Language Models cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Arushi Gupta, Sheng Liu, James Zou

TL;DR: 论文借鉴认知负荷理论，构建CogLoadBench，系统隔离LLM中由任务呈现方式引起的外在认知负荷与任务难度带来的内在负荷，覆盖五种呈现因素和三类推理任务。研究发现高外在负荷常通过增加竞争或复杂化推理路径降低准确率；提出基于激活的ELS指标，并验证推理时表征偏移可降低负荷、提升平均性能。

Details

Motivation: 人类认知负荷理论表明任务呈现方式会影响解题，LLM也表现出类似效应，但此前未将外在负荷与任务难度系统分离。论文旨在区分并诊断LLM中的外在认知负荷，并探索缓解方法。

Result: 在CogLoadBench上跨模型发现，高外在负荷条件常降低准确率；ELS可跨推理任务类型和负荷水平泛化；推理时向低负荷方向移动表征可在不改变提示内容的情况下提升平均模型性能。

Insight: 创新点在于用受控benchmark将CLT中的呈现因素操作化为外在负荷变量，提出基于激活的prompt-time指标ELS，并展示推理时表征干预可缓解负荷效应。客观看，其价值在于分离呈现效应与任务难度，并提供可诊断、可干预的负荷视角。

Abstract: Cognitive Load Theory (CLT) shows that human problem-solving is affected by both intrinsic load, arising from task difficulty, and extraneous load, arising from how task information is presented. Large language models (LLMs) show analogous effects: presentation alone can affect model performance, but these effects have not yet been systematically isolated from task difficulty. To address this gap, we introduce CogLoadBench, a controlled benchmark that varies five CLT-inspired presentation factors across three reasoning task families. Across models, we find that higher extraneous load conditions often reduce accuracy by either adding competition or complicating the reasoning path. To support diagnosis and mitigation of load-related effects, we propose the Extraneous Load Score (ELS), a prompt-time metric computed from activations that estimates a model’s extraneous load, and show that it generalizes across reasoning task types and load levels. We further show that shifting model representations toward lower-load directions at inference time can improve average model performance without changing prompt content.

[3] Focus On Facts: Stylistically Invariant and Factually Sensitive Text Embeddings cs.CLPDF

Maximus Adeola

TL;DR: 论文关注文本嵌入模型将表面形式相似与事实一致性混淆的问题，构建了跨五领域、83,263组三元组的FOF-Bench，并微调80M参数嵌入模型FOF-80M，用硬负例对比学习使事实保持的改写优先于被篡改事实的近拷贝。作者将其定位为研究风格变化下事实保留的“软事实指纹”，而非通用事实性指标。

Details

Motivation: 现有嵌入模型存在表面相似性捷径：面对只篡改一个事实的近拷贝和不同措辞但事实保持的改写时，基座模型反而更偏好前者。论文试图通过硬负例对比监督缓解这一问题。

Result: 在8,327个留出测试三元组上，基座模型99.58%偏好事实篡改文本（事实偏好率0.42%，平均间隔-0.1650）；微调后平均正负间隔为+0.0329，98.5%样本间隔朝预期方向变化，事实偏好率升至29.37%。在DiSC外部风格迁移语料上，平均跨风格相似度从0.7905提升至0.9559；但对NLI、摘要等方向性任务迁移较差，且会惩罚信息省略。

Insight: 创新点在于构建事实保持改写与事实篡改近拷贝配对的三元组基准，并用硬负例对比目标显式惩罚“表面相似但事实改变”的负例。客观来看，其价值更多是揭示并量化嵌入模型中的表面相似捷径，提供可研究事实保留的软指纹，而非通用事实性度量。

Abstract: Text embedding models can conflate surface-form similarity with factual identity. Given an altered near-copy with one corrupted fact and a differently worded fact-preserving rewrite, the base model prefers the altered near-copy. We test whether hard-negative contrastive supervision can reduce this surface-similarity shortcut. We construct FOF-Bench, an 83,263-triplet benchmark spanning five domains. Each anchor is paired with an LLM-generated, fact-preserving stylistic rewrite and a matched fact-altered near-copy. We fine-tune FOF-80M (Focus On Facts), an 80M-parameter embedding model, with a triplet contrastive objective that ranks the rewrite above the factual alteration. On 8,327 held-out test triplets, the base model chooses the factually altered text in 99.58% of cases (0.42% factual preference rate; mean separation -0.1650). After fine-tuning, mean positive–negative separation is +0.0329, 98.5% of individual margins move in the intended direction, and the preference rate reaches 29.37%. On DiSC, an external style-transfer corpus, mean cross-style similarity increases from 0.7905 to 0.9559. The model also penalizes omission and transfers poorly to directional tasks such as NLI and summarization. We characterize FOF-80M as a soft factual fingerprint for studying factual retention under stylistic variation, not as a general-purpose factuality metric.

[4] Learning from Recurrence: Internalizing Depth-Induced Preferences in Looped Language Models cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Hui Chen, Joshua Elsdon, Can Goksen

TL;DR: 论文研究循环语言模型中共享块能否从更大循环深度产生的预测中学习。作者将预训练OLMo-2-1B改造为带共享循环块的模型，并在不同循环深度下继续中期训练；发现四遍推理的额外遍数主要在高排名token间重新分配概率。于是提出transfer preference write-back：用更深循环生成的续写训练共享块的单遍应用，无需参考答案或正确性过滤，从而把深度诱导的偏好内化。

Details

Motivation: 循环语言模型通过增加循环深度在相同参数下获得更多每token计算，但如何让共享块从自身更深层预测中受益仍不清楚。论文希望利用深度诱导的预测偏好作为自监督信号，提升单遍和多遍推理表现。

Result: Write-back在大多数任务上提升单遍和多遍准确率；在六个数学推理benchmark的目标深度上，更新模型均超过匹配中期训练数据和token预算的非循环控制，平均相对准确率增益最高达46.2%。四遍推理时，带输入注入的模型中单遍训练优于全深度训练，但该优势在无注入匹配模型中消失；GSM8K上仅在第2至4遍使用更新块即可恢复近全部四遍增益。

Insight: 创新点在于将循环深度视为自监督偏好来源，提出无需参考答案或正确性过滤的transfer preference write-back，把深层预测偏好写回共享块的单遍应用。客观看，论文还分析了额外循环主要重排高概率token，并揭示输入注入对单遍训练优势的影响，说明一次应用中学到的更新可改善同一块的后续递归应用。

Abstract: A looped language model repeatedly applies the same block to its hidden state before predicting each token. The number of passes is its recurrent depth. Increasing this depth gives the model more computation per token with the same parameters. Can the shared block learn from the predictions it makes at greater depth? Following prior work, we retrofit pretrained OLMo-2-1B with a shared recurrent block and continue mid-training over varying recurrent depths. In the four-pass model, additional passes mainly redistribute probability among tokens that already rank highly after one pass. We use the continuations generated at greater depth to train only the shared block through a single pass, without reference answers or correctness filtering. We call this transfer preference write-back. Write-back further improves one-pass and multi-pass accuracy on most tasks. At their target depths, the updated models outperform a non-recurrent control matched for mid-training data and token budget on all six mathematical reasoning benchmarks, with a mean relative gain in accuracy of up to 46.2% across tasks. At four-pass inference, one-pass training outperforms full-depth training on the same targets in the model with input injection. This advantage disappears in the matched model without injection. On GSM8K, using the updated block only on passes two through four recovers nearly the entire four-pass gain, showing that an update learned in one pass can improve later applications of the same block.

[5] Evaluating Large Language Models on conversational African American English: Feature classification and next-turn generation cs.CLPDF

Romeo De Timmerman, Thomas Demeester, Els Lefever

TL;DR: 论文在真实非裔美国人英语（AAE）对话数据上评估LLM，使用CORAAL中100段六轮对话，比较Gemini 2.5 Flash（含/不含推理）与GPT-4.1 mini。任务包括五个AAE形态句法特征的分类，以及生成下一轮对话并从AAE特征频率、多样性和语用质量评估。研究发现推理版Gemini检测最强，但所有模型在生成中均少用AAE且输出较单一，说明检测能力不等于生成中自发使用方言的能力。

Details

Motivation: 现有对LLM方言偏见的评估多依赖合成、风格化或去语境化数据，不能反映聊天机器人部署时遇到真实AAE对话的情况。论文希望用真实会话语料更贴近实际部署场景地评估LLM对AAE的理解与生成能力。

Result: 在AAE特征分类上，推理版Gemini取得最高macro F1 0.91，明显优于其他LLM和微调BERT基线。在下一轮生成中，所有LLM相对人类说话者都低估AAE特征，输出较同质化，其中推理版Gemini生成的AAE最少且多样性最低。

Insight: 创新点在于使用真实、多轮AAE会话语料，并设计“特征分类+下一轮生成”互补评估，揭示检测AAE能力与生成中自发使用AAE之间存在脱节。客观启发是：仅提升模型识别方言的能力不够，还需从预训练和对齐数据上游改善方言变体的代表性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are known to misinterpret and misrepresent regional and social varieties of English other than Mainstream American English (MAE), such as African American English (AAE), yet most evaluations of these biases have relied on synthetic, stylized or decontextualized data. This paper instead evaluates LLMs on authentic, conversational AAE to more closely reflect how such models may encounter AAE when deployed as chatbots. Concretely, we perform two complementary evaluation tasks on 100 six-turn segments randomly sampled from the Corpus of Regional African American Language (CORAAL; Kendall and Farrington, 2024), comparing three closed-weight LLM configurations: Gemini 2.5 Flash with and without reasoning, and GPT-4.1 mini. The first task compares the models against gold-standard manual annotations for five AAE morphosyntactic features, while the second assesses their ability to generate contextually appropriate next turns, evaluating outputs for AAE feature frequency, diversity (Self-BLEU), and pragmatic quality. Reasoning-enabled Gemini is the strongest feature detector by a clear margin (macro F1 of 0.91), outperforming both other LLMs as well as a finetuned BERT-based baseline (Masis et al., 2022). In generation, however, all LLMs underproduce AAE relative to the human speakers and produce relatively uniform continuations, with reasoning-enabled Gemini producing the least AAE and the least diverse output. We conclude that a model’s ability to detect AAE does not straightforwardly translate into its spontaneous use of AAE in generation, and argue that addressing this gap requires changes upstream, in how pretraining and alignment data represent dialectal variation.

[6] Sports Agent: A Self-Evolving System for Conversational Data Analytics cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Chao Chen, Fuqi Lin, Ze Chen, Ziyan Liu, Chen Li

TL;DR: 论文提出 Sports Agent，一个面向对话式体育数据分析的自进化系统，集成了 Agentic SQL 模型、规划与工具编排 harness，以及连接在线执行与离线进化的统一 Feedback Engine。系统通过评估回答与执行轨迹、事实校验和失败诊断生成进化信号，并结合问题合成、轨迹蒸馏、迭代监督微调与后 RL 来进化模型和 harness。实验表明其在体育问答与分析上优于 Qwen3.8-Max，并显著提升产品中的事实正确性、回答丰富度与推理效率。

Details

Motivation: 对话式体育分析需要准确、有证据且低延迟的回答，但查询分布、数据资源和模型行为变化会使静态 agent 配置性能退化，因此需要由运行时证据引导的可控进化。

Result: 基于 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 的模型在体育问答与分析上超过 Qwen3.8-Max，推理效率约为其 3 倍；30 轮 harness 进化使综合性能提升 10.8 分；在线产品中事实正确性提升 10.5 分，平均回答长度提升 158.1%，平均延迟降低 41.3%。

Insight: 创新点在于将模型进化与 harness 进化统一到在线反馈闭环中，并用 SQL-to-Question Scaling、Graph-Guided Scaling、轨迹蒸馏与后 RL 提升覆盖和纠错能力。客观看，其可借鉴之处是受限且可审计的自我进化机制，包括独立审核、执行回放、回归测试和性能容忍度检查，以避免简化或更新导致性能退化。

Abstract: Conversational sports analytics requires accurate, evidence-based responses with low latency. Changes in query distributions, data resources, and model behavior can degrade static agent configurations, motivating controlled evolution guided by runtime evidence. We present Sports Agent, a self-evolving framework integrating an Agentic SQL Model for structured-data reasoning, a harness for planning and tool orchestration, and a unified Feedback Engine connecting online execution with offline evolution. The engine assesses responses and execution trajectories, verifies factual claims, diagnoses failures, and generates evolution signals. Model evolution combines SQL-to-Question Scaling for coverage expansion with Graph-Guided Scaling for error-targeted synthesis. Generated questions undergo trajectory distillation and quality filtering for iterative supervised fine-tuning, followed by a separate Post-RL stage. Harness evolution translates recurrent failures into bounded prompt and skill updates, validated through independent audits, execution replay, and regression testing. Performance-tolerance checks guard against unacceptable degradation during simplification. The resulting Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-based model outperforms Qwen3.8-Max on sports question answering and analysis while achieving approximately three times the inference efficiency. Across 30 harness-evolution rounds, the aggregate performance score improves by 10.8 points. In a live sports analytics product, the evolved system improves factual correctness by 10.5 points and average response length, a proxy for answer richness, by 158.1%, while reducing average latency by 41.3% relative to the baseline.

[7] NORMCRAFT: Cultural Norm Generation for Context-Aware Language Model Alignment cs.CLPDF

Tangyuan Zhang, Zhuang Li, Fatemeh Shiri, Xiangyu Zhao, Junyu Yan

TL;DR: 论文提出 NORMCRAFT，一个将社会规范推断与下游回复生成解耦的模块化框架。它通过以规范为中心的多视角监督训练 LLM 作为规范生成器，从预期、遵守和违反场景中恢复共享规范，再用生成的规范指导一个独立且冻结的回复生成器，从而提升文化上下文感知的语言模型对齐。

Details

Motivation: 文化对齐要求 LLM 从场景和文化背景中推断往往隐含的社会规范。现有方法多通过适配单个模型或提供外部文化知识，而独立学习规范推断并与下游回复生成解耦的研究仍不充分。

Result: 以 Qwen3.5-4B 作为回复生成器时，NORMCRAFT 在五个下游指标中的四个上优于提示和监督微调基线；在 SafeWorld 上，其 Coverage 和 Faithfulness 分别超过 Responder SFT 18.0 和 14.1 个百分点。直接规范评估也显示其规范质量高于基础生成器和少样本生成器，且同一生成器可提升覆盖五个模型家族、0.8B 至 27B 参数的十四个冻结 LLM 的文化对齐，小模型增益最大。

Insight: 创新点在于将规范推断与回复生成解耦，并用无需下游回复标签的规范中心多视角监督训练规范生成器；客观来看，这种可插拔的规范生成模块能在不微调回复模型的情况下提升多类冻结 LLM 的文化对齐能力，尤其有利于小模型。

Abstract: Cultural alignment requires large language models (LLMs) to infer often-implicit social norms from a scenario and its cultural context. Existing approaches typically adapt individual models or supply external cultural knowledge, while learning norm inference independently of downstream response generation remains underexplored. We introduce NORMCRAFT, a modular framework that decouples norm inference from response generation. Through norm-centric multi-view supervision, we train an LLM as a norm generator to recover shared norms from prospective, adherence, and violation scenarios without downstream response labels. The generated norms guide a separate, frozen LLM (the responder). With Qwen3.5-4B as the responder, NORMCRAFT outperforms prompting and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baselines on four of five downstream metrics. On SafeWorld, its responses reflect the reference norms more often (Coverage) and more precisely (Faithfulness), exceeding Responder SFT by 18.0 and 14.1 percentage points, respectively. Direct norm evaluation also shows higher norm quality than the base and few-shot generators. The same trained generator improves cultural alignment on SafeWorld across fourteen frozen LLMs spanning five families and 0.8B to 27B parameters, with the largest gains for the smallest models.

[8] Multimodal Safety Evaluation of Vision-Language Models in Japanese cs.CLPDF

Su Myat Noe, Hisami Suzuki, Kouta Nakayama, Naoaki Okazaki

TL;DR: 论文将多模态安全测试套件 MSTS 扩展到日语，构建了包含 320 个机器翻译并后期编辑的测试样例、参考答案以及 82 张文化本地化日语图像的数据集。作者提出新的评测框架，用 5 分制同时衡量安全性与有用性，并将图像识别错误与安全判断分离，进而评估五个日语视觉语言模型和两个 LLM-as-a-Judge 模型。

Details

Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型在日语场景中应用增多，但多模态安全评估主要集中于英语，缺乏日语及文化本地化的安全测试资源与更细粒度的评估方法。

Result: 在日语多模态安全测试上，五个现代 VLM 的安全准备程度差异显著；LLM-as-a-Judge 提供参考答案可提升与人工安全判断的一致性，但检测不安全回答的召回率仍有限，说明自动化多模态安全评估仍较困难。

Insight: 创新点在于日语及文化本地化多模态安全数据构建、引入奖励有用性的 5 分制安全评估，以及将图像识别与安全判断解耦以隔离识别错误；同时验证参考答案对 LLM 评判器的帮助与局限。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in Japanese-language settings, yet multimodal safety evaluation has been largely English-centric. We extend the Multimodal Safety Test Suite (MSTS) of Röttger et al. (2025) to Japanese as the 12th language, contributing 320 machine-translated and post-edited cases with reference answers plus 82 culturally localized Japanese images. We introduce an evaluation framework that (i) extends MSTS’s 3-class safety grouping with a 5-point scale that also rewards useful (not just safe) responses, and (ii) separates image recognition from safety judgment to isolate recognition errors. Using this framework, we evaluate five modern Japanese VLMs (Gemini-3-Flash, GPT-5, InternVL3, LLM-JP-4-VL, Qwen3-VL) against human evaluation, and find substantial variance in the safety readiness across the models. Finally, we evaluate two LLM-as-a-Judge models and find that providing a reference answer improves agreement with human safety judgments, though recall for detecting unsafe responses remains limited, underscoring the difficulty of automating multimodal safety evaluation. The dataset created for our study will be released upon publication.

[9] Pitfalls of Dependency-Ready Decoding for Masked Diffusion Language Models cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Lina Berrayana, Christopher Malon, Yoichi Ishibashi, Taichi Aida, Masafumi Oyamada

TL;DR: 论文从实践和理论两个角度审视掩码扩散语言模型中的“依赖就绪解码”（dependency-ready decoding），即先揭示某token的依赖再对其进行解码。实践上，作者分析了一个通过注意力实现依赖就绪的近期解码器，发现其优于置信度或未来效用基线时，解码顺序往往坍缩为从左到右。理论上，作者提出不依赖注意力的信息论依赖度量，并证明当预测margin较大时，Maximum-Margin解码已经会提交依赖就绪的token。因此，依赖就绪解码的潜在收益范围很窄，未来工作应质疑对注意力的依赖。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注掩码扩散语言模型中token解锁顺序的自由度，检验“先解决依赖再解码”这一直觉是否真能提升准确率，并质疑近期基于注意力实现依赖就绪的解码策略。

Result: 实验分析发现，基于注意力的依赖就绪解码器在优于置信度或未来效用基线时，其解码顺序会退化为从左到右解码；理论证明当margin相对于信息论敏感度较大时，Maximum-Margin解码已提交依赖就绪token，使该策略的收益仅限窄范围。

Insight: 论文的创新在于用实践分析揭示依赖就绪解码的顺序退化现象，并提出不依赖注意力的信息论依赖定义与理论边界，说明未来依赖就绪解码器的潜在收益有限且应重新审视注意力信号的使用。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) have shown strong performance on structured reasoning tasks such as Sudoku, Trip Planning, and Countdown. MDMs introduce a freedom in the order in which tokens may be unmasked, and to exercise this freedom for good accuracy, early decoding strategies relied on signals of confidence or the downstream impact of decoding a token. However, simple examples seem to illustrate that resolving a token’s dependencies before unmasking it should be important. Therefore, we examine whether this principle, which we call dependency-ready decoding, would contribute to better accuracy, from a practical and a theoretical perspective. We examine a recent decoder that implements dependency-readiness through attention, and generally find that when it performs better than confidence-based or future utility baselines, its decoding order collapses to left-to-right decoding. For theoretical analysis, we establish a definition of dependency without resorting to attention, measured by an information-theoretic score of how much revealing a single masked token (or a set) would shift the model’s prediction for another. We prove that when the margin is large relative to this score, no such revelation can change the top prediction, so in this regime Maximum-Margin decoding already commits dependency-ready tokens. These findings leave only a narrow scope for future dependency-ready decoders: their potential benefit is limited to positions with margins smaller than the information-theoretic sensitivity, and reliance on attention should be questioned.

[10] ChemDeduce: Benchmarking LLMs for Unknown Chemical Identification through Interactive Simulated Experiments cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Hengyi Cai, Yuqi Tang, Zichang Li, Huajun chen, Qiang Zhang

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM能否通过主动实验识别未知化学物质，并提出ChemDeduce交互式评测框架。该框架将化学鉴定建模为顺序决策过程，提供有状态、带仪器和操作约束的模拟实验室，支持可扩展、可复现的迭代实验推理评估。作者对8个前沿LLM在200种化学品上进行基准测试，并评估识别准确率、操作合规性、交互效率和轨迹级推理质量。

Details

Motivation: 现有LLM在化学知识和推理上表现较强，但其能否解决必须通过实验发现答案的问题仍不清楚。论文聚焦未知化学物识别，要求模型主动设计实验、解释观测、更新假设并最终识别隐藏物质。

Result: 论文在200种化学品、1600个模型-目标对和4800个episode上评测8个前沿LLM，平均识别准确率为23.83%至67.33%。结果暴露出模型在实验选择、证据解释和顺序假设细化方面存在明显局限。

Insight: 创新点在于将化学鉴定形式化为顺序决策任务，并构建带显式仪器与操作约束的有状态模拟实验室进行交互式评测。其可借鉴之处是结合识别准确率、操作合规、交互效率和轨迹级推理质量，对LLM迭代实验推理能力进行多维度、可复现评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance in chemical knowledge and reasoning, yet it remains unclear whether they can solve problems whose answers must be discovered through experimentation. We investigate this question through Unknown Chemical Identification, where a model must actively design experiments, interpret observations, update hypotheses, and ultimately identify a hidden substance. We introduce ChemDeduce, an interactive evaluation framework for assessing LLMs through simulated experiments. ChemDeduce formulates chemical identification as a sequential decision process and provides a stateful simulated laboratory with explicit instrument and operational constraints, enabling scalable and reproducible evaluation of iterative experimental reasoning. We benchmark eight frontier LLMs on 200 chemicals, yielding 1600 model–target pairs and 4800 episodes over three independent repetitions, and evaluate identification accuracy, operational compliance, interaction efficiency, and trajectory-level reasoning quality. Despite generating seemingly plausible experimental plans, current models achieve an average identification accuracy ranging from 23.83% to 67.33%, revealing substantial limitations in experiment selection, evidence interpretation, and sequential hypothesis refinement.

[11] JHealthBench-L: Clinician-Reviewed Localization of HealthBench for Rubric-Based Medical LLM Evaluation in Japan cs.CLPDF

Shohei Hisada, Keisuke Orimo, Kenji Igarashi, Kota Sato, Masahiro Kazama

TL;DR: 论文针对HealthBench等基于评分标准的医疗LLM评测基准存在的地域偏差问题，通过LLM起草、专家修订、双医师独立审核和元审等多阶段流程，构建了面向日本的本地化基准JHealthBench-L（含79个面向患者的问诊案例和1022条标准）。作者将它与纯机器翻译的基线在18个LLM上进行了对比，发现翻译版评测虽然保持了粗粒度的模型排名，却掩盖了条目级的临床误判。

Details

Motivation: 现有HealthBench等评分基准预设了其来源地区（如美国）的医疗制度、药物和诊疗路径，仅靠机器翻译会将这些地域假设保留在标准中，使在日本临床上合理的回答得不到认可或被误判，因此需要在不从零撰写评分标准的前提下把成熟的临床评测视角本地化到日本。

Result: 专家仅需从零重写4.4%的标准，93.6%的二阶段标准在独立审核中无异议通过；与机器翻译基线在18个LLM上对比，模型排名相关性τ=0.817、平均分数偏移+0.027，但17.7%的修改标准判定发生翻转（而重新评测带来的噪声仅为7.3%），日本适配负向标准的误罚减少7.1个百分点。分析显示，情境意识和日本特异性临床标准仍是主要瓶颈，日语继续预训练与推理后训练能显著提升本地化表现。

Insight: 创新点在于提出“本地化而非重写”的基准构建范式（带源引用的LLM起草、专家最小修订、多级医师审核），并通过翻译基线诊断揭示出稳定排行榜背后被掩盖的条目级临床误测问题；其思路可借鉴为用机器翻译基线来量化评测基准的地域偏差。

Abstract: Rubric-based benchmarks such as HealthBench evaluate large language models (LLMs) on open-ended health consultations using case-specific physician criteria, many of which presuppose the healthcare institutions, medications, and care pathways of their source region. Under machine translation alone, these regional assumptions remain in the rubric, leaving responses appropriate in Japan uncredited or falsely penalized under foreign clinical rules. To adapt established clinical evaluation perspectives to Japan without authoring rubrics from scratch, we construct JHealthBench-L (79 patient-facing consultations, 1,022 criteria) through source-cited LLM drafting, single-specialist selection and revision, independent dual-physician review, and meta-review. Specialists rewrote only 4.4% of criteria from scratch, and independent reviewers approved 93.6% of Stage-2 criteria without issue. Comparing JHealthBench-L with a machine-translated baseline across 18 LLMs reveals that translation-only evaluation preserves coarse model rankings ($τ= 0.817$, mean shift +0.027) yet flips verdicts on 17.7% of modified criteria (vs. 7.3% re-grading noise) and reduces false penalties on Japan-adapted negative criteria by 7.1 percentage points, masking item-level clinical mismeasurement behind stable leaderboards. Axis-level and paired analyses further show that context awareness and Japan-specific clinical criteria remain primary bottlenecks, whereas Japanese continual pretraining and reasoning post-training substantially improve locally grounded performance. Datasets, physician revision logs, and evaluation code are available at https://github.com/horshohei/JHealthBench-L.

[12] Too Smart for Its Own Good? Cognitive Fidelity and Competence Leakage in LLM Simulations of Young Learners cs.CLPDF

Zhen Xu, Beata Beigman Klebanov, Jamie N. Mikeska

TL;DR: 论文关注基于LLM的交互式学习者模拟器中的认知保真度问题，提出“能力泄漏”现象：模拟学习者可能表现出超出其设定认知水平的知识或解题能力。作者分析130段教师-学习者模拟对话中的3339条话语，发现过度能力是认知画像错位的主要方向，并在五种模型配置和四种匹配触发评估协议下检验能力泄漏案例的复发情况。

Details

Motivation: LLM学习者模拟器被用于教师培训和辅导系统开发，要求模拟学习者持续保持指定的知识、误解和推理过程；但LLM可能“过于聪明”，泄露超出设定学习者认知状态的能力，从而损害模拟可信度。

Result: 在3个学习场景、130段对话和3339条模拟学习者话语中识别出过度能力导致的认知画像错位；多轮交互协议下能力泄漏违规复发率高于孤立问答，能力更强的模型通常复发更低，而增加推理努力未稳定提升认知保真度，在多轮协议下甚至略增复发。

Insight: 论文的贡献在于明确提出并实证探索LLM学习者模拟中的“能力泄漏”与认知保真度问题，并指出仅靠单轮问答评估不足，需发展系统可靠的多轮评估协议；其匹配触发和多轮复发评估思路对模拟器可信度评测有借鉴意义。

Abstract: LLM-based interactive learner simulators are increasingly used in educational applications, including teacher training and tutoring system development. A critical requirement for those simulations is cognitive fidelity: the simulated learner should maintain the assigned knowledge, misconceptions, and reasoning procedures throughout interaction. However, LLMs may express knowledge or problem-solving skills beyond the assigned learner’s cognitive status, a phenomenon we refer to as competence leakage. In this exploratory study, we examine 3,339 simulated learner utterances from 130 teacher-learner simulator transcripts across three learning scenarios and identify over-competence as a prominent direction of cognitive-profile misalignment during interaction. We then investigate the recurrence of previously observed competence leakage cases under four matched-trigger evaluation protocols, ranging from isolated questioning to multi-turn interactions, across five model configurations. Overall, we observe higher violation recurrence under multi-turn protocols than under isolated question answering. More capable models generally show lower recurrence, whereas increased reasoning effort does not consistently improve cognitive fidelity and is even associated with slightly higher recurrence under multi-turn protocols in our matched-model comparison. These findings provide exploratory evidence of competence leakage phenomena in interactive learner simulation and suggest that single-turn question-answering type of evaluations alone may be insufficient, motivating the development of more systematic and reliable multi-turn protocols for evaluating cognitive fidelity in learner simulation.

[13] Reliable User Simulation in Complex Multi-Step Workflows cs.CLPDF

Pusala Pavan Teja, Jayasimha Talur, Saab Mansour, Marcello Federico

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM用户模拟器在复杂多步工作流中的可靠性，指出现有模拟器会偏离目标、未穷尽选项且受助手回复风格影响。作者构建了500个基于HTN抽象的工作流基准，并提出ReUST提示策略，通过跨对话轮维护用户状态来提升模拟保真度。

Details

Motivation: LLM用户模拟器常用于评估对话助手，但其目标漂移或探索不充分会把模拟器错误归因给助手；已有工作只关注单任务目标对齐，缺少对多步工作流、回退逻辑和跨任务依赖场景下模拟器保真度的测量。

Result: 实验表明模拟器保真度随工作流复杂度显著下降，目标漂移是主要失败原因，且同一模拟器会因助手回复风格不同而评分差异明显；ReUST使中大型模型的目标成功率达到提升超过12个百分点，并降低模拟器对助手回复风格的敏感性。

Insight: 创新点在于构建复杂多步工作流的HTN基准来系统测量用户模拟器保真度，并提出简单有效的ReUST提示策略，通过跨轮用户状态跟踪缓解目标漂移和风格敏感问题。

Abstract: LLM-based user simulators are increasingly used to evaluate conversational assistants. Yet, these simulators can drift from their assigned goals or fail to exhaustively explore all specified options, causing evaluation errors incorrectly attributed to the assistant. Prior work has examined goal alignment in single-task settings, but many practical scenarios require simulators to execute multi-step workflows with sequential tasks, fallback logic, and cross-task dependencies, where simulator fidelity has not been measured. To close this gap, we introduce a benchmark of 500 workflows represented using the Hierarchical Task Network (HTN) abstraction. Our experiments reveal that the simulator’s fidelity degrades sharply with workflow complexity, and that goal drift accounts for the majority of failures. Fidelity also depends on the assistant’s response style, with the same simulator scoring substantially better or worse across styles. To address this, we propose ReUST (Reasoning + User State Tracking), a simple prompting strategy to maintain state information across dialogue turns. ReUST improves goal success rate by more than 12 points for mid-sized and large models, and reduces simulator sensitivity to the assistant’s response style.

[14] Familiar Words, Unfamiliar Minds: Conventionalization without Calibration in the Vocabulary of Mind cs.CLPDF

Matthieu Queloz

TL;DR: 论文讨论将“心智词汇”用于语言模型时出现的语义变化问题，区分“常规化”与“校准”：词语可迅速变得不再像隐喻，但其引发的推断未必被新指称对象验证。作者以“hallucinate”的语料库研究说明常规化可在三年内大部分完成，并通过Transformer导航机制的案例分析指出语言模型与人类心智差异大、校准难以自动跟上，进而主张通过互惠的语言分工和“针对性限定”来组织校准。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注用旧有心智词描述语言模型等新技术时，隐喻和语言变化理论常混淆的两个过程：词语的常规化与其推断含义的校准。核心问题是：心智词汇用于语言模型后，其适用条件和隐含推断难以判断，且这种校准差距可能不会自行闭合。

Result: 语料库研究显示，“hallucinate”在8,568篇摘要和31,866条论坛评论中的常规化在三年内大部分完成；但校准未必同步。机制分析案例表明，一个被训练导航曼哈顿的Transformer拥有并使用忠实的街道地图，却在长距离绕行中丢失位置感；论文未报告基准性能或SOTA比较。

Insight: 创新点在于明确区分常规化与校准，并用语料证据和机制案例说明二者可脱钩；进一步指出语言模型不透明、快速变化且卷入话语循环，使传统校准路径受限，因此提出互惠语言分工、针对性限定规范及三个可检验预测。

Abstract: Speakers describe new technologies by stretching old words, letting experience of the new referents sort out which inferences carry over. I argue that stretching the vocabulary of mind to language models pulls apart two processes that theories of metaphor and linguistic change tend to conflate: conventionalization, whereby a stretched word ceases to be marked as such, and calibration, whereby the inferences it invites are fitted to what its new referents bear out. As a gene variant can reach fixation without being an adaptation, a word can become conventional without being calibrated. A corpus study of “hallucinate” in 8,568 abstracts and 31,866 forum comments shows conventionalization running most of its course within three years. Calibration need not keep pace: these models are organized so unlike human minds that it is hard to tell when mental words apply and what they imply. Even warranted attributions can mislead: mechanistic analysis shows that a transformer trained to navigate Manhattan has and uses a faithful street map, yet loses track of its position on long, diverted journeys. Nor is the gap likely to close by itself. The usual routes to calibration (direct experience, designers’ knowledge, experiment) presuppose a referent that is accessible, stable, and independent of the discourse about it. But language models are opaque, fast-changing, and looped into the discourse: they are trained on past uses of mental vocabulary and on the experimental literature about them, and join the discourse themselves. Calibration must therefore be organized, through a reciprocal, not merely deferential, division of linguistic labour. This yields a norm of targeted qualification (flag the specific inferences an attribution invites without support), a reply to the worry that meaning change eludes deliberate control, and three testable predictions about when qualifications calibrate expectations.

[15] Lightweight Reasoning Transfer via Chain-of-Thought Vector Projection cs.CLPDF

Yulin Qi, Shuo Xu, Shuxia Lin, Qingjian Ni, Xu Yang

TL;DR: 论文提出一种两阶段跨模型Chain-of-Thought向量迁移方法：先在大模型教师中学习任务级CoT向量，再训练轻量线性投影器将其映射到小模型学生的残差空间。教师和学生模型均保持冻结，学生侧仅用少量样本的最终答案交叉熵进行适配，从而以较低监督成本提升小模型推理能力。

Details

Motivation: 从大语言模型向小模型迁移推理能力通常依赖大量教师监督或昂贵的学生适配。论文希望避免教师输出和中间解题轨迹，仅通过轻量投影器复用教师侧推理向量，降低推理迁移成本。

Result: 在GSM8K和MATH上，投影向量使基础学生模型在不同师生设置下提升10.37-21.98个准确率点；在四个代码生成基准上，Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct学生提升3.23-4.67个pass@1点，基础Qwen2.5-7B学生最高提升9.65个点。

Insight: 创新点在于将教师侧任务级CoT向量作为可复用的推理表示，并通过仅训练线性投影器实现跨残差维度的小模型适配，学生侧只需最终答案监督。客观来看，该方法把推理迁移简化为向量投影与注入层/尺度选择，具有轻量、冻结模型和低监督的特点。

Abstract: Transferring reasoning capabilities from large language models to smaller ones often requires extensive teacher supervision or costly student adaptation. We study projected cross-model Chain-of-Thought (CoT) Vector transfer, a two-stage approach that keeps both models frozen. First, we learn a task-level CoT Vector in the teacher using dataset-provided solution traces and final-answer supervision. We then train a lightweight linear projector to map the fixed vector into a smaller student’s residual space. Student-side training uses only final-answer cross-entropy on a small subset of task training examples; it requires neither teacher outputs nor intermediate solution traces. We select the student injection layer and intervention scale on a labeled support set and report results on the disjoint remainder of each evaluation split. On GSM8K and MATH, the projected vector improves base students by 10.37-21.98 accuracy points across the evaluated teacher-student settings. On four code-generation benchmarks, the Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct student gains 3.23-4.67 pass@1 points, while the base Qwen2.5-7B student gains up to 9.65 points. These results show that a teacher-side reasoning vector can be reused across smaller students with different residual dimensions through projector-only adaptation.

[16] IRTIQA: Evolving in English, Evaluated in Urdu – Does Agent Self-Evolution Transfer from English to Urdu and Code-Switched Input? cs.CLPDF

Abdullah Hashmat, Danish Javed, Usman Naseem, Agha Ali Raza

TL;DR: 论文提出并发布 IRTIQA 基准，包含 DROP、SNIPS、AppWorld 和 SkillsBench 的英语原版、乌尔都语翻译版及乌尔都语代码切换版，用于检验 LLM 智能体的自进化能力能否从英语迁移到乌尔都语和代码切换输入。作者评估了 Tool-R0、AgentEvolver 和 Autogenesis 三种自进化框架及其未进化骨干模型，发现自进化通常能提升任务能力，但很少缩小语言差距，甚至可能对乌尔都语推理造成更大损害。

Details

Motivation: 当前 LLM 智能体越来越多地从自身经验中学习，但训练和评估任务几乎都以英语为主。论文关注自进化能力是否能迁移到乌尔都语及英语-乌尔都语代码切换输入，并指出语言公平性不会仅靠自我改进自然出现。

Result: 自进化能提升能力：AgentEvolver 将 AppWorld 目标完成率从接近零提升到所有条件下 20-36%，Autogenesis 将 SkillsBench 通过率从 16.48% 提升到 22.22%。但进化后的系统在英语上仍更强，DROP F1 差距最高达 0.26，多步任务差距最大，代码切换表现为独立条件而非退化的翻译版本；在共享 Gemma-4 31B 骨干上，差距在进化前已存在，基于 RL 的进化对乌尔都语推理的损害可能大于英语。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于构建了对齐的英语、乌尔都语翻译和乌尔都语代码切换三版本基准，并采用矩阵语言代码切换流程与人工验证。客观来看，其重要贡献是揭示了智能体自进化带来的能力提升并不自动转化为跨语言迁移，且代码切换应作为独立评估条件，语言对齐需要作为显式设计目标。

Abstract: As LLM agents increasingly learn from their own experience, the tasks they learn from and are evaluated on are almost always in English. We ask whether self-evolution transfers from English to Urdu, and release IRTIQA, a benchmark of aligned English, Urdu-translated, and Urdu code-switched variants of DROP, SNIPS, AppWorld, and SkillsBench, built with a matrix-language code-switching pipeline and human verification. Using IRTIQA, we evaluate three self-evolving frameworks, Tool-R0 (self-play RL), AgentEvolver (experience-guided RL), and Autogenesis (protocol-level evolution), against their unevolved backbones. Self-evolution often raises capability: AgentEvolver lifts AppWorld goal completion from near zero to 20-36% across all conditions, and Autogenesis improves SkillsBench pass rate from 16.48% to 22.22%. But it rarely closes the language gap. Evolved systems remain stronger in English, by up to 0.26 DROP F1, the gap is largest on multi-step tasks, and code-switching behaves as a distinct condition rather than degraded translation. On a shared Gemma-4 31B backbone, the gap exists before evolution, and RL-based evolution can hurt Urdu reasoning more than English. Language parity must therefore be an explicit design target, as it does not emerge from self-improvement alone.

[17] Puzzling-Bench: Evaluating Language Models on 23,777 Human-Written Puzzles with Community-Verified Answers cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

TL;DR: 论文介绍了 Puzzling-Bench，一个包含 23,777 个来自 Puzzling Stack Exchange 的人类编写谜题的基准，答案经过社区验证，涵盖谜语、密码、逻辑网格等多种类型，三分之一含图像。评估了九个前沿和开源模型，使用双层评分器，最佳模型 GPT-6.1 在 255 个谜题上达到 74.8%，并分析了模型失败相关性、多模态影响等。

Details

Result: 最佳模型 GPT-6.1 在 255 谜题集上达到 74.8%；17 个难题击败所有模型；模型失败高度相关；oracle 集成达到 91%；多模态试点中不提供图像导致最佳模型损失 24 和 35 分。

Insight: 创新点包括大规模社区验证谜题基准、包含提示和元数据支持消融分析、污染检查的滚动切片、双层评分器和多评审验证、多模态试点揭示图像重要性。

Abstract: We introduce Puzzling-Bench, a benchmark of 23,777 puzzles with community-verified answers drawn from the Puzzling Stack Exchange archive (2014-2026): riddles, ciphers, logic grids, lateral-thinking problems, wordplay and recreational mathematics, a third of them with images. Every puzzle carries the answer accepted by its author, a short reference answer taken from it (LLM-extracted for the evaluation sets and checked on a 50-puzzle human review), the author’s own hints, community tags and a creation date, so that the benchmark supports hint ablations, category analysis and a rolling post-cutoff slice (254 puzzles posted after the training cutoff of six of the nine models we evaluate, including the best one) as a contamination check. We evaluate nine frontier and open models with a two-tier scorer (normalized exact match, then an LLM judge) and validate the judge with three judges from two model families: they agree on 95-97% of verdicts (kappa 0.82-0.96), move no model by more than 1.6 points, and show no own-family preference. The best model, GPT-6.1, reaches 74.8% on a shared 255-puzzle set; a hard core of 17 puzzles defeats all nine models; model failures are strongly correlated, and the second-best model contributes a single puzzle to a greedy ensemble, yet an oracle over all nine reaches 91%. On a 100-puzzle multimodal pilot, withholding the question’s image costs the two best models 24 and 35 points. We release the dataset, the scoring pipeline and all per-row provenance.

[18] Learning from Unreliable Operator References: Claim-Level Rewards for Customer Support Agents cs.CLPDF

Details

Motivation: 客服操作员回复即使整体正确，也可能包含缺乏知识库和工具记录支持的事实声明，直接监督学习会把这些不可靠内容当作正确答案模仿，导致智能体产生幻觉。论文旨在解决不可靠操作员参考下的训练目标设计问题。

Result: 在离线基准上，GRPO智能体的文本答案有8.6%被标记为幻觉，低于监督微调基线的13.6%。在与现有流程机器人的随机A/B测试中，部署系统将无需人工操作员介入即可关闭的对话比例从63.3%提升到80.8%。

Abstract: Even a correct operator reply can be an unreliable training target for a customer-support agent. About a quarter of the factual claims in these replies lacked full support in the agent’s knowledge base and tool records. We treat operator replies as claims to verify rather than answers to imitate. Unverified claims guide revisions to the knowledge base and tools. Verified claims serve as coverage targets in a claim-level GRPO reward that scores factual precision against the evidence the agent retrieved for its answer. On an offline benchmark, 8.6% of the GRPO agent’s text answers were flagged for hallucinations, compared with 13.6% for the supervised fine-tuning baseline. In a randomized A/B test against the existing procedural bot, the deployed system increased the share of conversations closed without a human operator from 63.3% to 80.8%.

[19] Where Does the Asymmetry Enter? Staging a Fairness Audit Across a Fused Multimodal Moderation Pipeline cs.CL | cs.CY | cs.MMPDF

Kardelen Subasi, Jarosław A. Chudziak

TL;DR: 论文对已发布的TikTok多模态内容审核分类器进行后验公平性审计，沿融合管线分阶段定位按源语言分组的差异性。研究发现不对称在“模型可读取内容”的准入阶段和文本分支编码中显著，而在最终决策阶段因最小子组仅12个测试项而无法检出。

Details

Motivation: 现有平台常用单一准确率判断跨语言公平性，这可能掩盖错误集中在某一语言群体的问题；论文要回答融合多模态审核管线中，子组不对称究竟在哪一阶段进入并可被检测。

Result: 对TikTok分类器的审计显示：准入阶段的语言无关上限使越南语视频比英语视频更少被接纳，原因是切分/分段而非内容冗长；文本分支编码也存在显著不对称；最终决策层因样本过少检测不到。

Insight: 提出分阶段公平性审计，将准入、分支编码、最终决策置于同一显著性标准下，并用日志决策、标签和融合前特征定位偏差来源；关键创新是把审计从证据最薄的最终决策上移到上游阶段。

Abstract: Almost every short-video upload now passes through a classifier that fuses video, speech, and on-screen text into one decision about whether it may be seen. It moderates every language community on the platform at once, and fairness across them is judged, if at all, from one accuracy score. That score is compatible with error falling almost entirely on one language group. An audit that goes looking reads the model’s decisions, the last stage of a long pipeline and the one whose test has the fewest items. We ask where in a fused pipeline a subgroup asymmetry can be detected at all: in what the classifier may read, in what its branches encode, or in what it decides. We answer this with a post-hoc audit of a published TikTok classifier, holding all three stages to one significance standard using only its logged decisions, labels, and pre-fusion features, with source language as the subgroup axis. The asymmetry is significant at the stage deciding what the model may read, and again in what the text branch encodes. At the earlier stage, a language-blind cap admits less of a Vietnamese clip than an English one, for reasons of segmentation, not verbosity. The asymmetry is undetectable at the decision, where the smallest subgroup leaves twelve items to test. An audit reading only decisions looks where its evidence is thinnest; the same test staged upstream recovers a disparity the decision-level view returns as noise.

[20] AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Nikolay Skachkov, Ekaterina Redina, Maksim Konoplev, Evgenii Uskov, Veronika Zykova

TL;DR: 论文发布 AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base，一个从零训练、Apache 2.0 开源的 MoE 语言模型，总参数 80B、每 token 约 3B 激活参数。模型结合 Kimi Delta Attention 与全注意力、Attention Residuals 和 Muon 训练，经过 17.5T token 通用预训练、两阶段上下文扩展及推理与智能体交互专用阶段，并系统描述数据管线、评测协议、架构消融和缩放律实验。

Details

Motivation: 论文旨在提供一个高效开源的大规模 MoE 基座模型，在控制激活参数量的同时提升推理、编程、事实知识和智能体能力；同时关注 held-out 训练损失作为下游性能代理的局限，以及训练稳定性和俄语事实知识评测缺失等问题。

Result: 零样本推理评测中，该模型在可比规模开源基座模型中领先，IMO-AnswerBench 达 88.7% pass@8，LiveCodeBench v5-v6 达 82.9% pass@8。俄语事实知识上，WikiWebFacts 达 86.5%、HardMultiQA 达 67.9%，超过参数量更大的 DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Base（83.2%/65.4%）和 Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-Base（72.8%/54.5%）。

Abstract: We present AliceAI-Foundation-80B-A3B-Base, a mixture-of-experts language model with 80 billion total parameters and approximately 3 billion active parameters per token, trained from scratch at Yandex and released under the Apache 2.0 license. The model combines Kimi Delta Attention with full attention, uses Attention Residuals, and is trained with Muon. Training comprises general pretraining on 17.5 trillion tokens, two context-extension stages, and a dedicated stage for reasoning and agentic interaction. We describe the data collection and augmentation pipelines, evaluation protocols, architectural ablations, and scaling-law experiments. We identify the limitations of held-out training loss as a proxy for downstream performance and introduce an auxiliary loss on mixture-of-experts outputs to stabilize training at the predicted learning rate. Domain-specific data selection and augmentation improve factual knowledge, mathematics, and programming skills, while agent trajectories introduced during pretraining improve performance on agentic benchmarks after supervised fine-tuning. In zero-shot reasoning evaluation, the model achieves leading results among open base models of comparable size, with 88.7% pass@8 on IMO-AnswerBench and 82.9% pass@8 on LiveCodeBench v5-v6. To evaluate Russian-language factual knowledge, we introduce and release two benchmarks with their evaluation protocols: WikiWebFacts, which tests short factual questions drawn from encyclopedic sources and search queries, and HardMultiQA, which tests more demanding factual questions inspired by user interactions. Our model scores 86.5% on WikiWebFacts and 67.9% on HardMultiQA, compared with 83.2% and 65.4% for DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Base (284B total and 13B active parameters) and 72.8% and 54.5% for Nemotron-3-Super-120B-A12B-Base (120B total and 12B active parameters), outperforming both larger models on these benchmarks.

Abstract: Poetry-to-video generation requires more than translating text into visual scenes. We introduce PoVi, a benchmark and agentic framework for grounded poetry-to-video generation, evaluating semantic grounding, discourse coherence, and audiovisual realization. PoVi reconstructs poem semantics into an evidence-bounded representation and progressively transforms them into narration, interpretation, character assets, and shot-level visual plans, followed by narration-aligned video generation. Across three planning backbones and four video generators, PoVi consistently improves over single-call generation, with the largest gains on implicit background knowledge and metaphorical relations. [22] Repairing the Trustworthiness of Distilled Language Models cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Kumar Shubham, Devendra Vyas, Vidushi Agarwal, Jayin Khanna, Prathosh AP

TL;DR: 论文针对知识蒸馏后小模型可信性下降的问题，提出基于近端Bregman响应函数（PBRF）的定向修复框架。该方法用代理数据集估计PBRF所需曲率，摆脱对原始蒸馏语料的依赖，并通过odds-ratio目标扩展到偏好监督，在约束模型变化的同时利用偏好/非偏好回复对修复可信性。

Details

Motivation: 知识蒸馏可将大模型压缩为小模型，但即使下游性能保持，学生模型的可信性也可能退化。修复困难在于原始蒸馏语料常不可用，而直接偏好优化又可能改变从教师模型继承的能力。

Result: 实验表明，该方法可提升蒸馏模型的目标可信性行为，跨不同蒸馏模型可信性回归最多改善8%，困惑度下降不到1%，并较大程度保持下游性能。

Insight: 创新点在于将PBRF用于蒸馏模型的可信性定向修复，用代理数据估计曲率以去除对原始迁移语料的依赖，并设计odds-ratio偏好目标在偏好监督下约束模型改动，提供了替代再蒸馏或无约束偏好优化的实用方案。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation enables the deployment of large language models through smaller student models, but the compression process can introduce regressions in trustworthiness even when downstream performance is preserved. Repairing such failures is challenging because the original distillation corpus is often unavailable, while direct preference optimization may alter capabilities inherited from the teacher. We propose a targeted repair framework based on the proximal Bregman response function (PBRF) that addresses both limitations. We estimate the curvature required for PBRF using a surrogate proxy dataset, removing dependence on the original transfer corpus, and extend the framework to preference-based supervision through an odds-ratio objective. This enables the use of preferred and dispreferred response pairs while constraining changes to the distilled model. Our results show that the proposed approach improves targeted trustworthiness behaviors for both pre-training and post-training models while largely preserving downstream performance, providing a practical alternative to re-distillation or unconstrained preference optimization. Our experiment results show that across different distilled models, the trustworthy regression can be improved by up to 8% with less than 1% decrement in perplexity.

[23] Proverbs As A Window On Collective Prescriptions cs.CL | cs.CYPDF

Tomás Lejarraga, Dirk U. Wulff, Ralph Hertwig

TL;DR: 论文提出一个计算框架，利用大语言模型并经过人工判断验证，分析近5万条英语谚语的主题分布、规训强度及其在人类判断与行为关键维度上的推荐倾向。研究发现多数谚语具有规训性，主要在个人可控领域提供指导；在一致立场上偏好谨慎、长期、诚实和善良，但在自主与服从等真正权衡处给出矛盾建议。

Details

Motivation: 谚语长期承载传统智慧，但其编码的集体规训和推荐倾向尚未被大规模量化。论文旨在用计算方法系统刻画文化谚语中的规范性与行为指导。

Result: 论文未涉及传统benchmark或SOTA比较；基于近5万条英语谚语的分析发现，多数谚语是规训性的，且对谨慎、长期、诚实、善良有一致偏好，对信任略偏怀疑，而在自主与服从等权衡维度上建议相互矛盾。

Insight: 创新点在于用LLM大规模映射谚语主题、评估规训力并量化多维行为推荐，且以人工判断验证。客观看，其可借鉴之处是将文化规范转化为可计算维度，并区分可控制情境下的规训与不可控情境下的描述。

Abstract: Proverbs, as repositories of traditional wisdom, have informed human reasoning for millennia. Yet the collective prescriptions they encode have remained largely unquantified. We introduce a computational framework to analyze nearly 50,000 English-language proverbs. Using large language models, validated against human judgments, we map the topical landscape of proverbs, assess their prescriptive power, and quantify their recommendations on key dimensions of human judgment and behavior. We find that most proverbs are prescriptive, offering guidance in domains under individual control, such as preparation and prudent action, whereas proverbs about uncontrollable circumstances, such as fate and mortality, tend to describe rather than prescribe. When prescriptive proverbs take a consistent stance, they favor caution over risk, long-term over short-term outcomes, honesty over deception, and kindness over malice, and show a moderate tendency toward suspicion over trust. On dimensions involving genuine trade-offs, especially autonomy versus compliance, proverbs provide contradicting advice rather than a single recommendation. This analysis provides a quantitative map of the collective prescriptions in a culture’s proverbial wisdom, revealing a core of prudent, honest, and forward-looking guidance alongside varied advice where circumstances dictate the best course of action.

[24] Diverse Alternative Reasoning Traces for Hard Math Problems cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Lukas Aichberger, Adam Golinski, Arno Blaas, Arwen Bradley, Jacob Springer

TL;DR: 论文提出DART，一种面向困难数学题的推理轨迹挖掘方法：从被拒绝的推理轨迹出发，将其分解为推理步骤并按位置加权不确定性排序，再借助参考答案引导和元思考为高排名步骤生成明显不同的替代候选，进而补全轨迹。该方法在DeepMath-103K上比蒙特卡洛采样恢复更多问题，并可用于LLM数学推理后训练。

Details

Motivation: 数学推理后训练通常依赖最终答案通过验证器的被接受轨迹，但困难数学问题在独立蒙特卡洛采样下很难产生此类轨迹。论文希望利用被拒绝轨迹来扩大困难问题的覆盖范围，并获得有效的后训练数据。

Result: 在贪心未解决的DeepMath-103K问题上，DART在生成轨迹数匹配时比MC采样多恢复37%的问题；每个恢复问题的估计生成成本几乎相同，而每个被接受轨迹的成本低30%。用DART挖掘轨迹后训练Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct，在MATH-500和AIME的六项评估中有五项取得最高点估计。

Insight: 创新点在于不丢弃失败轨迹，而是将其步骤化、按位置加权不确定性定位关键步骤，并用参考答案引导和元思考生成多样化替代推理路径。客观上看，其可借鉴之处是把被拒绝样本转化为可挖掘的后训练数据，通过步骤级不确定性重采样提升困难问题的轨迹覆盖。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) for mathematical reasoning often relies on accepted traces whose final answers pass a verifier. For hard math problems, however, such traces can be rare under independent Monte Carlo (MC) sampling. To address this, we propose Diverse Alternative Reasoning Traces (DART), a trace-mining method that starts from a rejected trace, decomposes it into reasoning steps, and ranks them by position-weighted uncertainty. It then generates deliberately distinct alternative candidates for highly ranked steps using reference-answer guidance and a meta-thought before completing the resulting traces. On greedy-unsolved DeepMath-103K problems, DART recovers 37% more problems than MC sampling at a matched number of generated traces. Its estimated generation cost per recovered problem is nearly identical, while its cost per accepted trace is 30% lower. Post-training Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct on traces mined by DART achieves the highest point estimate in five of six evaluations across MATH-500 and AIME benchmarks. These results demonstrate that revisiting rejected traces with DART can expand problem coverage and provide effective post-training data.

[25] How Many Samples Is RL Worth? Best-of-N versus RL Post-Training for Text-to-Image Models cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Salil Bhatnagar

TL;DR: 论文比较文本到图像模型中基于可自动校验奖励的RL后训练与更简单的best-of-N采样，提出“兑换率”指标：一个RL样本相当于多少个基模型样本。作者在Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium及其Flow-GRPO检查点上，用398个GenEval风格提示、人工标注和两个自动评委进行评估，发现等价关系高度依赖提示类型，并揭示自动评委存在方向相反的偏差。

Details

Motivation: RL已成为文本到图像模型常用的最后训练步骤，但best-of-N从基模型多采样再由选择器挑图更简单；论文要回答RL相对best-of-N到底值多少样本，并避免仅依赖自动奖励或评委导致不公平比较。

Result: 在非位置类提示（约4/5）上，基模型+best-of-16+VLM选择器约等于一个RL图像，人工最多看3张基模型图可达到与一个RL图像相近的正确率；在位置类提示上RL显著领先，人工在8张基模型图中找到正确图仅覆盖52%提示，而一个RL图像正确率为92%。总体主测试中best-of-16+VLM选择器在双自动评委通过标准下落后一个RL图像9.3分，人工检查差距为5.5/6.5分，FLUX.1-dev呈类似差距；RL还能解决基模型128次尝试未中的20个提示，1024次尝试可恢复其中一半。

Insight: 创新点在于提出可量化的RL-vs-best-of-N兑换率，并按位置约束分层，结合人工标注与多自动评委评估。客观看，其价值在于揭示RL优势主要集中在位置关系等难提示，且检测器型评委夸大差距、VLM评委低估差距，提醒后续比较必须人工核验选中图像。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) on a reward that can be checked automatically has become a common last step in the training of text-to-image models. A simpler way to get more correct images is best-of-N: draw N images from the base model and keep the one that a picker prefers. We ask how many base model samples one RL sample is worth, and call this number the exchange rate. We measure it for Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium and its public Flow-GRPO checkpoint on 398 new prompts in the style of GenEval, the benchmark behind the RL reward, with human labels on every prompt and two automatic judges other than the reward. The answer depends on the prompt. Four fifths of the prompts do not ask for a position (for example, one object left of another). On these, the base model catches up: best-of-16 with an off-the-shelf vision-language model (VLM) as the picker is about as good as one RL image. A person who looks through up to 3 base images finds a correct one as often as one RL image is correct. On the prompts that ask for a position, RL is far ahead: a person finds a correct image among 8 base images for only 52% of the prompts, against 92% for one RL image. Over all prompts, our primary test, which we fixed in advance, finds best-of-16 with the VLM picker 9.3 points behind one RL image when both automatic judges must pass an image. People see a smaller gap, 5.5 and 6.5 points in two checks. FLUX.1-dev shows a similar gap in the same test. RL also solves 20 prompts that the base model misses in 128 tries, and 1,024 tries recover half of them. The automatic judges are wrong in opposite directions: a detector-based judge like the reward, as used in our primary test, reports a larger gap than people see, and VLM judges report little or none. Comparisons of RL with best-of-N should therefore check the selected images by hand.

[26] CriticalScenes: Scalable Photorealistic Critical-Scenario Generation for Closed-Loop Driving Adaptation cs.CVPDF

Alexandros Theocharous, Holger Caesar, Ziqi Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 CriticalScenes，一个基于重建的框架，将普通记录驾驶场景转化为真实感的安全关键交互，并自动生成避碰监督，用于提升端到端驾驶规划器的安全规划能力。作者在53个重建源场景上生成979/1060条可行威胁轨迹（92.4%），并验证插入的威胁目标在5-35米非遮挡观察中检出率超过95%。实验表明生成监督能改善关键场景规划，但闭环迁移因规划器架构而异：SparseDrive在独立真实感闭环基准上碰撞率相对降低46.5%，UniAD闭环提升有限。

Details

Motivation: 真实驾驶记录中安全关键交互稀少，导致端到端规划器训练时缺乏规避行为和碰撞避免监督，可能产生不安全行为甚至碰撞。论文旨在可扩展地生成真实感安全关键场景及对应监督，弥补这一数据缺口。

Insight: 创新点在于基于重建将普通驾驶场景转化为真实感安全关键交互，并自动提供避碰监督，实现可扩展的临界场景生成。客观来看，论文还揭示了开环轨迹预测提升不一定转化为闭环安全收益，强调了闭环评估的重要性以及不同规划器架构对生成监督的迁移差异。

Abstract: Safety-critical interactions are rare in recorded driving data, leaving end-to-end driving planners with limited exposure to evasive behaviour during training. This lack of critical supervision can lead to unsafe behaviour and, in rare instances, collisions. To address this gap, we introduce CriticalScenes, a reconstruction-based framework that transforms ordinary recorded driving scenes into photorealistic safety-critical interactions with corresponding collision-avoidance supervision. Across 53 reconstructed source scenes, 979 of 1,060 attempted interactions yield feasible threat trajectories (92.4%), while the inserted threat actors remain detectable in more than 95% of non-occluded observations within 5–35,m. Experiments with two structurally different end-to-end planners show that the generated supervision improves critical-scenario planning, while closed-loop transfer varies across planner architectures. SparseDrive achieves a 46.5% relative reduction in collision rate on an independent photorealistic closed-loop benchmark, whereas UniAD shows only a modest closed-loop improvement despite substantial open-loop gains. The results further highlight the importance of closed-loop evaluation, as improvements in open-loop trajectory prediction do not necessarily translate into equivalent safety gains.

Yulun Wu, Zihan Min, Minshen Zhang, Kaiqin Kong, Ji Xie

TL;DR: 论文提出 JetDiffusion 视频生成系统，基于可训练的 Video Sparse Quantized Attention（VSQA），结合自定义推理内核、原生 NVFP4 计算与 kernel fusion，并联合 NVFP4 线性量化和步蒸馏，以大幅加速视频生成。通过分阶段训练配方，使模型在量化、稀疏化和少步去噪联合适配下保持生成质量。

Details

Motivation: 长序列注意力是视频生成的主要计算瓶颈，稀疏注意力和低比特量化可缓解该问题，但将稀疏化、NVFP4 量化和少步蒸馏三者联合适配十分困难，尤其在激进 NVFP4 量化下容易损害生成质量。

Result: 在 90% 稀疏度下，VSQA 相比 FlashAttention 在不同硬件上实现 8-29 倍注意力加速；在 Wan2.1-14B 上，最终模型于 RTX 5090 上实现 273 倍端到端视频生成加速，同时保持可比的视频质量。

Abstract: Attention over long sequences is a major computational bottleneck in video generation, which can be alleviated through sparsity and low-bit quantization. We propose JetDiffusion, a video generation system built on trainable Video Sparse Quantized Attention (VSQA), with custom inference kernels that combine native NVFP4 computation and kernel fusion. At 90% sparsity, VSQA accelerates attention by 8-29x compared with FlashAttention on different hardwares. To further accelerate video generation, we combine VSQA with NVFP4 linear quantization and step distillation. Although each technique can individually preserve generation quality, jointly adapting to all three remains challenging, particularly under aggressive NVFP4 quantization. Through empirical studies, we develop an effective staged training recipe that enables stable adaptation to quantization, sparsity, and few-step denoising. On Wan2.1-14B, the resulting model achieves 273x end-to-end video generation speedup on the RTX 5090 while maintaining comparable video quality.

[28] CoSMo: A Compact Spectral Space of Body Motion in Video cs.CVPDF

Maham Tanveer, Hao Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 CoSMo，一种视频人体运动的紧凑频谱表示：用固定 DCT 基建模 DINOv2 时序特征变化，将一段运动压缩为 384 维单一编码。该编码无需训练即可比较、编辑、迁移到新主体、插值、组合、缩放强度/细节和重采样，并可通过轻量适配器驱动冻结的图像到视频模型渲染任意主体。

Details

Motivation: 现有逐帧运动表示通常冗余且与主体、场景耦合，难以直接比较、编辑、组合和迁移，生成时还常需针对每个运动训练。论文希望构建一个紧凑、时间对齐、可解耦的运动编码空间。

Result: 在未见视频上，CoSMo 的运动匹配排序优于 DisMo，最近邻中出现同一主体的比例约低 40%，且编码紧凑约 7 倍；生成时无需逐运动训练，运动迁移优于 FlexiAct 和文本提示基线，自动指标至少不弱于 DisMo，人形主体间明显更好，混合时保留更多源运动；用户研究中运动迁移首选率 59% vs. 30%，前二名 94%，插值偏好 76%。

Insight: 核心创新是用固定 DCT 基让不同运动编码在时间上对齐并按粗到细频带组织，从而支持无需帧匹配的融合与编辑；同时利用常数项抵消和少频带保留减少主体/场景信息，再以轻量适配器连接冻结图像到视频模型，实现统一频谱空间中的运动迁移与生成。

Abstract: We introduce CoSMo, a compact spectral space of body motion in video, in which each motion is a single code that can be compared and edited without training, transferred to a new subject, interpolated, composed, scaled in intensity and detail, or sampled anew. Our encoder expresses how a clip’s DINOv2 features change over time in a fixed temporal basis, the discrete cosine transform (DCT), and compresses this change into 384 numbers. Because each frequency band is the same cosine pattern in every code, codes are aligned in time, ordered from coarse to fine, and can be blended or added without matching frames. What stays constant over a clip cancels, and keeping fewer frequencies leaves less of the performer and scene. The encoder is trained on real videos and on synthetic motions of several characters, and lightweight adapters let a frozen image-to-video model render any code with any subject. On unseen videos, our code ranks the same motion on other bodies higher than DisMo, a per-frame representation, while its nearest neighbors show the same performer about 40% less often with a seven times more compact code. In generation, without per-motion training, CoSMo transfers motion more faithfully than FlexiAct and a text-prompt baseline, at least as faithfully as DisMo on automatic metrics and clearly better between humanoid bodies, and its blends keep much more of both source motions than DisMo’s. Users rank CoSMo first nearly twice as often as DisMo for motion transfer (59% vs. 30%), place it top-two 94% of the time, and prefer it in 76% of the interpolation comparisons.

[29] What Does RL Post-Training Teach Text-to-Image Models? A Skill-Wise Analysis of Flow-GRPO cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Cheng Zhang, Jiesong Liu

TL;DR: 论文对Flow-GRPO这类强化学习后训练在文生图流模型上的作用做了按技能拆解的分析。作者在共享噪声种子、训练未见的prompt上，逐技能比较RL前后的pass@k，发现GenEval六项技能中四项的提升主要来自「锐化」（提升采样效率），即好样本原本就存在，只是被选中；而空间关系的提升来自「解锁」，即pass@32从0.69升到1.00，以及基座模型原本解不出的prompt有93%被解出。作者据此提出统一解释：在单个prompt内，group-relative更新只是重新加权已有结果，流策略无法拓宽噪声；跨prompt时，共享参数能让某些prompt上的成功修复多数prompt共有的缺陷。

Details

Motivation: Flow-GRPO把SD3.5-M的GenEval分数从0.63提到0.95，此前通常被理解为模型学会了组合式生成，但作者质疑这些提升有多少其实已包含在基座模型自身的采样分布中，因此需要按技能区分RL后训练究竟「教会」了什么。

Result: 在GenEval六项技能中，四项的提升表现为锐化：一个后训练样本约等于基座模型的4到10个样本，pass@32几乎不变；空间关系表现为解锁：pass@32从0.69升至1.00，128个基座样本仍不及一个后训练样本，原本32个样本从未解出的prompt有93%被解出。关系类技能贡献了35%的总提升；其余65%中，用奖励背后的judge在8个基座样本中做选择可恢复十分之九。基准平均值「一个后训练样本相当于31个基座样本」无法描述任何单项技能。

Insight: 本文的创新在于用逐技能、共享噪声种子和pass@k的诊断框架，把RL后训练的收益分解为锐化与解锁两种机制，并给出可检验的理论解释（prompt内的group-relative重加权与prompt间的共享参数修复共同缺陷）。其分析思路可迁移到其他RL后训练场景，提示评估不应只看基准均分，而应区分采样效率提升与能力边界扩展；同时说明奖励模型或judge的选择机制本身就能解释大部分收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has become a standard way to improve text-to-image flow models: Flow-GRPO lifts the GenEval score of SD3.5-M from 0.63 to 0.95, a gain read as the model having learned compositional generation. We ask how much of that gain the base model’s own samples already contained, by measuring pass@k over shared noise seeds, one skill at a time, before and after RL, on prompts never used in training. The answer differs by skill. On four of GenEval’s six skills, where 32 base samples already solve almost every prompt, the gain is sharpening: one post-trained sample is worth 4 to 10 base samples, and pass@32 barely moves. On spatial relations it is unlocking: pass@32 rises from 0.69 to 1.00, 128 base samples do not match one post-trained sample, and prompts the base model never solved in 32 samples are solved 93% of the time, as often as those it solved. The benchmark average, 31 base samples per post-trained sample, describes no skill. Relations carry 35% of the gain; of the other 65%, selecting among 8 base samples with the judge behind the reward recovers nine tenths. We propose an account of when each occurs: within a prompt, a group-relative update re-weights only the outcomes its samples contain, and a flow policy cannot widen the noise that produces them; across prompts, shared parameters let successes on some prompts repair a deficit common to many. The same anatomy applies to a text-rendering checkpoint. The account’s predictions are tested with a checkpoint trained without KL, within-prompt diversity, and controlled training that holds object classes out, where sharpening comes first and transfers, and unlocking follows, on practised objects before unseen ones.

Ali Asgarov, Chris Thomas

TL;DR: 论文提出时间权重手术（TWS），让通用LLM读取视频语言模型的权重与激活，定位导致视频定位时间输出错误的部位并进行无梯度修复。论文的核心是诊断模型失败的位置和原因，并在TimeLens-8B和Qwen3-VL-8B上验证该局部修复思路。

Details

Motivation: 视频语言模型在长视频中常能找对时刻，却把时间写错，例如丢失数字或把分钟写成秒；论文希望像外科手术一样只修复错误来源，而不是整体重训或微调。

Result: 在TimeLens-8B中定位到单个MLP层，最大编辑在1215个未见问题上提升7.9 mIoU，在30分钟以上视频提升18.3；在Qwen3-VL-8B中定位到时间分隔符，编辑分别提升2.9和1.3，仅修复分隔符使mIoU从13.1提升到19.2；有效编辑均优于同规模随机编辑，且推理速度不变。

Insight: 创新点在于用LLM作为编辑器进行无梯度、可解释的权重/激活诊断与局部修复，将错误归因到具体层或时间分隔符；客观看，‘先诊断再局部编辑’的范式对多模态模型输出格式和时间推理错误的低成本修复有借鉴意义。

Abstract: A surgeon first finds what is wrong, then fixes only that part. We use this idea for video grounding, where video-language models often find the right moment in a long video but write its time wrong. In Temporal Weight Surgery (TWS), a general-purpose LLM reads the model’s weights and activations, finds where the error arises and repairs it there, without gradients. Separate sessions find the same cause, and this diagnosis of where and why the model fails is our main contribution. Three GPT-6 Astra sessions trace a dropped digit in TimeLens-8B to one MLP layer, and the largest edit gains 7.9 mIoU on 1,215 unseen questions and 18.3 on videos of 30 minutes or more. Two Claude Opus 5.5 sessions trace minutes written as seconds in Qwen3-VL-8B to the separator and gain 2.9 and 1.3. Before any edit, its answers often have the right minute and the wrong separator, and fixing only the separator lifts their mIoU from 13.1 to 19.2. Every improving edit beats a random edit of its size, and the edited models run as fast as before.

Ce Zhang, Baiqi Li, Seongsu Ha, Gedas Bertasius

TL;DR: 论文提出 DeLoVi 框架，利用预训练决策模型从超长视频中检索与问题相关的稀疏证据。DeLoVi-S 先生成短视频片段字幕，再用决策模型对字幕与问题的相关性排序，最后由生成式 LLM 基于高排名字幕作答；DeLoVi-E 进一步通过自动化策略演化降低检索成本。

Details

Abstract: Understanding ultra-long videos requires retrieving sparse, query-relevant evidence from extensive video histories. Existing similarity-based retrieval methods are efficient, but they encode the question and the video content independently and thus often miss evidence whose relevance requires reasoning over both. Generative large language models (LLMs) can perform such reasoning, but they typically generate text over multiple rounds of search, which makes each query slow and costly. In this work, we introduce DeLoVi (Decision Models for Long Video), a framework that leverages pretrained decision models to retrieve evidence from ultra-long videos. Like an LLM, a decision model reads the question together with the candidate evidence, so it can reason over both. Unlike an LLM, it directly outputs a relevance score without generating text, which makes it fast and inexpensive. We first present DeLoVi-S, a simple method that generates captions for short video clips and uses a decision model to rank those captions by their relevance to a question. A generative LLM then answers the question using the top-ranked captions. Despite its simplicity, DeLoVi-S achieves state-of-the-art performance on three ultra-long video understanding benchmarks, EgoLifeQA, EgoMemReason, and MM-Lifelong, and generalizes effectively to long-video understanding benchmarks, VideoMME-L and LVBench. However, DeLoVi-S scores every caption for every question, so its cost grows linearly with video length. To reduce this cost, we propose an automated policy evolution framework that iteratively refines the memory construction and retrieval policies around a fixed pretrained decision model. The resulting method, DeLoVi-E, maintains comparable performance while reducing input tokens by 34x on average compared with DeLoVi-S. Motivation: 现有视频扩散和世界模型虽能生成合理视频，但不能可靠匹配用户请求的相机方向和移动距离，缺乏准确的相机运动控制。

Result: 在 VBench 上提升方向准确性和移动校准，同时保持有竞争力的视频质量；在 WorldScore 上增强链式运动中的相机控制和场景一致性。摘要未宣称全面 SOTA。

Insight: 创新点在于无需训练的空间引导流程：冻结 inpainting 扩展场景、深度渲染近似目标视图、Layout Steering 对早期 latent 做衰减低频校正，以及长视频自回归分段维持相机控制。客观看，其价值在于将显式几何/布局引导注入预训练视频扩散模型，避免额外训练并改善可控性。

Abstract: Video diffusion and world models generate plausible videos but do not reliably match the requested camera direction and travel distance. We present DreamTour, a training-free framework that generates camera tours from a single image with more accurate control over camera motion. DreamTour first uses a frozen image inpainting prior to extend the photograph beyond its original field of view while preserving the input pixels. Depth-based rendering of this extended scene along the requested camera path produces approximate target views that serve as spatial guidance. We introduce Layout Steering, which aligns early video latents with these views through decaying low-frequency corrections, leaving later denoising to refine appearance and dynamics. For longer tours, we autoregressively generate the video segments, repeating guidance construction from each segment’s final frame to maintain camera control across the full trajectory. DreamTour improves directional accuracy and travel calibration on VBench while maintaining competitive video quality. On WorldScore, it strengthens camera control and scene consistency across chained movements. Together, these results demonstrate that training-free spatial guidance enables more accurate and consistent camera control in pretrained video diffusion and world models.

[34] Synchronizing Cameras via 3D Object Relationships cs.CVPDF

Sujong Shin, Sang-Il Choi, Kwang Moo Yi

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于三维物体关系同步同一动态事件多视角视频的方法。方法先用视觉语言模型识别各相机中的物体，结合开放词表分割与跟踪、预训练深度估计获得物体三维位置，并用点轨迹的二维运动补足失败区域，最后通过成对及联合鲁棒优化搜索使跨相机物体关系最一致的时间偏移。

Details

Motivation: 现有动态视频同步方法多依赖像素级对应或重建光度损失，但快速运动和遮挡使承载时序信息的运动区域难以跨视角匹配。论文回到“物体可作为相机全局推理可靠线索”的思路，利用物体间三维关系在跨视角下尺度内稳定的特性进行对齐。

Result: 论文在四个标准基准上评估，所提方法优于当前最先进方法（state of the art）。

Insight: 创新点在于将物体级关系线索引入多视频时间同步：只需各视角物体身份即可比较跨相机关系，并融合三维物体线索与二维点轨迹运动以提升遮挡和分割/深度失败场景下的鲁棒性。客观看，其可借鉴之处是混合语义物体关系与几何运动、先成对后联合鲁棒优化的同步框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we synchronize multiple videos of the same dynamic event through object relationships. Driven by dynamic reconstruction and novel view synthesis, many recent synchronization methods rely on pixel-level evidence, such as point correspondences across views or the photometric losses of a reconstructed scene. In dynamic scenes, however, fast motion and occlusions make the moving regions that carry much of the timing information hard to match across views. We thus propose to bring back the traditional wisdom that objects are reliable cues for global reasoning about cameras. Measured in 3D, the relationships between objects, such as the distance between two people, are stable across all views up to scale; they can serve as cues for alignment. To compare these relationships across cameras, we only need the identity of each object in every view, as the cues are relational. We obtain these identities by giving a vision-language model a few frames of every camera and prompting it to identify the objects in the scene. We then segment and track each identified object through every video with an open-vocabulary segmentor. To obtain the 3D locations of objects, we use a pretrained depth estimator. Where depth or segmentation fails, we complement these 3D cues with 2D motion from point tracks. Finally, we synchronize the cameras by finding the temporal offsets at which the cues of different cameras agree best, first for each camera pair and then for all cameras jointly with robust optimization. We evaluate our method on four standard benchmarks, outperforming the state of the art.

Details

Motivation: 退化图像会使视觉语言模型给出错误答案，论文旨在理解并预测图像令牌修复的边界，即哪些图像状态能恢复正确回答，从而为模型鲁棒性提供可预测的修复机制。

Result: 在四个编码器模型中，12到23个排序令牌即可恢复一半全补丁效果，而随机顺序需4到15倍更多；无编码器Gemma需22.2对53.1。全块修复在模型特定语言模型交叉点失效。干净图像灰度消融交叉预测留出修复误差1.53层，优于共享深度的3.65层。Gemma MMBench有唯一大的注意力到修复点差距，Restormer提供非预言一致性检查。

Abstract: Degraded images make vision-language models answer wrongly. Using synthetic paired blur, noise, and low light, we trace which clean image states can still repair those answers from the vision encoder through the language model. In four encoder models, 12 to 23 ranked tokens recover half of the full-patch effect, whereas random orders require four to fifteen times as many; encoder-free Gemma needs 22.2 versus 53.1. Full-block repair expires at a model-specific language-model crossing. The matched reverse-direction intervention is algebraically identical to text-token restoration, so its gap from image restoration measures a redundancy band rather than an independent predictor. A clean-image gray-ablation crossing independently predicts held-out repair with 1.53-layer error, against 3.65 for shared depth. Gemma MMBench has the only large attention-to-repair point gap; Gemma GQA does not. Restormer provides a non-oracle consistency check on two models.

Details

Motivation: 现有从视频观测铰接物体时通常只能看到物体一侧和部分运动，难以恢复完整部件、隐藏几何与正确运动结构；论文希望用生成先验补全未见几何，并用视频观测验证，从而统一处理家居物品和机器人手等多样铰接对象。

Result: 在 Articulate-100 和新提出的 HArt 基准（60 个 PartNet-Mobility 物体与 8 个机器人手/夹爪的 RGB-D 视频）上，ArtKit 将物体上的关节轴误差和部件运动误差相对最强基线降低一半以上，并降低机器人手上的轴误差；方法也可运行于真实录制和生成视频。

Abstract: An RGB-D camera that films an object being moved, often from one viewpoint, sees one side of it and how its parts move. We present ArtKit, which takes such a video as input to create an articulated model with complete parts, a kinematic tree, and typed joints. Our central idea is generating the hidden geometry and verifying it against what the camera sees. A generative part prior proposes complete, part-decomposed geometry based on the first frame. Point tracks, grouped into rigid trajectories, split and merge the generated parts into links. We then fit revolute and prismatic joints, with a visibility-aware energy ensuring that the articulated model explains the depth observed across the video. A rule checks against the video to choose between synchronous group fits and per-frame chain fits, so that one pipeline serves a diverse range of articulated objects, such as household items and robotic hands. Finally, we regenerate each link of the chosen fit as one part and keep it wherever it agrees better with the observations. We evaluate on Articulate-100 and on HArt, a new benchmark of 60 PartNet-Mobility objects and 8 robotic hands and grippers rendered as RGB-D videos with ground-truth parts and joints. ArtKit reduces the axis and part motion errors of the strongest baseline by more than half on the objects, as well as the axis error on the hands. ArtKit also runs on real recordings and generated videos.

Abstract: We propose the Audio-Visual Query Transformer (AVQFormer), a highly efficient architecture built upon a 2.7B parameter LLM featuring a fixed-length $K=16$ semantic alignment module, stabilized via Adaptive Self-Critical Sequence Training (ASCST) with Entropy Regularization. Multimodal Large Language Models (LLMs) often rely on global audio representations to compensate for inefficient, frame-based visual encoders. While our Phase 1 prompted evaluations demonstrate that the Multimodal Base Model achieves strong deep semantic alignment (scoring 0.889 on BERTScore F1), optimizing this architecture for legacy metrics reveals a performance gap. We explore this gap as a hypothesis we term temporal alignment friction: a conflict where global audio representations disrupt the chronological sequence of visual frames, leading to penalties under visually-focused evaluation metrics. Consequently, by stripping away the audio, our Video-Only ablation conforms more closely to this rigid syntax, achieving 63.0 CIDEr. While trailing larger models such as Qwen2.5-VL (67.4 CIDEr), this establishes a strong baseline for sub-3B parameter LLM-based models. Motivation: 现有基于梯度的迁移对抗攻击虽成功率高，但每对图像需数百步昂贵优化，且通常从无信息的随机扰动出发。论文希望利用摊销式对抗生成提供更好的初始化，减少逐样本优化成本。

Abstract: Gradient-based transferable adversarial attacks achieve state-of-the-art attack success rates against black-box vision-language models (VLMs). However, they require hundreds of costly optimization steps for each image pair. One possible reason is that these attacks typically start from random perturbations that contain no information about the source or target image. In this work, we study whether amortized adversarial generation can provide better initializations for gradient-based attacks. By learning across many image pairs, amortization allows a generator to produce adversarial perturbations for new inputs without optimizing each perturbation from scratch. We introduce SPARK, a lightweight looped perturbation generator conditioned on both the source and target images. SPARK repeatedly applies the same network to refine a perturbation, producing effective adversarial attacks in a few forward passes without requiring gradients through surrogate vision encoders at inference time. We show that these perturbations provide effective warm starts for existing gradient-based attacks, substantially improving attack success rates while reducing the number of optimization steps required. These improvements generalize across different attack methods, VLMs, and unseen image categories. SPARK also produces effective standalone attacks at a fraction of the computational cost of gradient-based optimization. Code and model weights are available at https://github.com/Patchwork53/SPARK.

Abstract: Confidence-gated co-training assumes that pseudo-label errors are uncertain and concentrated at organ boundaries. We argue that for pancreas CT, where the target organ is a single connected structure and detached fragments are anatomically implausible, the dominant failures arise at three levels: where the organ is, what its boundary should look like, and how a correct boundary is penalized. Anatomical Rectification relabels implausible fragments of a teacher prediction as confident background, without adding any loss or parameter; an audit confirms that its rejections are overwhelmingly detached hallucinations and that thin, tail-like slices are not eroded. Boundary Grafting transplants labelled surface regions with their annotation into unlabeled volumes, replacing unreliable boundary pseudo-labels with ground truth. The residual error is coherent surface displacement, weakly penalized by permutation-invariant Dice and cross-entropy losses. Homotopy-Annealed Hausdorff (HAH) supervision anneals a distance-weighted penalty from an annotation-anchored objective that is convex in the prediction to a Hausdorff surrogate, admitting the prediction-dependent distance term only after the predicted surface becomes reliable. On NIH pancreas CT, under the standard semi-supervised split, the full method reaches 84.60% Dice and 4.97 voxels 95HD, improving all four metrics over compared methods; on MSD Task07, 78.90% Dice and 4.19 voxels 95HD. We further contribute PanTS-SSL, a semi-supervised evaluation protocol derived from the public 9,901-case PanTS corpus, with a curated 1,015-volume main pool, labelled tiers of 50, 100 and 250 volumes, an 80-case tumor track, shift probes for acquisition and anatomy change, and a fixed 901-volume test set. On it, the method exceeds the co-training baseline at 100 labels, scales to 85.29% Dice at 250 labels, and reaches 85.50% Dice at 100 labels with HAH.

[40] ViCARD: Visual Credit Assignment with Reward-Gated Distillation for Multimodal Reasoning cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Zijian Huang, Jiasi Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出ViCARD方法，针对多模态推理中基于结果强化学习的两个问题：感知错误导致全失败时无学习信号，以及响应内每个token均匀分配优势。ViCARD结合逐token视觉监督与验证正确性奖励，利用裁剪相关视觉区域的教师模型提供逐token指导，并在全失败组中采样裁剪教师答案，仅当答案正确时激活on-policy蒸馏。在多个模型尺度上，ViCARD提升了细粒度视觉推理性能，优于RL和蒸馏基线，同时保持广泛的视觉推理能力。

Details

Motivation: 解决基于结果强化学习在视觉推理中的两个挑战：当感知错误导致所有采样响应失败时缺乏学习信号，以及响应内所有token共享统一优势而缺乏细粒度信用分配。同时，视觉自蒸馏虽提供密集指导，但学生仅从token分布学习难以得出正确答案。

Result: 广泛比较表明，ViCARD在多个模型尺度上提高了细粒度视觉推理性能，超越了RL和蒸馏基线，同时保留了广泛的视觉推理能力。摘要未提及具体基准和数值。

Insight: 创新点在于将逐token视觉监督与验证正确性奖励相结合，利用裁剪相关视觉区域的教师提供细粒度视觉信用，并通过奖励门控的蒸馏机制为全失败组提供学习信号。客观分析：巧妙融合RL和蒸馏，用教师裁剪图像实现细粒度信用分配，门控机制避免错误蒸馏信号，具有借鉴价值。

Abstract: Visual reasoning requires models to identify relevant visual evidence and use it to derive correct answers. Outcomebased reinforcement learning (RL) faces two key challenges in this regime: it yields no learning signal when perceptual errors cause all sampled responses to fail, and it broadcasts a uniform advantage across every token within a response. Conversely, while visual self-distillation offers dense guidance from evidence-centered image crops, student learning from token distributions alone often fails to elicit correct final answers. Motivated by an audit of training rollouts that exposes these complementary failure modes, we introduce ViCARD (Visual Credit Assignment with RewardGated Distillation), which integrates per-token visual supervision with verified correctness rewards. For each rollout group, we leverage a teacher with its input image cropped to the relevant visual area, and use this to provide per-token supervision to the student while also valuing correctness. If all sampled rollouts fail, ViCARD samples an additional crop-teacher answer and activates on-policy distillation only if that answer is correct. Together, these mechanisms provide fine-grained visual credit within informative reward groups and a learning signal for otherwise uninformative all-fail groups. Extensive comparisons across model scales demonstrate that ViCARD improves fine-grained visual reasoning performance over RL and distillation baselines, while retaining broad visual reasoning capabilities.

Motivation: 现有VLM后训练多依赖结果奖励模型，只监督最终决策，容易在视觉证据不足时仍给出自信回答；已有不确定性方法多为事后检测或假设总存在客观结论。论文希望提升模型在模糊情境下基于视觉证据校准置信并合理弃权的能力。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值或benchmark，但定性表明：在模糊输入上弃权率显著提升，清晰图像准确率大致保持不变，且改进可迁移到Gemma 4 E4B。

Insight: 创新点在于将多模态推理分为感知与校准两阶段，并用轻量PRM分别评分，以证据与置信度一致性作为奖励信号。客观看，其价值在于把弃权能力前置到过程监督中，而非仅做事后不确定性检测或只优化最终结果。

Abstract: Reliable deployment of Vision Language Models (VLMs) requires that a model’s expressed confidence remain grounded in what it can actually see, and that it recognize when the visual evidence is too ambiguous to commit to a confident response. Standard post-training uses outcome reward models (ORMs) that supervise only the final decision, rewarding a confident answer regardless of whether the image supports one, while existing methods either tend to detect uncertainty post-hoc or assume an objective verdict always exists. We instead split multimodal reasoning into two stages: perception, where the model must factually describe the scene, and calibration, where it must judge whether that evidence is sufficient to commit to a conclusion. We introduce HumblePRM, a lightweight process reward model (PRM) that scores each stage independently, rewarding agreement between evidence and expressed confidence rather than outcome correctness alone. Training with HumblePRM increases abstention due to uncertainty on ambiguous inputs considerably while leaving accuracy on clear images roughly unchanged. We also find that the improvement transfers to Gemma 4 E4B, a larger, popular edge model. Our findings show that process reward supervision is a promising route to VLMs with better calibrated abstention, ensuring more reliability rather than unpredictable behavior.
