Weijia Mao, Kevin Qinghong Lin, Zhen Fang, Danze Chen, Dongxing Mao

TL;DR: 论文提出 Play2Evolve，一个面向编码智能体游戏开发的递归框架，包含初始化、精炼和演化三个阶段。它把可玩性作为反馈，让智能体试玩游戏并诊断功能、视觉呈现和玩法问题，再据此修复 bug、改进素材并生成新的玩法和美学目标。为丰富游戏素材，方法结合视频生成模型与代码生成静态和动态资产并融入交互玩法。

Details

Motivation: 编码智能体虽能根据自然语言生成可运行游戏，但可运行不等于良好游玩体验；现有工作较少探索如何让智能体提升核心功能可靠性、视觉设计与素材吸引力以及支持探索和进度的丰富玩法。

Result: 在 GameCraft-Bench 上达到所评估方法中的 SOTA，得分 60.05，高于 Game Initialization 的 50.81 和 Play2Code 的 52.46；人类整体质量评分达 3.67，也高于对比的 2.38 和 2.43。

Insight: 创新点在于以可玩性作为反馈驱动游戏递归开发，通过试玩诊断功能、视觉和玩法问题，并将反馈用于修复、素材改进和后续玩法与美学目标演化；同时结合视频生成模型与代码生成静态/动态游戏资产，增强交互玩法表现。

Abstract: Coding agents can generate runnable games from natural-language descriptions. However, a runnable game does not guarantee an excellent gameplay experience for players. In our view, a playful game should provide 1) reliable core functionality, 2) appealing visual design and assets, and 3) rich gameplay that supports exploration and progression. How agents can improve these aspects of playfulness remains underexplored. In our work, we propose Play2Evolve, a recursive game development framework with three stages: Initialization, Refinement, and Evolution. Using playfulness as feedback, agents playtest the game and diagnose issues in its functionality, visual presentation, and gameplay. Their feedback guides bug fixes and asset improvements. It also helps agents propose new gameplay and aesthetic goals for continued game evolution. To enrich game materials, we combine a video generation model with code to create appealing static and dynamic assets and integrate them into interactive gameplay. Finally, Play2Evolve achieves state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated methods, scoring 60.05 on GameCraft-Bench compared with 50.81 after Game Initialization and 52.46 for Play2Code. Human overall-quality ratings reach 3.67, compared with 2.38 and 2.43, respectively. Our code will be released at https://github.com/showlab/Play2Evolve.

[43] OrdiHand-GS: Ordinal-Guided Tracking and Hand-Refined Gaussian Avatars for Digital Signers cs.CVPDF

Mingxuan Ma, Ronglai Zuo

TL;DR: 论文面向数字手语虚拟人（digital signers），针对双手交叉时单目人体追踪的序数深度歧义和神经渲染中手部外观模糊问题，提出 OrdiHand-GS。该方法用视频深度估计与手部表观尺寸进行置信度加权投票并允许弃权，推断前后手关系并以合页约束注入追踪；随后在冻结头像主干与渲染器的情况下，用源条件残差头微调手部相关 UV 高斯局部位置。

Details

Motivation: 数字手语虚拟人的可懂度高度依赖手部，而双手交叉时，同一组 2D 关键点可对应任一只手，导致单目追踪出现序数深度歧义；即使几何修正后，渲染中的手部外观仍易模糊。

Result: 在 How2Sign 的 365 个标注重叠帧上，方法将手腕前后顺序准确率从 32.9% 提升到 77.3%，仅增加 0.39 像素重投影误差；随机符号安慰剂无平衡准确率增益，2x2 因子评估表明交互渲染提升主要来自追踪阶段细化。

Insight: 创新点在于将双手交叉问题拆分为序数深度消歧与手部外观细化：用多源投票和弃权机制获得可靠前后关系，并以冻结主干的条件残差头局部修正手部高斯，避免破坏整体头像表示。

Abstract: Digital signers, also known as signing avatars, convey sign language through animated virtual humans, where visual fidelity and linguistic intelligibility hinge primarily on the hands. When one hand crosses over the other, two failure modes dominate. First, monocular expressive-human tracking suffers from ordinal depth ambiguity: either hand can satisfy the same 2D landmarks, and the baseline tracker orders wrists at chance level on 365 annotated overlapping frames from How2Sign. Second, hand appearance remains blurred in neural rendering even after geometry is corrected. We propose OrdiHand-GS to address these errors separately. Relative depth from a video depth estimator and apparent hand size vote, with confidence weighting and abstention, for a front-back relation, which enters expressive-human tracking as a hinge on median hand-surface depth. A source-conditioned residual head then refines the local positions of hand-associated UV Gaussians with the avatar backbone and renderer frozen. We instantiate the framework with GUAVA. Against matched unconstrained refinement, wrist-order accuracy rises from 32.9% to 77.3% at a cost of 0.39 pixels of reprojection error, while a random-sign placebo gives no balanced-accuracy gain. A 2x2 factorial evaluation attributes most of the interaction-rendering improvement to tracking-stage refinement, with a smaller additional benefit from the residual.

[44] SplatWAM: Splatting World Action Models for Real-Time 3D Imagination cs.CV | cs.LG | cs.ROPDF

Frank Tsu-Fang Lu, Karthik Sundar Senthilkumar, Christopher Thirgood, Oscar Mendez, Simon Hadfield

TL;DR: 论文提出 SplatWAM，一种直接在紧凑 3D 高斯原语上学习动作条件动力学的生成式世界模型。它先用 SplatWAM-T 将可变数量的无位姿视图编码为固定预算的高斯 token，再用 SplatWAM-D 对每个动作单次前向预测未来高斯状态，并通过可微渲染监督和任意视角光栅化保证跨视图一致的显式 3D 表示。论文还引入与动力学模型联合训练的轻量 flow-matching 策略头 SplatWAM-P，并在 OGBench Scene、8 个任务套件和 LIBERO 上验证实时 3D 想象与策略学习效果。

Details

Motivation: 现有世界模型通常在像素或潜空间预测未来观测，难以直接表示真实 3D 场景演化，也限制实时新视角渲染和跨视图一致性。论文希望直接在显式 3D 高斯表示上学习动作条件动力学，从而支持高效、可任意视角渲染的世界模型与策略学习。

Result: 在 OGBench Scene 上，SplatWAM 新视角渲染比视频世界模型快 12 倍，约 80 FPS（RTX 5090），PSNR 最高提升 2.0 dB。8 个任务套件中，无模型高斯 token 策略最多超过像素策略 24.8 个百分点，SplatWAM-P 超过 LPWM 12 个百分点并匹配无学习价值函数的 HIQL；在 LIBERO 上，约 1B 模型超过约 7B 的 WorldVLA 和机器人预训练 OpenVLA，并在均无大规模机器人预训练时匹配 OpenVLA-OFT。

Insight: 主要创新是在 3D 高斯原语空间直接构建动作条件世界模型，而非像素或潜空间预测；通过固定预算高斯 token 编码无位姿多视图、log-Euclidean 协方差坐标和可微渲染监督提升显式 3D 动力学学习。客观来看，其价值在于显式 3D 表示带来的实时任意视角渲染、跨视图一致性和高效策略学习，以及用轻量策略头复用世界模型表示。

Abstract: World models typically predict future observations in pixel or latent space, limiting their ability to directly represent true 3D scene evolution. We present SplatWAM (Splatting World Action Model), a generative world model that learns action-conditioned dynamics directly over compact 3D Gaussian primitives. A tokenizer SplatWAM-T first encodes a variable number of unposed views into a fixed budget of Gaussian tokens. A dynamics model SplatWAM-D then predicts future Gaussian states in a single forward pass per action, using log-Euclidean covariance coordinates and differentiable rendering supervision to facilitate learning in an explicit 3D space. Each predicted state can be rasterized from arbitrary viewpoints, enforcing a shared 3D representation across views by construction. On OGBench Scene, SplatWAM renders novel views 12x faster than video world models (80 FPS on an RTX 5090) with up to 2.0 dB higher PSNR. We further introduce SplatWAM-P, a lightweight flow-matching policy head jointly trained with the dynamics model. Across 8 task suites, model-free Gaussian-token policies outperform pixel-based counterparts by up to 24.8 percentage points. SplatWAM-P improves over Latent Particle World Model (LPWM) by 12 percentage points and matches HIQL without a learned value function. On LIBERO, our ~1B model outperforms WorldVLA (7B) and the robot-pretrained OpenVLA (~7B), while matching OpenVLA-OFT when both are trained without large-scale robot pretraining. Code and checkpoints will be released.

[45] Streaming Tax: Can Streaming VLMs Replace Their Offline Parents? cs.CVPDF

Zhi Yang, Junzhou Chen, Jindong Wang

TL;DR: 论文研究流式视觉语言模型（streaming VLMs）能否替代其离线父模型完成离线任务，并提出Streaming Tax在理解、可信性和泛化三个维度、15个基准上系统评估。研究发现离线能力保持与实际可替换性之间存在关键差距，且该差距来自流式适配和原生流式协议两个阶段。作者进一步提出免训练插件Stream++以改善流式VLMs在离线任务上的表现。

Details

Motivation: 流式VLMs擅长实时交互，而离线父模型擅长离线视频理解；若流式模型能替代离线父模型，可降低部署成本并提升效率，因此需要系统评估这种替代是否可行。

Result: 在15个基准上的评估表明，流式模型即使大体保持离线理解能力，流式适配仍会改变可信行为，原生流式协议还会带来进一步性能损失；安全方面，模型常对有害和合法请求都不作答，而非真正拒绝不安全请求。摘要未给出具体定量分数或SOTA水平，主要贡献是揭示可替换性差距并提出Stream++免训练插件。

Insight: 创新点在于提出Streaming Tax评估框架，将“离线能力保持”与“实际可替换性”区分开，并识别流式适配和流式协议两阶段损失；同时指出历史视觉证据访问受限和可用响应交付失败等原因，以及安全拒绝行为的细粒度问题。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是把部署替代性作为评估目标，并强调流式协议本身对离线任务的影响。

Abstract: Most vision-language models (VLMs) specialize in offline video understanding; in contrast, their post-trained versions, streaming VLMs, can perform live interactions. We investigate whether streaming VLMs can replace their offline parent models on offline tasks. Such replacement could reduce deployment costs and improve efficiency. We propose Streaming Tax to systematically evaluate streaming VLMs on offline tasks. Our exploration spans three core dimensions: understanding, trustworthiness, and generalization across 15 benchmarks. Our findings reveal a critical gap between offline capability preservation and practical replaceability. This gap emerges at two stages: streaming adaptation can alter trustworthy behavior even when offline understanding is largely preserved, while the native streaming protocol can introduce further performance losses. Our analyses show that some of these losses arise from restricted access to historical visual evidence and failures to deliver usable responses. Importantly, regarding safety, streaming models often leave both harmful and legitimate requests unanswered rather than genuinely refusing unsafe ones. We then propose Stream++, a training-free plugin to improve streaming VLMs on offline tasks.

[46] EvOrderBench: When do sampled frames establish event order? cs.CVPDF

Xiaoqin Feng, Yuqi Li, Xinyuan Li, Neil Rodrigues, Xianglong Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出 EvOrderBench，一个基于真实视频的事件顺序判定基准，包含 579 个事件族。它固定视频片段和问题，只改变采样帧，构造出能否从帧中判断两个动作先后顺序的不同输入，并标注每个动作是否出现以及帧是否足以确定顺序。论文据此评估视觉语言模型在证据不足时是否会拒绝给出顺序，发现开源模型在可判定时排序接近闭源参考，但在不可判定时正确保留判断的比例明显不足。

Details

Motivation: 现有强制选择式时间顺序基准默认所有输入都可回答，容易奖励猜测；而可回答性基准又未区分“帧中缺少某个动作”和“帧中两个动作都出现但不足以确定先后”两种情况。论文希望评估模型是否只在采样帧真正建立事件顺序时才作答，并在证据不足时正确保留判断。

Result: 在 16 个开源权重配置和 3 个闭源参考模型上，最好的开源模型在帧足以确定顺序时排序准确率接近参考模型；但在帧不足以确定顺序的输入上，正确保留判断的比例最高仅 83%，而所有闭源参考至少达到 90%。强制选择准确率无法揭示这一差距；思考模式可缩小差距，主要改善对充分与不充分证据的区分。

Insight: 创新点在于用同一事件族仅变化采样帧来控制变量，并细粒度标注动作出现与顺序可判定性，从而把“会排序”和“知道何时不能排序”分开评估。可借鉴之处是设计需要模型主动拒绝作答的时序基准，并将证据充分性判断与最终排序能力解耦分析。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are often asked which of two actions in a video happened first, given only a few sampled frames. Such frames can show that both actions occurred without showing which came first, so a reliable model should state an order only when the frames establish it. Forced-choice temporal benchmarks treat every input as answerable and reward lucky guesses, while answerability benchmarks do not separate frames that miss an action from frames that show both actions but not their order. We introduce EvOrderBench, a real-video benchmark of 579 event families. Each family fixes an episode and a question and varies only the frames: of two four-frame inputs, only one establishes the order, and two six-frame inputs extend the other one with either the frames that resolve the order or frames that do not. Every input is labeled for whether its frames establish each action and their order. Across sixteen open-weight configurations and three closed-source references, the best open models order nearly as accurately as the references when the frames establish the order, but withhold correctly on at most 83% of the inputs where they do not, against at least 90% for every reference; forced-choice accuracy cannot reveal this gap. Open models withhold mainly when the frames show neither action; once the frames show one or both, most of them commit to an order. Thinking narrows the gap, even when it is switched on within a single checkpoint, mainly by separating sufficient from insufficient evidence rather than by making models more cautious overall. Accurate ordering thus does not imply knowing when the frames fall short, and benchmarks should reward withholding alongside ordering.

The pipeline couples controllable scene geometry and motion with a sensor-informed observation model that accounts for photon shot noise, dark counts, and spatial crosstalk. We evaluate the pipeline through calibrated photon-count comparisons and zero-shot sim-to-real transfer across three downstream perception tasks. We show that classifiers trained only on synthetic SPAD data achieve high accuracy on real low-light recordings at illumination levels less than 2 lux, without real-data fine-tuning. Abstract: In real-world deployments, vision systems must operate under varying environmental conditions, such as illumination changes. Recent work has explored adaptive sensing strategies that select camera parameters according to the current environment and downstream task to improve task performance. Reliable physical benchmarks are therefore essential for evaluating such strategies. We study ImageNet-ES-Diverse, a physical benchmark that evaluates the adaptive exposure control strategy Lens by recapturing printed ImageNet images under varying illumination conditions. We construct four controlled acquisition pipelines that vary printed-image scale and camera zoom, and conduct a pilot study with directly captured physical objects. We find that the acquisition choices substantially change benchmark difficulty on all downstream baselines. Moreover, the average classification-accuracy advantage of Lens over auto exposure observed on the original benchmark does not persist in either our collected pipelines or real-object pilot study. Oracle exposure selection nevertheless retains substantial gains, indicating remaining potential for sensor control. TL;DR: 论文提出 ArtLang-4D，从单目 RGB-D 视频中重建带关节的物体（articulated object），并为其构建以“关节状态归一化时间 t”而非视频时间为索引的动态语言场。几何表示采用规范化三角面片场，面片沿从视频中无初始化发现的带类型螺旋关节运动；语言场由带 t 标签的描述库监督，用比较性状态短语在开放词表文本空间中描述各部件。给定自由文本查询时，模型联合优化部件与状态相关性，返回对应部件及其关节状态，并可只驱动该部件沿恢复的关节执行命令。

Details

Motivation: 现有 3D 语言场对每个点只附加一个特征，无法区分“抽屉拉出最多”“门半开”这类关于关节状态的描述；4D 语言场以视频时间为条件，缺乏运动学含义且用户无法设定。论文旨在让语言描述与关节状态直接关联，并支持语言查询与指令执行。

Result: 在 Articulate-100 的 100 个物体上，ArtLang-4D 恢复的关节和部件几何比重建与关节建模基线更准确，检索目标状态比状态无关的语言场可靠得多，执行语言命令的运动误差也更低。

Insight: 核心创新是以关节状态归一化时间 t 替代视频时间作为语言场索引，使语言查询与运动学状态对齐；同时无关节初始化地从单目视频发现带类型螺旋关节，并通过联合优化部件与状态的相关性来回答自由文本查询与执行命令，这种“状态感知”的语言场设计值得借鉴。

Abstract: Language that describes an articulated object is often about its state: the drawer pulled out the furthest, the door opened halfway, push the drawer all the way in. Existing 3D language fields attach one feature to each point and cannot tell these descriptions apart, and 4D language fields condition on video time, which has no kinematic meaning and cannot be set by a user. We present ArtLang-4D, which reconstructs an articulated object from a single monocular RGB-D video and equips it with a dynamic language field indexed by articulation time t, the normalized state of its joints, instead of video time. The geometry is a canonical triangle field whose triangles follow typed screw joints, discovered from the video without any joint initialization. The language field is supervised by a t-tagged caption bank that describes each part with comparative state phrases in a whitened open-vocabulary text space. A free-form query is answered by maximizing part relevance jointly over parts and states, which returns both the part and the articulation state it describes; a command is then executed by moving only that part along its recovered joint. On the 100 objects of Articulate-100, ArtLang-4D recovers more accurate joints and part geometry than reconstruction and articulation baselines, retrieves the queried state far more reliably than a state-agnostic language field, and executes language commands with lower motion error.

[50] AVERT: Anomalous Value Erasure with Response Testing against Visual Prompt Injection cs.CVPDF

Newt Hue-Nam K. Nguyen, Jui-Yu Ho, Wei-Han Chen, Pin-Yen Ting, Yangqiu Song

TL;DR: 论文提出 AVERT，一种针对视觉语言模型视觉提示注入的防御方法。它通过比较输入与干净图像在注意力头输出上的统计差异，识别异常 value 子空间并投影去除注入内容；同时仅对依赖这些方向的答案执行擦除，以降低对良性输入的影响。

Details

Motivation: 视觉提示注入可使 VLM 忽略用户问题并输出攻击者指定内容，而现有扩散净化、图像 token 掩码等防御会显著损害良性输入上的模型效用，因此需要更精准、低副作用的防御。

Result: 在 Qwen2-VL、Qwen2.5-VL、LLaVA、InternVL2.5 四个模型和四个注入基准的白盒攻击下，AVERT 将平均攻击成功率从 86-90% 降至至多 0.6%，并在十三个基准上平均保留至少 96% 的无防御效用；其防御-效用 F-score 为 98.0-99.1，在每个模型上均高于对比防御方法。

Insight: 创新点在于将防御从全局净化或 token 掩码转为条件性特征擦除：先用注意力头输出统计定位异常 value 子空间，再通过答案依赖测试决定是否移除这些方向。客观看，其可借鉴之处是按模型响应依赖性触发防御，从而在阻断注入内容的同时尽量保留良性视觉信息。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) are vulnerable to visual prompt injection, in which an adversarially perturbed image forces the model to respond with the attacker’s chosen content, such as malicious tool calls, website links, or advertisements, sometimes regardless of the user’s question. Existing defenses, such as diffusion-based purification and image-token masking, mitigate these attacks at a substantial cost to the model’s utility on benign inputs. To address this challenge, we propose AVERT, which removes adversarial feature directions only when the model’s answer depends on them. Our key procedures involve identifying anomalous value subspaces and detecting answer dependence. To identify the anomalous value subspace, we compare the statistics of each input’s attention-head outputs with those of clean images and extract the directions along which the input deviates the most. We then project out these directions to remove the injected content. Because the same directions also carry benign visual information, we apply the removal only to answers that depend on them, namely answers whose likelihood collapses once the directions are removed or that are as likely under unrelated questions as under the user’s question. Against white-box attacks on four VLMs (Qwen2-VL, Qwen2.5-VL, LLaVA, InternVL2.5) and four injection benchmarks, AVERT lowers average attack success from 86-90% to at most 0.6% while retaining at least 96% of undefended utility averaged over thirteen benchmarks. Its defense-utility F-score ($F_{DU}$), the harmonic mean of attacks stopped and utility retained, is 98.0-99.1 and the highest among all compared defenses on every model.

Abstract: Egocentric video records what a person looks at, but often misses what their body does: the hands leave the frame, and arm motion goes unseen. An exocentric camera recovers this context, but it must be installed wherever the activity happens. Active acoustic sensing can recover it from the wearables themselves: a device’s speaker emits inaudible chirps, and its microphone senses the motion of the hands, arms, and nearby objects from their echoes. Yet no public dataset pairs it with egocentric video. We present EgoCARBS, with two deliverables. The first is a multimodal dataset and benchmark: 15 participants cook unscripted meals in their own kitchens wearing camera glasses and an off-the-shelf smartwatch that both sense with active acoustics, synchronized with egocentric and exocentric video and the watch’s audio and IMU, with 4,019 verb-noun action annotations in 11.4 hours. The second is a study of what the wearables add to egocentric video. On verb recognition, they add at least as much as an exocentric camera for participants seen in training, and even more for new participants, for whom the exocentric camera does not help. Active acoustics alone provides most of this gain, and helps most where the camera does not see the hands. Trimmed action recognition, detection, and anticipation remain open. Lebin Zhou, Zhenghao Chen, Nam Ling, Wei Wang, Wei Jiang

Details

Motivation: 现有生成式视频压缩将高层语义和密集视觉细节压缩到统一潜在空间，导致熵编码和解码复杂，激进压缩下易出现语义漂移。LLMVC旨在利用视频大语言模型的总结能力提供高层语义指导，改善表示质量和语义一致性。

Abstract: Recent generative video methods achieve strong perceptual compression by encoding videos into compact latent representations and reconstructing them with powerful generative decoders. However, visual encoders must compress both high-level semantics and dense visual details into a unified latent space, which complicates entropy coding and decoding and may cause semantic drift under aggressive compression. In this work, we propose Large Language Model-Guided Video Compression (LLMVC), which leverages the summarization capability of frozen video large language models (Video-LLMs) to generate compact semantic tokens aligned with temporal latent slices for guiding video compression. Specifically, Hybrid Bidirectional Bimodal Attention (HBBA) enables bidirectional interactions between visual and semantic features during encoding and decoding. Semantic-Conditioned Cross-Attention (SCCA) incorporates semantic features into probability modeling during entropy coding. These semantic features provide high-level guidance throughout the pipeline, improving representation quality and encouraging semantic consistency through a transmitted text side channel. Experiments on UVG, MCL-JCV, and HEVC show that LLMVC consistently improves compression performance over traditional and recent neural video compression methods in terms of LPIPS and DISTS, while also achieving better fidelity than recent perceptual codecs.

Details

Motivation: 现有方法将运动标记器与语言模型分两阶段训练，目标不一致：标记器仅最小化重构误差，生成器依赖刚性one-hot参考标记；直接端到端使用直通估计器又面临有偏梯度和码本坍塌，因此需要一种对齐标记与生成质量、并能稳定端到端联合优化的框架。

Result: SEEL在CSL-Daily、How2Sign和Phoenix-2014T三个手语生成基准上取得最先进（SOTA）性能，表明其端到端协同进化策略有效提升了3D手语生成质量。

Abstract: 3D sign language generation aims to produce signing motion from written text. Motivated by the linguistic nature of sign language, recent methods combine a motion tokenizer with a language model to predict motion tokens from text. However, both components are optimized through a two-stage pipeline with mismatched objectives: the tokenizer minimizes reconstruction error in isolation, while the generator learns from rigid one-hot reference tokens. A straightforward end-to-end extension bridges discrete token selection with straight-through estimators (STE), suffering from biased gradients and codebook collapse. To address these limitations, we propose SEEL, an end-to-end framework that aligns tokenization and generation with motion quality through two complementary mechanisms. First, to overcome rigid one-hot supervision, reconstruction-aware generation decodes candidate tokens in context and converts their 3D motion errors into soft targets, guiding the generator with physical motion quality. Second, to propagate generation feedback without STE, generation-aware tokenization passes continuous codebook expectations through the frozen generator, routing generated-motion gradients to the tokenizer to prevent codebook collapse. Combining both mechanisms enables the tokenizer and generator to co-evolve, achieving state-of-the-art performance on CSL-Daily, How2Sign, and Phoenix-2014T. Code and models will be released to facilitate future research.

Abstract: Long-horizon video generation is key to interactive world modeling, physical intelligence, and spatial reasoning, yet maintaining geometric consistency and accurate camera control remains challenging without 3D grounding. We introduce \textbf{VivaWorld}, a framework for generating geometry- and view-consistent long videos from an image. We first construct a compositional hybrid 3D representation that combines reconstructed point clouds, which preserve observed scene geometry and appearance, with generated textured meshes, which provide complete object geometry across $360^\circ$ novel views. Abstract: Transformer-based 3D reconstruction models such as VGGT and $π^3$ predict cameras, depth and point maps directly from many input views. Causal attention models such as StreamVGGT extend them to online video but cache all past tokens, so their total compute grows quadratically with the stream length. Recurrent models such as CUT3R and TTT3R keep a fixed-size state at constant cost, but they progressively forget early geometry. We present LinearVGGT, a hybrid model distilled from StreamVGGT, which uses Mamba2 layers for efficient sequence modeling in three of every four global layers. Our distillation needs only 9% of the GPU hours and 31% of the training frames that StreamVGGT uses to learn from VGGT. Insight: 创新点在于构建统一细粒度动作分类体系和多源标注系统，融合车辆信号、自车位姿、HD 地图拓扑与描述等证据，并标准化时间边界和长尾评测；方法上采用稠密逐步动作监督，并探索与语言模型智能体结合进行驾驶视频检索。

Abstract: Fine-grained ego-vehicle action recognition turns large-scale driving video into structured, searchable driving scenarios, providing a foundation for discovering critical maneuvers and systematically developing and evaluating autonomous driving models. Abstract: Feed-forward 4D trackers recover camera motion, depth, and point trajectories from short video clips, but chaining windows over long videos degrades their accuracy. We find that replacing inter-window alignment with ground-truth transforms leaves tracking error essentially unchanged. Static-point error instead grows with time since last observation: repeatedly predicting a static point from windows that no longer observe it corrupts previously recovered geometry. We call this failure forgetting. Merely retaining the last observed positions of unseen points reduces error by 26% on PointOdyssey without modifying the tracker. We introduce Recall4D, a training-free framework that augments a frozen tracker with a persistent world state and sparse motion anchors. Static points retain their world-space positions outside the camera view and are refined by averaging observations decoded at sparse keyframes while in view. For dynamic objects, the tracker decodes sparse representative anchors, while geometric skinning propagates their displacements to dense queries. Across two long-horizon benchmarks, Recall4D improves the accuracy of its backbone while running 7.6-9.0$\times$ faster, and its accuracy gains carry over to five further backbones and three other long-sequence tasks, demonstrating its generality.

To differentiate these failure modes, we propose VTK-Bench, a benchmark that explicitly separates three information sources: visual information (V), textual information (T), and prior knowledge (K). Abstract: Fine-grained few-shot class-incremental learning (FG-FSCIL) aims to incrementally learn visually similar new classes from limited images without catastrophic forgetting. Prior work typically learns strong representations on base classes or leverages vision foundation models (VFMs) directly as backbones or via knowledge distillation. However, VFM backbones incur high computational costs and do not guarantee fine-grained recognition. Although knowledge distillation reduces inference cost, our preliminary experiments reveal that distilled students still lag substantially behind their VFM teachers, particularly on new classes. To address this limitation, we propose STAND, a systematic VFM-to-student distillation design tailored for FG-FSCIL. Specifically, STAND connects the teacher and student by equipping the student with a fixed copy of the teacher’s classifier, aligning their outputs via projectors, and feeding both models composite images that combine different training images. On these composite images, relation distillation matches how the teacher and the student relate each image to other images, while difference distillation matches how this relation changes between two composite images. Finally, we freeze the student and classify new classes using length-calibrated prototypes, which restore the reduced similarity of few-shot prototypes. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that STAND achieves state-of-the-art performance across FG-FSCIL datasets at low computational cost, while generalizing across student backbones and broader datasets.

[65] VAPOR: Improving Multimodal Reasoning with Sparse Visual Updates cs.CVPDF

Mingyu Su, Wei Wu, Erle Zhu, Chengxing Xie, Deyao Hong

TL;DR: 论文提出 VAPOR，一个无需训练的 MLLM 解码加速框架。它针对长视觉上下文自回归生成中视觉注意力被重复计算的问题，通过跨解码步复用视觉注意力输出、同时持续更新文本注意力，并在需要时按注意力头动态刷新缓存，从而在保持完整视觉 KV 缓存的前提下降低冗余计算。

Details

Motivation: 多模态大模型在长视觉上下文推理时，每个生成 token 都重新计算视觉注意力，即使相邻 token 依赖相似视觉证据，导致计算开销高；现有加速方法可能带来视觉 grounding 漂移或准确率损失。

Result: 在 8 个图像和 4 个视频推理 benchmark、两个 MLLM 家族上，VAPOR 相比 dense attention 在几乎所有 benchmark 上提升准确率，并实现 1.11-1.21 倍解码加速；相比各设置下最强 SOTA 加速基线，准确率再提升 0.35-2.15 个百分点，解码延迟相当或更低。

Insight: 创新点在于训练无关地复用视觉注意力输出但持续更新文本注意力，并用低秩近似估计视觉注意力 mass、结合 prefill 注意力一致性和缓存年龄，为每个 attention head 独立决定 Persist 或 Refresh；客观看，其细粒度动态稀疏视觉更新和保留完整视觉 KV 的設計有助于兼顾加速与长推理中的视觉 grounding 稳定性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made substantial progress in visual reasoning, but extended generation over large visual contexts remains computationally expensive. During autoregressive decoding, visual attention is recomputed for every generated token, even when consecutive tokens rely on similar visual evidence. We introduce VAPOR (Visual Attention Persistence with On-demand Refresh), a training-free framework that reduces this redundancy by reusing visual attention outputs across decoding steps and refreshing them when needed. VAPOR caches the visual attention output while continuing to update textual attention at every step, helping reduce visual grounding drift during extended reasoning. To decide when to reuse cached outputs, VAPOR estimates visual attention mass using a low-rank approximation. It also uses prefill-based attention consistency and cache age to make independent Persist and Refresh decisions for each attention head. VAPOR retains the full visual key-value (KV) cache, so all visual tokens remain available when new evidence is needed. Experiments on eight image and four video reasoning benchmarks across two MLLM families show that VAPOR improves accuracy over dense attention on nearly all benchmarks while achieving 1.11-1.21x decoding speedups. Compared with the strongest state-of-the-art acceleration baseline in each setting, VAPOR further improves accuracy by 0.35-2.15 percentage points while achieving comparable or lower decoding latency. Our code is available at https://github.com/Su-my/VAPOR.

[66] GlassGround: GUI Grounding Beyond Native Screenshots with Egocentric Wearable Vision cs.CV | cs.HCPDF

Yun Wang, Siyue Sun, Jiaying Du, Xinyi Wei, Xiaoming Zhai

TL;DR: 论文提出 GlassGround，一个面向可穿戴设备第一视角相机图像的 GUI grounding 基准，在同一 GUI 状态、指令和目标下配对原生截图与穿戴相机观测，覆盖 Android/iOS、4 种设备和 4 种视角，共 3250 个任务。作者系统评估了截图到相机图像的性能下降，并提出训练期基线 PairDistill 来缩小差距。

Details

Motivation: 可穿戴助手在无法直接获取截图时，需要从相机观测中定位界面元素，但现有 GUI grounding 基准主要评估原生截图，缺乏对真实穿戴视觉场景的系统评测。

Result: 十个开源模型平均 grounding 准确率从截图的 94.8% 降至相机图像的 48.5%。PairDistill 在两个骨干网络上分别提升 9.4 和 12.2 个点，结合局部缩放达到 69.6% 和 64.4%，但仍未完全弥合差距。

Insight: 创新点在于构建配对视角基准以控制 GUI 状态、指令和目标不变，从而隔离视觉域差异；PairDistill 利用配对视图对齐目标表示并结合坐标监督，且推理时不使用截图，可迁移到未见设备和操作系统。

Abstract: Wearable assistants must localize interface elements from camera observations when direct screenshot access is unavailable, yet existing GUI grounding benchmarks primarily evaluate native screenshots. We introduce GlassGround, a new benchmark that pairs native screenshots with wearable camera observations while holding GUI states, instructions, and targets fixed. GlassGround contains 3,250 tasks across Android and iOS, captured using four wearable devices under four viewing conditions. Across ten open-source models, mean grounding accuracy drops from 94.8% on screenshots to 48.5% on camera images. Further analysis reveals that changes in target scale, screen geometry, and background cannot fully explain this performance degradation. We then study two ways to narrow this gap. At inference time, local zooming and screen rectification help but leave most of the gap. For training-time adaptation, we build on GlassGround’s paired-view design and introduce PairDistill, a baseline that aligns target representations across views alongside coordinate supervision. Trained on independently collected data and using no screenshots at inference, it improves camera-image accuracy by 9.4 and 12.2 points on two backbones and transfers to unseen devices and operating systems. Combined with local zooming, it reaches 69.6% and 64.4%, but does not close the gap. The GlassGround dataset and code are available at https://github.com/yunnw/GlassGround.

[67] ScanAR: Parallel Row-Scan Decoding for Efficient Image Generation in Unified Autoregressive Models cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Jingen Qu, Xi Chen, Yingbo Tang, Xinghang Li, Tao Kong

TL;DR: 论文提出ScanAR，一种面向统一多模态自回归模型的并行行扫描解码方法。该方法仅重新定义视觉token的前驱关系：首行视觉token按从左到右生成，后续每一行的所有token在一个并行步骤中根据上一行垂直对齐的token预测，从而将视觉解码深度从H×W降至H+W。ScanAR不改变主干、词表或输出头，也不增加参数，并支持从头训练和轻量后训练。

Details

Motivation: 统一自回归模型的图像生成通常按光栅顺序逐token解码H×W视觉网格，需要H×W个串行步骤并主导推理时间；现有视觉AR加速方法往往修改tokenizer、预测头或模型接口，或难以在不损失质量的情况下实现高并行，因此难以迁移到统一AR模型。

Result: 在Emu3.5-Image-34B上，ScanAR后训练取得GenEval 82.36，为同数据训练的后训练加速方法中最佳，并在相同推理设置下生成512×512图像比光栅解码快13.8倍；在ImageNet上从头训练时，各模型规模均取得最高吞吐，FID与光栅LlamaGen可比；在34B Xiaomi-Robotics-U0上保持具身场景生成、交错图文生成和视频生成的总体质量。

Insight: 创新点在于仅通过重定义视觉token的前驱关系实现行扫描并行解码，将视觉解码深度从H×W降至H+W，且无需修改模型主干、词表、输出头或增加参数；同时兼容从头训练与轻量后训练，具有较强的统一AR模型适配性和实用加速价值。

Abstract: Unified multimodal autoregressive (AR) models represent text and images in one token sequence and generate all modalities with a shared next-token prediction head. Their image generation, however, decodes an $H\times W$ visual grid token by token in raster order, which takes $HW$ sequential steps and dominates inference time. Existing acceleration methods for visual AR generation either change the tokenizer, the prediction head, or the model interface, or keep the model but cannot reach high parallelism without losing quality, so they do not carry over to unified AR models. We propose ScanAR, a row-scan parallel decoding method that only redefines the predecessors of visual tokens. The first visual row is generated from left to right as usual, and each later row is generated in a single parallel step, with every token predicted from the vertically aligned token of the previous row. Nonvisual tokens keep the standard autoregressive rule. ScanAR reduces the visual decoding depth from $HW$ to $H+W$ without changing the backbone, vocabulary, or output head and without adding parameters, and it supports both training from scratch and lightweight post-training. On Emu3.5-Image-34B, ScanAR post-training reaches a GenEval score of 82.36, the best among post-training acceleration methods trained on the same data, and generates $512\times512$ images $13.8\times$ faster than raster decoding under the same inference setting. On ImageNet, ScanAR trained from scratch has the highest throughput at every model size with FID comparable to raster LlamaGen. On the 34B Xiaomi-Robotics-U0 model, ScanAR preserves the overall quality of embodied scene generation, interleaved image-text generation, and video generation.

[68] Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Immediate and Prospective Credit Assignment for Medical Visual Reasoning cs.CVPDF

Yuqi Cheng, Rui Meng, Yijin Li, Chandra Sekhar Reddy Edula, Xiuwei Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出MedPACT，一个面向医学视觉推理的智能体强化学习框架，通过即时答案支持与前瞻性引导两种信用分配来学习选择性观察策略。该方法用内容控制估计器隔离观察内容对后续决策的影响，并在GRPO中把观察信用分配给对应工具调用token，同时保留轨迹级结果监督。模型仅需单个视觉语言模型和图像-问题-答案监督，信用估计只在训练阶段使用。

Details

Motivation: 医学视觉问答需要主动获取有用证据并决定下一步观察位置，但仅依赖最终答案奖励无法揭示哪些观察支持回答，也难以指导后续检查。论文旨在解决稀疏结果奖励下观察行为信用分配不明确的问题。

Result: 在六个医学VQA基准上，基于4B和8B骨干的实验显示MedPACT取得更高准确率、较强的分布外泛化能力，并显著降低推理成本。案例研究还表明其能避免重复观察，并利用已获取证据指导后续检查。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点是将观察信用分解为即时答案支持与前瞻性引导，并通过内容控制估计器在共享真实观察历史下比较真实与匹配替代观察诱导的下一动作。客观来看，其可借鉴之处在于把智能体工具调用级别的信用分配与GRPO结合，并仅在训练阶段进行信用估计，从而在不增加推理成本的情况下提升医学视觉推理的选择性观察能力。

Abstract: Medical visual question answering (VQA) requires acquiring useful evidence and deciding where to look next. Yet final-answer rewards do not reveal which observations support answering or guide subsequent inspection. We introduce MedPACT, an agentic reinforcement learning framework that learns selective observation policies through immediate answer support and prospective guidance. Our content-controlled estimator compares next actions induced by real and matched alternative observations under a shared real-observation history, isolating how observed content improves subsequent decisions. We combine both forms of credit within GRPO, assigning observation credit to the corresponding tool-call tokens alongside trajectory-level outcome supervision. MedPACT uses a single vision-language model and image-question-answer supervision, with credit estimation confined to training. Experiments with 4B and 8B backbones on six medical VQA benchmarks demonstrate improved accuracy, strong out-of-distribution generalization, and substantially lower inference costs. Case studies further illustrate how MedPACT avoids repeated observations and uses acquired evidence to guide subsequent inspection, demonstrating real-world practice potential.

[69] DuoWM:Integrating Dual 6-DoF Visual Flows into Action-Conditioned Drone World Model cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Zixuan Li, Kangyao Huang, Meng Xu, Wan Tian, Xiaoyu Tang

TL;DR: 论文提出 DuoWM，一种面向第一人称无人机视频的动作条件世界模型。它通过动作流将未来相机位姿投影到目标帧，通过物体流对初始观测进行前向变换并叠加局部6-DoF变换，以同时建模相机运动与场景动态。模型还引入变换感知预训练，在标准RGB视频训练前显式学习物体外观与变换轴的关系。

Details

Motivation: 现有动作条件视频世界模型难以将相机指令与视觉动态对齐，也不能充分表示完整的6-DoF场景变换。论文旨在让生成的未来视频更准确地跟随相机轨迹并保持3D一致性。

Result: 在 Sekai-drone 数据集上，DuoWM 在视频质量、3D一致性和轨迹跟随方面均取得最佳结果。相比SOTA方法Captain Safari，真实域FVD和LPIPS分别降低17.2%和21.6%，游戏域AUC@30从0.5003提升到0.5719，AUC@15从0.3088提升到0.3747。

Insight: 创新点在于用动作流和物体流两种互补视觉条件分别约束相机指令与场景运动，并通过变换感知预训练注入显式6-DoF变换先验。其可借鉴之处是将动作条件分解为相机位姿投影与物体局部变换建模，从而增强动作-视觉对齐和3D一致性。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video world models generate future observations from an initial frame and a camera trajectory, but often fail to align camera commands with visual dynamics or represent full 6-DoF scene transformations. We present DuoWM, a first-person drone video world model with two complementary visual conditions. Action flow projects future camera positions and orientations into each target frame, while Object flow forward-warps the initial observation and overlays local 6-DoF transformations to ground the induced scene motion. We further introduce transform-aware pretraining, which pairs object appearance with explicit transformation axes before standard RGB video training. On Sekai-drone dataset, DuoWM achieves the best results across video quality, 3D consistency, and trajectory following. Compared with Captain Safari, the state-of-the-art method, our approach reduces real-domain FVD and LPIPS by 17.2% and 21.6%, respectively, while improving game-domain AUC@30 from 0.5003 to 0.5719 and AUC@15 from 0.3088 to 0.3747.

Qirui Wang, Hengwei Zhao, Xiaoshu Yang, Jingtao Li, Bo Li

TL;DR: 论文面向跨灾害事件的建筑物损坏制图，针对跨事件分布偏移导致深度学习模型泛化受限的问题，提出正无标签域适应范式，并设计PANDA方法，通过少量目标域损坏建筑标签增强目标域判别性。PANDA包含用于判别性表示学习的PAIR和用于监督精炼的RAV，在全球多灾害基准和2025洛杉矶山火场景中验证了有效性。

Details

Motivation: 灾后建筑损坏制图对应急响应和人道援助至关重要，但灾害类型、地理环境、建筑特征和成像条件差异会造成跨事件分布偏移，使模型难以泛化到新灾害事件。现有无监督域适应在损坏比例偏移与损坏外观偏移共存且交互时仍面临挑战，论文希望提升目标域判别性。

Result: 在全球尺度多灾害基准上，PANDA总体优于代表性域适应和PU学习方法；在2025洛杉矶山火新灾害场景中，仅需少量标注损坏建筑即可有效适应，并展示了与地理空间基础模型结合用于跨事件适应的潜力。

Insight: 创新点在于引入正无标签弱监督构建正无标签域适应，仅需目标域少量损坏建筑标签来增强判别性；方法上通过PAIR进行成对对齐与类内鲁棒性学习，并通过RAV进行监督精炼，二者互补强化。客观看，其可借鉴之处是将弱监督判别性提升与域适应对齐结合，用于标签稀缺且分布偏移复杂的跨事件遥感制图。

Abstract: Mapping the damage to buildings following natural and human-caused disasters is essential for disaster response and humanitarian assistance. However, differences in hazard types, geographic environments, building characteristics, and imaging conditions cause cross-event distribution shifts that limit the generalization of deep learning models to new disaster events, even for geospatial foundation models (GFMs) pretrained on extensive Earth observation data. Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) can mitigate these shifts, but adaptation remains challenging when damage-proportion and damage-appearance shifts coexist and interact. We identify enhancing target-domain discriminability as key to addressing this challenge. To this end, inspired by positive-unlabeled (PU) learning, we introduce positive-unlabeled domain adaptation, a paradigm that incorporates PU weak supervision to enhance target-domain discriminability, requiring only a few labeled damaged buildings in the target domain. Building upon this straightforward intuition, we develop Positive-ANchored Domain Adaptation (PANDA), comprising two complementary and mutually reinforcing components: Pairwise Alignment with Intra-class Robustness (PAIR) for discriminative representation learning and Refine Gate and Verifier (RAV) for supervision refinement. On a global-scale multi-disaster benchmark, PANDA generally outperforms representative domain adaptation and PU learning methods. Results on the 2025 Los Angeles wildfires show its effectiveness in new disaster scenarios with few labeled damaged buildings. Further experiments demonstrate the potential of integrating PANDA with GFMs for cross-event adaptation. These findings highlight PANDA’s practical potential for rapid, reliable, and cross-event building damage mapping.

[71] DriftWAM: Faster World-Action Models via Action-Aware Video Drifting cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Minyang Li, Siyu Xu, Yunke Wang, Ying-Cong Chen, Chang Xu

Details

Motivation: 世界-动作模型依赖迭代去噪导致推理成本高，而现有少步蒸馏方法主要追求逐点轨迹和视觉保真度，未充分利用下游动作信息，也缺乏显式的分布级对齐，难以在激进减少步数时保持任务性能。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO 基准以及真实世界机器人操作任务中，DriftWAM 在相同推理速度下的少步设置中持续优于先前的一致性蒸馏方法。

Insight: 创新点在于提出动作感知视频漂移，用联合视觉-动作相似核进行视频分布匹配，并利用动作引导视频正则将动作级梯度直接回传至视频生成器；从客观角度看，其可借鉴之处是将下游动作反馈融入视频蒸馏优化，并结合分布级对齐提升少步 WAM 的任务表现。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) unify future video prediction and action generation for robotic control, but their reliance on iterative denoising incurs substantial inference costs. Distilling these models into few-step generators offers a promising route to efficient deployment, yet maintaining task performance under aggressive step reduction remains challenging. Existing methods rely on point-wise trajectory consistency distillation, which pursues high visual fidelity while leaving downstream action information unexploited in optimizing video generation, and also lacks explicit distribution-level alignment. To address this, we propose DriftWAM, a few-step WAM distillation framework that incorporates the actions induced by predicted videos into video learning. Specifically, DriftWAM integrates action-aware video drifting, which performs video distribution matching through a joint visual–action similarity kernel to steer sample evolution under the guidance of these induced actions, with action-guided video regularization, which leverages the compatible teacher–student matches identified by the joint kernel to backpropagate action-level gradients directly into the video generator. Evaluations on the RoboTwin 2.0 and LIBERO benchmarks, as well as in real-world robotic manipulation tasks, show that DriftWAM consistently outperforms prior consistency distillation methods across few-step settings at the same inference speed.

[72] Dynamic Forcing: Learning Action-Grounded Sparse Dynamic Representations with Dense Future Guidance for World Action Models cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Ziqiang Li, Shisheng Chen, Yunnan Wang, Wenyao Zhang, Zili Lin

TL;DR: 论文提出CompactWAM框架，用稀疏动态token表示未来变化，并引入Dynamic Forcing方法（结合直接动作监督与密集未来引导）学习动作相关的稀疏表示。推理时仅需单次视觉前向传播生成动作，在多个机器人操作基准上以极少token预算取得高成功率。

Details

Motivation: 现有世界动作模型进行密集未来建模时会重复编码当前观测中已有的静态上下文，而动作相关的变化在空间上往往是稀疏的，导致计算冗余。因此需要学习一种紧凑且动作相关的稀疏动态表示。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到98.4%成功率，LIBERO-Plus上77.9%；仅用9.2%的密集未来token预算仍保持98.3%和76.6%的成功率。RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界操作实验进一步验证了有效性。

Insight: 创新点包括：稀疏-稠密联合预测框架无需对稀疏路径使用重建目标；Dynamic Forcing通过动作功能引导和动态感知分布匹配，利用密集未来作为训练时特权信息引导稀疏token聚焦动作相关线索；推理时消除密集未来建模。客观来看，将未来预测稀疏化并借助密集未来监督来提升稀疏表示的动作相关性和动态保真度，是平衡效率与性能的有效思路。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) support robot control through visual prediction, but dense future modeling repeatedly encodes static context already available in the current observation, while action-relevant changes are often spatially sparse. We study sparse dynamic representation learning, retaining the current observation as a contextual anchor and using a small set of compact dynamic tokens to represent anticipated changes. We develop CompactWAM, a sparse-dense joint predictive framework that enables sparse representation learning within a video-supervised shared backbone, without requiring a reconstruction objective for the sparse pathway. Since a limited token budget alone does not specify which information to retain, we introduce Dynamic Forcing, which pairs direct action supervision with privileged dense future guidance to promote action relevance and dynamic fidelity. While action prediction gradients directly drive compact tokens to capture control-relevant cues, dense future guidance further shapes them via action functional guidance that matches sparse and dense contributions to the action expert, and dynamics-aware distribution matching, which aligns temporal relational changes relative to a static reference. At inference, a single visual forward pass encodes current observations and compact dynamic tokens for action generation, eliminating dense future modeling. CompactWAM achieves 98.4% success on LIBERO and 77.9% on LIBERO-Plus, retaining respective success rates of 98.3% and 76.6% with only 9.2% of the dense future token budget. Evaluations on RoboTwin~2.0 and real-world manipulation further demonstrate the effectiveness of the learned sparse predictive representations.

Details

Motivation: VAU 需要推理事件进展、交互和区分异常与常见活动的上下文条件，而现有视频智能体的预定义分析操作难以充分满足多样化的推理需求。

Result: 摘要称在多个 VAU 基准上取得较强性能，并体现进化工具库在不同异常领域间的灵活性；未给出具体数值或明确 SOTA 结论。

Insight: 创新点在于将可复用工具库进化引入 VAU，并用历史修正/回归执行轨迹进行跨版本对比反馈，同时以开发集准确率提升且修正多于回归为条件保守选择候选库，测试阶段固定进化库。

Abstract: Video anomaly understanding (VAU) requires reasoning about event progression, interactions, and the contextual conditions that distinguish abnormal behavior from common activity across diverse scenes. Although video agents enable multi-round analysis, the predefined analytical operations may not adequately satisfy these diverse reasoning demands. In this paper, we present EvoVAU, a framework that evolves a reusable tool library for a frozen video solver. Guided by task outcomes and execution trajectories, an external proposer identifies potential limitations in the library and refines it, retaining task experience as analytical operations that can be reused across questions. Specifically, Cross-Version Contrastive Feedback guides tool refinement by combining current errors with paired execution trajectories from historical corrections and regressions. Adoption-Aware Selection accepts a candidate library only when overall development-set accuracy improves and corrections outnumber regressions among questions on which the solver invokes new or modified tools. Using a small development set, EvoVAU alternates tool refinement with candidate evaluation and selection. At test time, the evolved library remains fixed, with no further involvement from the proposer. Experiments on several VAU benchmarks demonstrate strong performance and the flexibility of the evolved library across anomaly domains.

[74] Probabilistic Monocular Biomechanical Reconstruction via Guided Flow Matching cs.CVPDF

R. James Cotton, Fabian Sinz

TL;DR: 论文提出 Biomechanical Flow Model（BFM），用连续时间 flow matching 学习人体测量参数、关节角度轨迹和虚拟标记的先验，并在测试时以单目 2D 关键点引导，实现从单视频进行概率化 3D 生物力学重建。方法在临床步态数据上能与 SOTA 确定性轨迹优化器取得相当的关节角精度，同时给出可随遮挡变化的不确定性区间。

Details

Motivation: 单视频重建 3D 关节运动存在深度和身高歧义、肢体遮挡以及缺少不确定性估计等问题，论文希望在不依赖配对视频训练的情况下实现概率化、生物力学合理的单目重建。

Result: 在行走和 TUG 临床任务上，BFM 与拟合相同骨架的 SOTA 确定性轨迹优化器关节角精度相当；在留出对照、假肢和神经疾病受试者上，行走和 TUG 的平均绝对关节角误差分别为 3.91° 和 4.97°，并提供覆盖接近名义水平的可信区间。个体人体测量校准可消除大部分单目骨盆深度误差，但在分布外儿童蹲伏步态上会出现有偏且过度自信的伸展预测。

Insight: 创新点在于将 flow matching 作为连续时间生物力学先验，结合多相机临床数据训练和测试时 2D 关键点引导，避免配对视频训练并显式建模不确定性；客观看，其价值主要是把生成式先验、测试时引导和概率校准统一到单目生物力学重建中，同时揭示病理多样性先验的重要性。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D joint kinematics from a single video is ill-posed due to depth and stature ambiguity, limb occlusion, and missing uncertainty estimates. We introduce the Biomechanical Flow Model (BFM), a continuous-time flow-matching prior over subject anthropometry, anatomical joint-angle trajectories, and virtual markers, trained on multi-camera clinical recordings from 565 participants and steered toward monocular 2D keypoints at test time without paired video training. In a head-to-head comparison on the same clinical trials against a state-of-the-art deterministic trajectory optimizer fitting the identical skeleton, BFM achieves comparable joint-angle accuracy across walking and timed-up-and-go (TUG). Across held-out control, prosthetic, and neurologic participants, BFM reaches 3.91 deg and 4.97 deg mean absolute joint-angle error on walking and TUG while providing credible intervals that bridge temporal and whole-limb occlusions. Where keypoints are hidden, the intervals widen with the error and keep their coverage near nominal. Supplying individual anthropometric calibration removes most monocular pelvis depth error, whereas testing on out-of-distribution pediatric crouch gait produces biased, overconfident extension predictions, highlighting the need for pathologically diverse movement priors.

Zexi Zhao, Zizhen Liu, Jing Justin Ye, Huawei Li

TL;DR: 论文提出 FlipVLA，一种无需重新训练、仅通过少量固定参数比特翻转向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型植入对象触发后门的方法。该方法采用两阶段“选择器-放大器”结构，在 FC-GELU-FC 中利用已有视觉特征构造触发响应并放大为动作相关扰动。实验在 OpenVLA-OFT 和 GR00T N1.7 上验证了高攻击成功率且对干净场景性能影响很小。

Details

Motivation: VLA 模型直接驱动机器人动作，恶意模型篡改可能威胁物理安全。论文关注能否在不重新训练的情况下，仅通过少量固定比特翻转植入对象触发后门，并解决触发行为破坏与干净输入性能保持之间的冲突。

Result: 在 OpenVLA-OFT 和 GR00T N1.7 上、四个 LIBERO 套件中，攻击成功率达到 90.22% 至 100%，干净场景任务成功率最多下降 1.5 个百分点。

Insight: 创新点在于仅通过参数比特翻转实现 VLA 后门，无需重训练或数据投毒；方法利用 FC-GELU-FC 中已有视觉特征，通过通道选择器构造触发相关响应、GELU 抑制背景、下游权重放大器转化为动作扰动，并结合通道筛选与离散比特搜索控制修改预算。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models generate robot actions from visual observations and language instructions in real-world environments, making malicious model tampering a potential threat to physical safety. We study whether object-triggered backdoors can be implanted through a small, fixed set of parameter bit flips without retraining. The central challenge is to make persistent weight modifications disrupt trigger-present behavior while preserving performance on non-trigger inputs. We propose FlipVLA, a two-stage selector-amplifier method that exploits existing visual features within an FC-GELU-FC structure. The selector constructs a trigger-dependent response in a selected channel, using GELU to attenuate background activity. The amplifier modifies a connected downstream weight to convert this response into an action-relevant perturbation. Channel screening and discrete bit search construct both components under a limited modification budget. Experiments on OpenVLA-OFT and GR00T N1.7 across four LIBERO suites achieve attack success rates of 90.22% to 100%, with task success decreasing by at most 1.5 percentage points on clean scenes.

Abstract: In-Hand Object Scanning (IHOS) from monocular video captured by a commodity smartphone holds immense potential to democratize 3D digitization for non-expert users, facilitating applications in digital archiving, augmented reality, and custom fabrication. Yet, IHOS remains largely understudied due to a lack of realistic datasets and systematic benchmarks. To bridge this gap, we introduce ToysRUs, a high-quality dataset featuring diverse figurines captured by 4K Ultra High-Definition (UHD) monocular videos. Studying IHOS from first principles, we examine structure-from-motion (SfM) for camera pose estimation, which is key to IHOS. We identify two key challenges: small baselines between consecutive frames, and sparse feature matches across large-view, long-range frames, further exacerbated by dynamic hand occlusions. Guided by these insights, we propose performing SfM on video sub-sequences sampled at sufficient angular intervals, and iteratively stitches the corresponding sub-trajectories into a unified global trajectory. Furthermore, for high-fidelity surface reconstruction, we introduce occlusion-aware 2D Gaussian Splatting, which incorporates opacity rendering and omits the opacity loss on hand regions. Details

Motivation: 现有自回归文本到动作生成方法多关注动作表示和生成顺序，但对条件动作表示的内部形成过程探索不足。论文希望在不改变动作表示和外层生成机制的前提下，改进生成器内部隐状态的演化方式。

Abstract: Autoregressive text-to-motion generation requires local motion predictions to reconcile cross-temporal textual requirements with the available motion context. While existing methods primarily improve motion representations and generation order, the internal formation of conditioned motion representations remains less explored. We introduce MotionODE, a continuous-depth post-training framework that refines hidden-state evolution in pretrained autoregressive generators. MotionODE reinterprets selected Transformer residual branches as local vector fields and replaces their original updates with midpoint Runge–Kutta (RK2) integration, while preserving the motion representation and outer generation mechanism. To address the mismatch between pretrained update paths and newly introduced intermediate states, we propose mixed one-step/two-step RK2 post-training under motion-token supervision, with optional distillation and update-map constraints to preserve pretrained behavior. This minimally invasive framework supports both next-token and next-scale generation without training the generator from scratch. Abstract: This paper proposes a new paradigm and method for giving large language models spatial understanding. Standard language models operate exclusively over discrete words (secondary representations of things rather than continuous space itself), forcing them to guess geometry verbally. Whereas most multimodal approaches equate spatial grounding with camera images or video frames, spatial structure itself is not a picture: it consists of coordinate frames, solid boundaries, and how objects move and relate to each other in continuous space. Motivated by this distinction, we place continuous object tokens combining pose (position, orientation, and scale) and signed-distance shape embeddings alongside word tokens inside a single uninterrupted transformer sequence, allowing language and geometry to attend to each other as object scenes are built and updated step by step. [80] SPiXSeg: Rethinking MLLM-based Image Segmentation as Superpixel Classification cs.CVPDF

Piyush Tiwary, Sohini Dutta, Prathosh A. P

TL;DR: 论文提出 SPiXSeg，将基于多模态大语言模型的图像分割重新表述为超像素分类：先用现成模型将图像划分为与任务无关的超像素，并聚合 patch 特征形成超像素 token；再由 Spix-to-Mask 模块在单次 MLLM 前向中为每个超像素 token 打分，选中的超像素直接映射为像素掩码。该方法无需可训练解码器头、自回归生成或后处理细化。

Details

Motivation: 现有 MLLM 分割方法要么依赖解码器，要么采用 patch 级预测，在边界精度与决策数量之间权衡；粗网格会混合前景背景，细网格预测开销大，自回归掩码生成还带来顺序解码开销。论文希望以更高效且边界更准的方式完成语言引导分割。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are widely used for image segmentation. However, existing methods either use a decoder or use patch-based approach which trades boundary precision against the number of decisions as coarse grid cells mix foreground and background pixels, while finer grids require many more predictions, and autoregressive mask generation adds sequential decoding overhead. We propose SPiXSeg, which shifts the output from per-patch to per-superpixel label assignment, using superpixel tokens formed by aggregating patch-level visual features. An off-the-shelf model partitions each image into task-agnostic superpixels, so the partition is computed once and reused across tasks. A Spix-to-Mask (S2M) module scores each superpixel token in a single MLLM forward pass by directly comparing the MLLM’s hidden representations, without autoregressive generation or a separate scoring network, and the selected superpixels are mapped directly to pixels without refinement. Abstract: Infrared and visible image fusion (IVIF) is essential for robust perception, yet existing methods remain limited under complex degradations. To resolve these limitations, we propose a one-step fusion framework with hybrid reinforcement learning, termed OneHRL. We treat degraded inputs as intermediate noisy states along a generative trajectory, enabling a distilled one-step generator to rectify them directly to the clean fusion results. Multi-teacher distillation can consolidate these capabilities, but direct probability matching does not explicitly capture teacher prediction changes across audio-visual conditions. We introduce OmniMeld, a multi-teacher on-policy distillation method that integrates domain expertise through modality-response-guided distribution matching. We train four teachers covering grounding and correspondence, temporal dynamics and event structure, causal and inferential reasoning, and contextual and holistic understanding. Each sample is routed to its corresponding teacher for supervision conditioned on student-generated answer prefixes. We derive transport costs from teacher prediction changes over shared candidate tokens across audio-visual conditions, using optimal transport to match student predictions to those of the teacher under complete audio-visual input. We also construct OmniMeld-20K for domain-specialized teacher training. The dataset spans four capability domains, with responses structured into evidence, reasoning, and answer sections and verified through consistency checks and media-based review. Experiments on audio-visual and general video understanding benchmarks demonstrate competitive teacher performance among similarly sized open-source models. The student improves over its base model across all evaluated benchmarks and surpasses the teachers’ base model on several benchmarks.

Details

Motivation: 现有视觉脑解码器通常在同语义分布刺激上训练和评估，对未见概念的重建能力缺乏理解。论文通过在目标受试者训练数据中留出语义概念，系统研究概念泛化退化问题。

Result: 在三个解码器、四名受试者和十二个语义概念上，合成 fMRI 数据恢复了未见概念上损失性能的显著部分，并在广泛数据预算下提升通用解码性能，且无需额外耗时 fMRI 采集。

Abstract: Visual brain decoders are typically trained and evaluated on stimuli drawn from similar semantic distributions, leaving their ability to reconstruct unseen concepts poorly understood. We study this concept generalization problem by withholding semantic concepts from a target subject’s training data. We find that three recent fMRI-to-image decoders degrade substantially for the held-out concepts, even when the concept remains available for multi-subject models. In contrast, brain encoders that map a pretrained visual representation directly to voxels are markedly more robust to the same semantic holdout. Motivated by this asymmetry, we use such an encoder to generate synthetic fMRI responses for new images and add the resulting image-fMRI pairs to decoder training. Across three decoders, four subjects, and twelve semantic concepts, synthetic data recovers a substantial fraction of the performance lost on unseen concepts without requiring additional, time-consuming fMRI acquisition. We further show that the same approach improves general decoding performance across a wide range of data budgets. Analyses of encoder training conditions, encoder designs, and synthetic-data controls show that the gains depend on both encoder generalization and the semantic content of the synthetic supervision, rather than merely increasing the amount of training data. Together, our results show that encoder-generated fMRI can transfer semantic generalization to decoders.

[84] X-Matte: VAE-Free Pixel-Space Flow Matching for Single-Step Image Matting cs.CVPDF

Details

Motivation: 现有预训练生成模型用于抠图时通常依赖 VAE，但 VAE 的空间压缩会损失精细 alpha 细节，并带来额外推理开销。论文希望消除 latent 压缩，实现更准确且高效的单步抠图。

Result: 在 P3M benchmark 上性能与 SOTA 无 trimap 抠图方法具有竞争力，并在 RWP-636 和 AIM-500 上展现较好的零样本泛化；高分辨率下相比构建的单步 latent 对照模型最高实现 9.4 倍推理加速。

Insight: 创新点在于 VAE-free 的像素空间 flow matching 抠图范式，以及 trajectory-wide x-prediction 与 transition-aware alpha supervision 的结合，使模型可从中间状态学习并在单次前向中直接输出目标 alpha，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Pre-trained generative models offer rich visual priors for image matting, but their adaptation often relies on variational autoencoders (VAEs), whose spatial compression can degrade fine alpha details and introduce additional inference overhead. To address these limitations, we propose X-Matte, a VAE-free pixel-space flow matching framework for single-step image matting. By eliminating latent compression, X-Matte formulates the RGB-to-alpha transition as a continuous flow directly in pixel space. We adopt trajectory-wide x-prediction with transition-aware alpha supervision, enabling the model to learn from intermediate states while directly predicting the target alpha matte in a single forward pass. This design avoids both VAE encoding and decoding and iterative sampling. Extensive experiments demonstrate performance competitive with state-of-the-art trimap-free matting methods on P3M benchmarks, together with promising zero-shot generalization to RWP-636 and AIM-500. Moreover, X-Matte achieves up to a 9.4x inference speedup over a constructed single-step latent counterpart at high resolution, demonstrating the effectiveness of VAE-free pixel-space modeling for accurate and efficient image matting.

Insight: 论文宣称的创新在于 Road-KSU 自动构建道路中心场景图，并同时提供道路问答/推理目标和地图轨迹合规指标，再结合地理不相交划分形成输出模态与地点熟悉度的 2×2 诊断框架。客观看，其核心价值是把驾驶分数与可迁移道路理解解耦，揭示基准中的地点记忆偏差，并用数据缩放实验识别地理多样性对可迁移道路知识的因果作用，对未来驾驶 VLA 的评测与数据采集具有借鉴意义。

Abstract: End-to-end driving policies increasingly operate without explicit HD maps at inference, raising the question of what road knowledge they actually internalize. To probe this knowledge through model outputs, we introduce Road-KSU, a framework that automatically converts HD maps and actor annotations into a road-centric scene graph. From this, we derive road-focused question answering and reasoning targets, complemented by map-based trajectory compliance metrics. Pairing Road-KSU with geographically disjoint splits yields a $2 \times 2$ evaluation crossing output modality (language vs. action) with place familiarity (seen vs. unseen roads). On NAVSIM, 95% of test samples lie within 10m of a training sample, and scores of 13 published planners on turns rise steeply with the number of training drives through the test location, pointing to memorized road geometry. Retraining on geographically disjoint splits and probing with Road-KSU confirm that action-space performance degrades most on unfamiliar turns, with failures dominated by geometric compliance. Language accuracy, however, drops on road-centric VQA on straight and curved roads alike, and remains stable on dynamic scene content. Scaling the training data further reveals a double dissociation: more drives through the same places improve driving on unseen roads but leave road question answering unchanged, whereas spreading a fixed data budget over more places increases road question answering with little change in driving performance. Motivation: 现有latent视频生成器通常只能训练几秒片段，瓶颈在于视频tokenizer对静态帧和运动帧分配相同token，且生成时需要不存在的未来帧，难以高效支持长视频生成。

Abstract: Latent video generators are trained on clips of a few seconds, and much of this limit lies in their tokenizers, which spend as many tokens on a static frame as on a moving one and need future frames that do not exist during generation. We introduce DeltaRAE, a causal representation autoencoder for video. Its derivative tokenization encodes periodic anchor frames in full and all remaining frames with a few tokens each, and its causal encoder and decoder read a frozen pretrained image encoder. On UCF-101 and Kinetics-600, a standard diffusion transformer trained on DeltaRAE latents is competitive with state-of-the-art video tokenizers. DeltaRAE halves the tokenizer GFLOPs on 16-frame clips relative to keeping the same number of patch tokens for every frame, and it represents a minute of video with 18.8x fewer tokens. The tokenizer trains end to end on one-minute videos, and in video prediction a generator on its latents produces a full minute of video. Abstract: Visual process reward models (PRMs) can detect calculation errors yet assign high scores to steps that misread visual relations. Abstract: Diagnosing whole-slide images (WSIs) requires hypothesis-driven reasoning: pathologists form a differential diagnosis, seek discriminative evidence, weigh its reliability, and stop once it suffices. Consequently, a repeated model error counts as independent evidence for an incorrect diagnosis. We introduce BayesPath, a training-free WSI agent that casts the pathologist’s Scan-Look-Weigh-Decide process as reliability-aware Bayesian reasoning. An explicit belief over the candidates is updated with each observation through a reliability-aware likelihood and guides where to look next and when to stop. This likelihood corrects for the models’ answer bias, discounts correlated observations, and captures diagnostic confusions estimated without labels. BayesPath’s belief is also better calibrated than naive Bayes, so uncertain cases can be deferred to pathologists. Code is available at https://github.com/bryanwong17/BayesPath

TL;DR: 论文提出首个面向自然图像的连续识别记忆（CRM）计算模型，将图像编码、记忆衰减和决策判断统一建模：每张图像被编码为感知空间中的随机变量，记忆衰退表现为分布漂移，判断时从已编码图像分布中采样并与当前图像比较。模型能预测人类观察者逐试次反应，且参数效率很高。

Motivation: 理解人类连续识别记忆有助于机器视觉预测图像是否易被记住、生成更难忘的图像，并辅助记忆障碍诊断和干预；但此前缺乏针对CRM的计算模型。

Result: 模型预测人类观察者逐试次反应的效果落在人类参与者个体差异范围内；仅需4个共享参数和每个观察者2个参数，图像记忆性预测不逊于需数百万参数的先前方法。

Insight: 创新点在于首次为CRM建立统一计算框架，用随机变量分布及其漂移刻画编码与遗忘，并通过采样比较完成再认决策；其参数高效和个体化预测能力可连接人类记忆研究与机器视觉应用。

Abstract: Recognizing an image we have seen before is a basic function of human visual memory. Understanding human continuous recognition memory (CRM) can help machine vision systems predict which images people are likely to remember and generate more memorable images. It can also inform the diagnosis of memory impairments and the development of interventions to improve memory. This paper proposes the first computational model for CRM. The model captures three key components of continuous recognition memory (CRM): image encoding, memory decay, and decision making. It encodes each image as a distinct random variable in a perceptual space. As the memory of an image fades, the distribution of its random variable drifts within that space. To decide whether the current image has been seen before, the observer retrieves samples from the distributions associated with previously encoded images and compares them with the current image. Our model predicts human observers’ trial-by-trial responses in CRM tasks within the variability observed across human participants. Our model is parameter efficient, requiring only four shared parameters and two parameters per observer. It can accurately predict the memorability of an image no worse than prior methods that require millions of parameters.

Abstract: Geospatial foundation model (GFM) research papers are plagued by inconsistent evaluation settings (e.g., different pre-processing and probes), making it difficult to separate performance from evaluation choices. We propose geo-eval-harness}, an open-source framework for controlled model comparisons. We use this harness to evaluate 100 GFMs and general vision models on 6 classification and 14 segmentation datasets, using multiple probes on frozen or low-rank-adapted encoders. We show that under controlled evaluation, general vision backbones are competitive on RGB classification and high-resolution segmentation, but GFMs shine on multispectral tasks. When we compare any two of the top-ten classification models on a single dataset, their 95% confidence intervals overlap 73% of the time. We show that bigger models are not always better: inference FLOPs correlate weakly with segmentation performance. Moreover, translating inference compute into large-area mapping workloads shows how small accuracy differences have outsized deployment cost differences: with frozen encoders on SpaceNet2, a 0.9-point mIoU difference corresponds to 5.9x the compute and days of additional projected GPU time. Our findings stress the importance of comparing models under explicit and fixed settings, and reporting uncertainty and deployment compute alongside accuracy. Our geo-eval-harness makes controlled experiments easy, fast, and fully reproducible. We release the code, configurations, and results at https://github.com/torchgeo/geo-eval-harness.

Abstract: Unsupervised cross-modal homography estimation is challenging because appearance differences obscure shared geometric structures. Separating modality transfer from homography estimation helps reduce these differences, but accurate alignment still requires effective geometric representations and consistency constraints on the two subtasks. We propose GeoCo (Geometry-Aware and Consistency-Constrained Homography Estimation), a framework that addresses these two requirements without cross-modal transformation labels. Its homography estimator, RVHomo, adapts hierarchical features from a frozen vision foundation model (VFM) and fuses them with local CNN features to capture structural context and spatial detail. Two geometric consistency constraints encourage modality transfer to preserve geometry and homography predictions to remain consistent under synthetic geometric transformations. Through two-stage progressive distillation, we further obtain GeoCo-D, which uses the lightweight RVHomo-D estimator. An intermediate model first learns to perform cross-modal matching without modality transfer, then transfers prediction and feature knowledge to the student, eliminating online VFM extraction. Experiments on GoogleMap, OPT-SAR, FSD, and VIS-IR show that GeoCo reduces mean average corner error by 17.4-46.7% relative to the best-performing baseline on each dataset. GeoCo-D also outperforms all compared baselines while using 98.5% fewer parameters and 70.4% fewer FLOPs than the complete GeoCo teacher.

Abstract: Due to the Deep Learning requirement for a large training dataset, Transfer Learning has become a central method in the field of Computer Vision, which heavily used Deep Learning. The common transfer learning practice in computer vision research is to use state-of-the-art architectures in the ImageNet dataset as the backbone network. Abstract: We present SPARC, a geometry-guided attention pruning framework that turns the native self-attention of pretrained video diffusion transformers into a unified mechanism for camera control and persistent scene memory. Our key insight is that attention connectivity can simultaneously serve as geometric conditioning for camera control and spatial addressing for historical memory retrieval. Motivated by the asymmetric contextual requirements of reconstructing observed content and synthesizing novel regions, SPARC restricts observed tokens to geometrically corresponding historical tokens and local spatiotemporal context, while preserving dense attention for unobserved regions. Correspondence-aware attention biasing and order-invariant historical positioning further enable stable retrieval across long temporal gaps. Without introducing additional learnable control or memory modules, SPARC achieves precise camera adherence and faithful scene revisitation while substantially reducing attention computation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of aggressive attention pruning in improving camera adherence, revisit consistency, and visual fidelity, with an inference speedup of over 50x when combined with four-step distillation. Abstract: Given a continuous RGB-D stream and a language request, streaming 3D affordance grounding requires an agent to decide when to respond and where to locate the requested functional part using only observations available so far. Existing 3D affordance grounding methods typically assume complete scene observations, while streaming vision-language models lack fine-grained 3D functional grounding. Our goal is to jointly determine response timing and functional-part localization during continuous scene observation. We propose StreamAff, an evidence-guided response strategy that makes both decisions from the grounding model’s own visual evidence. The model scores the visual patches of the frames it reads: their combined support, weighed against a wait option, decides when to respond, and the highest-scoring patch selects the frame to ground in. A conditional look-back policy lets the model answer from an earlier observation at the moment of the request or wait for new evidence, and zoom-and-refine grounding segments the part named in the answer on the 3D points of the selected frame. We further introduce StreamFun3D, a benchmark derived from real-world 3D scenes that evaluates response timing and grounding across four evidence settings: Current, History, Future, and Unobserved, together with StreamIoU, a metric that credits a mask only when the response is timely and grounded in a valid source frame. We adapt offline 3D affordance grounding methods and build external-decider baselines that pair vision-language models with our mask decoder. StreamAff achieves 30.14% StreamIoU on 15-second streams, a 40.6% relative improvement over the strongest of these baselines.

Existing replay-free methods expand prompts or routed LoRA experts across multi-layer decoders, incurring heavy parameter and routing overhead while shifting internal feature distributions. Instead, we identify the final linear layer of the vision-language projector as a high-capacity, coordinate-stationary bridge, where sequential updates easily induce interface drift by displacing historical visual tokens. While low-rank subspace adaptation offers a compact remedy, selecting directions solely from historical statistics or task gradients sacrifices plasticity or stability, and unconstrained multi-step updates still accumulate large drift. To resolve both issues, we propose Projector Refinement through Optimized Update Directions (PROUD), a continual subspace optimization framework for the terminal projector layer. At task onset, PROUD combines retained input statistics with initial gradients to analytically extract a metric-orthonormal subspace maximizing first-order task gain under a drift budget. During training, it regularizes updates within this fixed subspace via an exact Frobenius penalty before losslessly merging them with zero inference or routing overhead. Experiments on UCIT and CoIN across MLLM backbones show that PROUD outperforms recent replay-free baselines in continual accuracy while curbing forgetting with a fraction of trainable parameters.

Abstract: Generating coherent and explorable 3D worlds from scattered images with little or no visual overlap is a fundamental and long-standing challenge in computer vision, which existing geometry-aware methods fail to address. Meanwhile, existing image- and video-based methods may generate unseen regions but produce inconsistent geometry across views. To bridge this gap, we present WorldBridge, a framework for coherent and controllable 3D world generation under scattered images with little or no visual overlap. WorldBridge encodes scattered references into a global scene context to guide geometry and appearance generation across views. Our temporal and spatial reference attention mechanism further enhances the fidelity of temporal references along the target trajectory and supports flexible spatial references outside it. A plug-and-play latent-reference consistency strategy improves reference fidelity through reprojection-guided test-time optimization. We also introduce the WorldBridge benchmark suite to evaluate 3D world generation from scattered, low-overlap references, which is rarely explored by current benchmarks. Both qualitative and quantitative results demonstrate that our method enables controllable and coherent 3D world generation, surpassing the existing state-of-the-art solutions. [101] TaCo: Rethinking Correlation for Visual Tracking on Edge Devices cs.CVPDF

Qingmao Wei, Fagui Liu, Quan Tang

TL;DR: 论文针对边缘设备上的轻量视觉跟踪，重新审视互相关在模板-搜索交互中的不足，指出注意力虽能同时完成目标条件化与空间匹配，但其softmax、归一化和reshape等内存受限算子在NPU/CPU上开销过高。作者提出TaCo（Target-aligned Correlation），通过目标描述符对搜索特征做通道级缩放与偏移，并用由模板预测权重的目标框对齐卷积核完成相关匹配，从而在不引入注意力的情况下补足条件化能力。

Details

Motivation: 轻量跟踪器为提升精度转向注意力，但在低功耗边缘硬件上注意力算子实际耗时远高于其算术量；而互相关虽快，却只做空间匹配、缺少对搜索特征的目标条件化，除非采用ViT主干又把注意力带回。论文旨在为边缘设备提供兼顾速度与精度的相关式跟踪方案。

Result: TaCo-T在LaSOT上达到62.3% AUC，在Rockchip RV1126B NPU上184 FPS、Raspberry Pi 5 CPU上96 FPS，NPU速度分别为HiT-Tiny和AsymTrack-T的3.0倍和3.5倍且精度更高；TaCo-B达到65.6% AUC、NPU上50 FPS，速度超过HiT-Base、AsymTrack-B和LightFC-vit两倍以上且精度更高。

Insight: 创新点在于将模板-搜索交互拆解为目标条件化与空间匹配，并用每视频仅计算一次的模板操作数实现轻量条件化：通道级仿射调制搜索特征，以及目标框对齐且权重由模板预测的相关核。客观上，其价值在于以NPU友好的矩阵乘和预计算操作替代注意力，在固定主干下取得更好的速度-精度折衷。

Abstract: Lightweight trackers have replaced cross-correlation with attention, and on low-power edge hardware they pay for it, since memory-bound softmax, normalization and reshaping operators of attention cost far more time than their arithmetic suggests. Benchmarking efficient trackers on an edge Neural Processing Unit (NPU) and an Arm CPU under one numerically validated protocol, we find that correlation trackers are the fast ones, but they lag in accuracy unless they adopt a ViT backbone, which brings attention back. We revisit what correlation lacks. Template–search interaction serves two roles: conditioning the search features on the target and matching them against it spatially. Attention does both, whereas correlation only matches, and does so on features extracted without knowledge of the target. We present TaCo (Target-aligned Correlation), which supplies the missing conditioning without attention by letting the template enter the per-frame network only as operands computed once per video. A descriptor of the target scales and shifts the search features channel by channel inside the backbone, and kernels aligned with the target box, each with a weight predicted from the template, correlate with them in one matrix product. With the backbone fixed, our correlation remains essential while incurring negligible NPU overhead, offering a better speed-accuracy trade-off than the attention-based interactions we tested. TaCo-T achieves 62.3% LaSOT AUC at 184 FPS on the Rockchip RV1126B NPU and 96 FPS on a Raspberry Pi 5 CPU, 3.0x and 3.5x the NPU speed of HiT-Tiny and AsymTrack-T at higher accuracy. TaCo-B reaches 65.6% at 50 FPS on the NPU, more than twice the speed of HiT-Base, AsymTrack-B and LightFC-vit, again at higher accuracy. Code and models are available at https://github.com/TsingWei/TaCo-Tracker.

Abstract: Multimodal drafts learn from multimodal (MM) and text-only (TXT) supervision, yet their effects on alignment and how to combine them remain unclear. We diagnose draft-target alignment through paired predictions with and without visual input, decomposing distributional error into text-conditioned and visual-response components. Drafts with similar text-conditioned alignment can still differ markedly in visual-response risk and acceptance. Controlled continuation training shows that high-risk groups outperform random and low-risk groups, and visual-response risk guides MM training more effectively than aggregate risk. Building on these diagnostics, we propose MNRS-Joint, which configures joint supervision from a given draft’s component-risk distributions, using modality-specific mean risks to determine the MM:TXT mixture. Across three warm-ups, its distinct mixtures fall in high-performing regions of the ratio sweeps. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, MNRS-Joint improves mean accepted length from 3.35 to 3.53 and mean speedup from $2.04\times$ to $2.16\times$ over composition-matched random training under greedy tree decoding. Details

Motivation: 研究像素空间扩散的关键设计因素，以理解并提升像素扩散的生成质量，同时探索其相对潜空间扩散的设计规律和可扩展性。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce EiT by exploring the key design factors of pixel-space diffusion from three perspectives: model architecture, representation, and frequency domain. Inspired by Denoising Autoencoders (DAE) which perform denoising through a bottleneck, we construct a U-shaped pixel diffusion transformer that performs explicit compression along the channel dimension. In terms of representation, we find that both pixel and latent diffusion transformers exhibit a three-stage representation dynamics across layers and timesteps: denoising/local feature expansion, basis lift-up, and posterior projection. Moreover, earlier denoising and a longer basis lift-up stage are associated with better generation, which EiT naturally achieves through its channel bottleneck and increased network depth. From the frequency perspective, we establish the equivalence between pixel-space and frequency-space diffusion when keeping the network operating in pixel space. On ImageNet $256\times256$ and $512\times512$, EiT consistently outperforms JiT (Just image Transformers) in generation quality. Specifically, our EiT achieves better FID than JiT: 1.61 vs. 1.85 at $256\times256$, and 1.73 vs. 1.99 at $512\times512$. Remarkably, EiT can be seamlessly applied to video pixel diffusion by simply extending the patch size to temporal dimension and outperforms video latent diffusion in FVD evaluated on Kinetics-600 (resolution 256), under comparable total network parameters. Details

Motivation: 跨模态图像存在显著外观差异，可靠对应关系难以获取；多尺度细化虽缩小搜索范围，但匹配特征区分近邻候选的能力有限。现有VFM特征通过固定插值融合，空间聚合不随图像内容和对应目标自适应。
