Abstract: Cross-modal homography estimation requires reliable correspondences despite appearance differences between sensing modalities. Multi-scale refinement progressively reduces the search range, but its accuracy remains limited by the ability of matching features to distinguish nearby candidates. The vision foundation model (VFM) provides complementary representations, yet integrating these features through fixed interpolation leaves their spatial aggregation independent of image content and the correspondence objective. We propose the Foundation Model-Enhanced Matching Network (FoMNet), which combines learned spatial adaptation of VFM features with contextual correspondence estimation. Patch Feature Upsampling (PFU) progressively predicts location-specific kernels to aggregate neighboring features on upsampled grids, preparing VFM representations for fusion with CNN features at different matching scales. Contextual Correlation Refinement (CCR) then jointly processes source descriptors and local correlations with spatial context to refine candidate scores before each homography update. To reduce upsampling overhead, we develop FoMNet-D, which replaces PFU with bicubic interpolation and uses multi-scale feature-relation distillation to recover accuracy. Experiments on five cross-modal datasets show that FoMNet achieves the lowest mean average corner error (MACE) among the evaluated methods, reducing MACE by 10.6%-72.7% relative to MCNet. Using these datasets, we examine how models use negative examples to identify novel concepts and analyze the respective roles of visual perception and reasoning. Abstract: The 3D points kept in a map decide whether a camera can be localized against it, and this choice matters more as the map becomes sparser. Existing 3D keypoint detectors usually define saliency by semantics, repeatability, reconstruction or descriptor matchability. None of these definitions asks whether a point will actually be detected in an image, and as a result most of the points they select are not detected again in images. In this paper, we define 3D saliency as image detectability: the response of a 2D interest-point detector at a surface point, aggregated over all views in which the point is visible. We propose DetField, a continuous field over 3D space that takes a point cloud as input and predicts detectability at any location around it. Details

Motivation: 现实场景中视频证据常只被部分捕获，可靠模型应能根据证据充分性选择回答、猜测或拒答。现有基准仅做二分类“可回答/不可回答”，无法衡量部分证据场景，也难以区分失败来自缺失证据还是充分性误判。

Result: 在 ClueProbe 上评估 10 个开源与专有 Video LLM，发现模型很少从部分线索猜测；对“可猜测”层级进行监督微调后，适当猜测率从 1.6% 提升到 40.4%，且不增加错误拒答，而传统拒答微调会拒绝 92% 的可猜测问题。

Insight: 创新点在于引入“可回答/可猜测/不可回答”三分类及受控扰动，将“感知证据”与“判断证据充分性”分离诊断；客观看，其价值是揭示 Video LLM 缺乏分级充分性意识和相应监督信号，并提供可复现的评测与微调验证框架。

Abstract: In real-world applications like egocentric recording, visual accessibility, and surveillance review, visual evidence for Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) is often only partially captured. A reliable model should match its response to the evidence: answering when sufficient, guessing from visible clues when partial, and abstaining when absent. Yet existing benchmarks enforce a binary answerable-or-unanswerable split, leaving partial evidence unmeasured and obscuring whether failures arise from missing evidence or misjudging sufficiency. We introduce ClueProbe, a diagnostic benchmark to (1) separate perceiving evidence from judging sufficiency, and (2) validate our diagnosis via targeted fine-tuning. ClueProbe comprises 1,721 human-annotated questions across 598 natural videos, labeled Answerable, Guessable, or Unanswerable, alongside controlled perturbations altering wording or visual input. Evaluating 10 open-source and proprietary Video LLMs reveals that failures stem from judgment rather than perception: models perceive the evidence yet lack graded sufficiency awareness, answering or refusing outright while rarely guessing from partial clues. Targeted fine-tuning shows that this reflects missing supervision rather than insufficient capacity: supervising the guess level lifts appropriate guessing from 1.6% to 40.4% without extra false refusals, whereas conventional refusal tuning rejects 92% of guessable questions. Data and code will be released at https://github.com/suat-llm/ClueProbe.

Abstract: Accurate 3D surface reconstruction from unposed images remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Recent feed-forward pointmap methods eliminate the need for camera poses but inherently produce discrete, sparse representations. In this paper, we present a generalizable pipeline that directly recovers continuous, high-fidelity 3D surfaces from unposed images by seamlessly integrating pointmap- and ray-based representations. Our approach first extracts explicit geometric priors using a pre-trained pointmap model. A query-view-centric selection module then identifies geometrically relevant reference views via a robust surfel voting mechanism. Next, a cross-attention regressor bridges target query rays with reference features to estimate a continuous coarse surface. Motivation: 主流视觉编码器很少显式建模图像中的几何对称性，作者希望实证探究等变归纳偏置在图像分类中何时以及为何有帮助，并避免插值或边界伪影带来的干扰。

Result: ConvNeXt-C4从头训练时在所有任务上优于基线，且参数约少6倍、FLOPs约少1.6倍；其性能优势随训练集规模减小呈对数线性增长。分析分类头发现方向信息被有效利用，在所有数据集上带来4.90%的性能提升。

Abstract: Equivariant neural networks promise improved representation learning through structural guarantees. However, mainstream vision encoders rarely incorporate equivariance for most geometric symmetries. Images are a particularly interesting modality with many such symmetries and extensive datasets from which to learn this structure. In this work, we investigate when vision encoders benefit from geometric inductive biases. We study the discrete rotation group of quarter-turns (C4) which acts losslessly without interpolation or boundary artifacts. Combined with image classification as a controlled invariant objective, this allows for a principled empirical investigation into the role of structural guarantees. We introduce ConvNeXt-C4 as a rotation equivariant ConvNeXt with dimension-equivalent yet orientation-structured representations. Training from scratch, our experiments show, that the C4 variant outperforms its base model across all tasks while operating with 6x fewer parameters and 1.6x fewer FLOPs. Analyzing the classifier head reveals that orientation information from the representations’ structure is actively used, improving performance by 4.90% across all datasets. Our results highlight the sample-efficiency benefits of equivariance and the value of retained orientation information, motivating further investigation beyond image classification. TL;DR: 论文提出HermiteVFI，用每个像素的三次Hermite连续轨迹替代传统按查询时间重采样端点光流的两帧视频帧插值方法。方法以端点位移固定轨迹两端位置，并从帧对学习两个潜在端点导数来控制中间路径；轨迹系数每帧对预测一次，可在任意t闭式求值，并结合多时间监督。

Details

Motivation: 传统两帧视频帧插值常按查询时间重采样端点光流，等价于选择恒速直线路径，但相同端点位移可对应多种中间运动轨迹，难以处理非均匀运动和高插值比例。论文希望显式建模每像素连续运动轨迹，并解决仅由端点位移无法确定内部路径的问题。

Result: 在X4K1000FPS、SNU-FILM和Vimeo90K-Septuplet上验证，最大增益出现在困难非均匀运动和高插值比例场景；摘要未给出具体定量数值或明确SOTA结论。

Insight: 创新点在于用三次Hermite曲线以两个位置和两个导数边界量显式表示每像素轨迹，残差形式精确包含线性运动并结构上保持测量端点位移；同时指出单一内部查询时间对两个自由系数约束秩亏，而两个不同内部时间在表示层面满秩，从而支持多时间监督，并利用外观方向线索推断潜在导数。

Abstract: Two-frame video frame interpolation commonly resamples endpoint optical flow by the query time, which selects a constant-velocity straight-line path even though the same endpoint displacement is compatible with many intermediate trajectories. We present HermiteVFI, which replaces independent query-time motion prediction with one explicit continuous trajectory per pixel. The measured endpoint displacement fixes the two trajectory positions, while two latent endpoint derivatives learned from the frame pair control the interior path. A cubic Hermite curve is the minimum-degree polynomial that can independently satisfy these two position and two derivative boundary quantities. Its residual form contains linear motion exactly, structurally preserves the measured displacement endpoints for any network output, and has coefficients that are predicted once per frame pair and evaluated in closed form at arbitrary t. We further show that one interior query time gives a rank-deficient constraint on the two free trajectory coefficients, whereas any two distinct interior times are full rank at the representation level, motivating multi-time supervision. Appearance-derived directional cues help infer the latent derivatives that endpoint displacement alone cannot identify. On X4K1000FPS, SNU-FILM, and Vimeo90K-Septuplet, the largest gains occur for difficult non-uniform motion and high interpolation ratios. The amortization applies to trajectory-coefficient prediction; downstream reversal, refinement, and synthesis remain query dependent.

Motivation: 现有网格游戏基准大多只报告端到端成功率，无法区分失败究竟源于视觉感知、规则应用还是多步规划，导致难以指导模型改进。

Result: 在 Sokoban 与迷宫游戏上，闭源模型主要受限于多步规划，开源模型则受限于规则应用或视觉网格索引（行列计数）；当规划任务变简单时，同一模型的视觉代价从 7.6 分升至 37.7 分，说明规划失败会掩盖感知失败；仅对感知受限模型的视觉模块在“读棋盘”数据上微调，可使其交互式 Sokoban 成功率从 5.8% 提升到 20.4%（3.5 倍），并零样本迁移到未见迷宫游戏（单轮成功率 13.8%→67.1%），而同样数据对规则受限模型无效。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel at static visual understanding and are increasingly applied to spatial planning, where a model interprets a visual layout and outputs an action sequence to reach a goal. Grid games are a common testbed for this ability because their rules are explicit and a solver can verify every move. Yet existing benchmarks mostly report end-to-end success rates, which hide whether a failure stems from visual perception, rule application, or multi-step planning. We introduce GridProbe, a diagnostic benchmark that 1) locates the failing stage between visual perception and planning and 2) verifies the diagnosis by targeted fine-tuning. On Sokoban, GridProbe pairs end-to-end solving with four probes (recognition, localization, single-step prediction, and state tracking) and gives every board both as an image and as an equivalent text grid. Evaluating seven MLLMs on Sokoban and a maze game reveals distinct bottlenecks behind similar success rates: proprietary models are limited by multi-step planning, whereas open-source models are limited by rule application or by visual grid indexing, i.e., counting rows and columns. Planning failures also hide perception failures: when planning is easier, the visual cost of the same model grows from 7.6 to 37.7 points. The diagnosis identifies what to train: fine-tuning only the vision module of the perception-limited model on board reading raises its interactive Sokoban success 3.5-fold (5.8% to 20.4%) and transfers zero-shot to the unseen maze game (single-turn success 13.8% to 67.1%), whereas the same data does not help a rule-limited model and training on solver solutions brings no gain. We will release the benchmark and code at https://github.com/suat-llm/GridProbe.

Motivation: 跨被试 EEG-to-image retrieval 会因 subject shift 在新被试上显著性能下降，而 TTA 可利用无标签目标被试 EEG 自适应预训练模型。现有 TTA 方法难以让监督粒度匹配每个 query 的预测置信度，导致许多 query 缺少可靠伪标签。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG-to-image retrieval suffers from substantial performance degradation on new subjects due to subject shift. Test-time adaptation (TTA) can mitigate this shift by adapting pretrained models with unlabeled target-subject EEG. However, most existing TTA methods struggle to match supervision granularity to each query’s prediction confidence, leaving many queries without reliable pseudo-labels. To address this, we propose a novel framework named Hierarchical Selective Test-Time Adaptation (HSTTA). HSTTA builds a hierarchy over gallery image embeddings, uses hierarchical selective prediction to assign each EEG query a supervision granularity matching its current prediction confidence, and constructs adaptation objectives accordingly. We further propose Cross-Session Consistency Calibration (CSCC), an unsupervised method that calibrates the global confidence threshold to target a predefined accuracy level for hierarchical predictions. Moreover, we introduce Reciprocal Hierarchical Backoff (RHB), which moves queries lacking reverse-matching support to coarser nodes to mitigate error reinforcement. Experiments on two public benchmarks and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of HSTTA and its key components. On THINGS-EEG2, HSTTA outperforms the strongest EEG-specific TTA baseline by 17.9 (13.4) and 8.9 (3.7) percentage points in Top-1/Top-5 accuracy, respectively, under cosine (CSLS) ranking. Further analyses show that HSTTA yields more accurate pseudo-labels during adaptation, supporting more reliable model updates.

Our repair model follows a two-stage training strategy that separates underwater appearance adaptation from geometric refinement. The first stage learns underwater appearance from reference observations and estimated medium features. The second uses camera poses and reference depth to align reference features, with relative-depth supervision guiding structural repair without requiring target-view depth at inference. To address this, we propose Modality-Shift Contrastive Decoding (MSCD), a training-free decoding method that emphasizes audio-visual temporal alignment in model predictions. Insight: 创新点包括运动保持的4D VAE、结合几何引导监督与预测重建的RGB VAE、轻量适配预训练RGB表示的几何VAE，以及共享权重和跨模态连接的双流循环扩散Transformer。客观看，其可借鉴之处在于通过跨循环复用Transformer块增加有效深度而不复制骨干参数，以额外计算换取少参数下的联合RGB-几何预测能力。

Abstract: Predicting the outcomes of robotic manipulation requires modeling both visual appearance and evolving 3D geometry. This poses two challenges: video compression can obscure fine-grained robot motion, while jointly predicting RGB and geometry demands sufficient generative capacity. We introduce Loop4D, a framework that addresses these challenges through motion-preserving 4D representations and recurrent generative refinement. Our 4D VAE encodes RGB and pointmap sequences into compact spatiotemporal latents while preserving motion details and geometric structure. Our RGB VAE combines geometry-guided supervision with predictive reconstruction to reduce compression-induced motion artifacts. Our geometry VAE uses lightweight adapters to adapt pretrained RGB representations while preserving their priors. Building on these representations, our Dual-Stream Looped Diffusion Transformer maintains separate appearance and geometry streams with shared Transformer weights and cross-modal connections. By reusing Transformer blocks across loops, we increase effective depth without replicating backbone parameters, enabling models with fewer parameters to improve joint RGB-geometry prediction through additional computation. Experiments demonstrate high-quality 4D generation and validate Loop4D in simulated robotic manipulation tasks.

Result: 摘要称大量实验表明ACD与现有CD方法结合（如VCD+ACD）能显著提升输出连贯性并进一步减少幻觉，同时消除限制性惩罚约束；但未给出具体benchmark名称、定量指标或SOTA对比细节。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) demonstrate impressive multimodal capabilities, yet suffer from hallucination - generating factually inaccurate content. Contrastive Decoding (CD) mitigates this by contrasting amateur and expert branches at the logit level. However, our investigation reveals that such logit-level interventions fundamentally compromise generation coherence, necessitating restrictive penalty constraints unrelated to hallucination suppression. We introduce Attention Contrastive Decoding (ACD), a training-free plug-in that complements logit-level CD by relocating part of the contrastive operations to the attention mechanism. Operating at an earlier stage of the forward pass, ACD performs smooth semantic-preserving interventions through an Adaptive Subtraction Strategy (ASS), which attenuates hallucination-associated attention patterns while amplifying critical visual information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that combining ACD with existing CD methods (e.g., VCD+ACD) produces substantially more coherent outputs with further reduced hallucinations, eliminating restrictive penalties while enabling trustworthy multimodal generation.

Abstract: Cross-modal self-supervised learning for paired side-scan sonar and underwater optical imagery is a promising direction for benthic habitat understanding, but it remains underexplored because existing methods do not fully exploit the one-to-many correspondences and geographic metadata available in real opti-acoustic surveys. This limits the ability to learn representations that are both semantically meaningful and geometrically consistent from abundant unlabeled marine data. To address this problem, we present GeoCAM, a geography-guided framework that learns a shared embedding space for sonar tiles and their associated optical views without semantic annotations. GeoCAM achieves its goal by combining three components: (i) a Set Attention Aggregator that adaptively summarizes multiple optical observations into a single tile-level representation; (ii) a geometry-weighted InfoNCE objective that converts GPS-derived footprint overlap into soft positive supervision; and (iii) an optional cross-modal masked reconstruction branch that encourages physically grounded cross-modal alignment. Abstract: Self-supervised video representation learning has achieved remarkable progress by leveraging large-scale unlabeled video data. Recent methods commonly extend Masked Image Modeling (MIM) to Masked Video Modeling (MVM), where video representations are learned by capturing semantic information from temporal contexts during masked content recovery. Unlike static images, videos exhibit inherent temporal variation, as visual objects continuously change their positions, appearances, and motion states across frames. Therefore, MVM needs to preserve semantic consistency while capturing temporal changes of corresponding regions, which raises two central challenges: 1) how to establish reliable temporal correspondence across frames, and 2) how to formulate an appropriate representation learning objective for modeling both semantic consistency and temporal variation. To address these challenges, we propose Variation-aware Temporal Correspondence for video Representation learning (VT-CoRe) framework. For challenge 1), VT-CoRe introduces a sandwich sampling strategy to construct two-sided temporal references and a Patch Registration Module (PRM) to establish reliable patch-level correspondence. For challenge 2), VT-CoRe imposes latent-space semantic alignment on registered representations through a self-distillation architecture and further proposes temporal variation modeling to constrain representation trajectories over time. Experiments on 8 downstream tasks across 16 benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and applicability of VT-CoRe across pixel-level, frame-level, video-level, and vision-based robotic learning scenarios. Complementary representation analysis shows that VT-CoRe learns a more structured latent space for variation-aware temporal correspondence learning.

Abstract: Physical hand interaction depends on configuration, contact, and force, yet nearly identical appearances can conceal different contact states and exerted forces. We therefore formulate physical hand state estimation from egocentric video and wrist surface electromyography (sEMG), combining visual geometry with neuromuscular evidence to jointly recover these coupled components. To support this task, we introduce EgoMyo, a benchmark spanning 5.75M video frames, 50 participants, 34 contact configurations, and repeated donnings, synchronizing these inputs with 3D hand pose and with contact and normal force registered onto the hand surface from bare-hand pressure. Controlled analysis reveals that vision resolves spatial structure while sEMG better estimates force magnitude, that sEMG’s gains concentrate in regions where vision is least reliable, and that contact localization limits force accuracy. Guided by these findings, we propose MyoBridge, which makes the hand region the unit of vision-sEMG fusion, lets each region gate its own share of sEMG, and distributes regional force magnitudes only over predicted contact vertices at inference. Abstract: Code-driven video generation asks a coding agent to write and run rendering programs. This gives exact control over typography, data visualizations, synchronized audio, and multi-shot scenes, and it leaves an editable project behind. A program that exits cleanly can still render overlapping labels, a camera aimed away from its subject, broken object interactions, or narration that drifts out of sync, and none of these raise a runtime error. Feeding rendered frames and audio back into the coding session bloats its long-horizon context, while critiquing only the finished full-resolution render turns early design mistakes into costly rewrites. We introduce VibeVideoAgent, a training-free framework that lets coding agents inspect their drafts throughout production. Multimodal perception runs in short-lived contexts kept apart from the coder and returns compact, grounded Findings. Seven primitives operate on a unified multi-clock representation of assets and drafts: they explore media from coarse to fine, verify quoted requirements with evidence from every required modality, compare a draft with a reference or an earlier revision on separate clocks, and measure stream signals locally. A lightweight staged protocol has the agent test its riskiest decision on a minimal preview first. [122] TORE: Token-Wise Taylor Order Selection via Decomposed Residual Error Estimation for Diffusion Acceleration cs.CVPDF

Lei Tang, Baoquan Zhang, Yakun Zhang, Yunming Ye

TL;DR: 论文针对扩散模型推理延迟大的问题，指出现有token自适应加速方法使用浅层代理分数选择泰勒阶数会导致代理-目标不匹配，提出TORE方法，通过估计全模型残差的预测误差来选择token-wise泰勒阶数，并将误差分解为截断误差和有限差分导数误差进行估计，从而在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1上实现4倍以上加速，同时保持生成质量。

Details

Motivation: 现有token自适应加速方法用浅层代理分数评估泰勒阶数，但该阶数用于预测全模型残差，导致代理偏好与最小化残差预测误差的目标不一致。TORE旨在解决这一代理-目标不匹配问题，直接基于全模型残差的估计误差选择阶数。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev上实现4.31倍加速，在Wan2.1上实现4.18倍加速，同时保持高生成质量和保真度。

Insight: 创新点在于：1) 提出基于全模型残差预测误差的token-wise泰勒阶选择，避免代理-目标不匹配；2) 将预测误差分解为截断误差和有限差分导数误差，并利用缓存残差进行估计，无需未来真实残差。客观分析认为，这种误差分解估计方法有效解决了阶选择中的信息缺失问题，提升了加速效果。

Abstract: Diffusion models excel at image and video generation, but iterative denoising incurs substantial inference latency. Recent token-adaptive acceleration methods select Taylor prediction orders for the full-model residual using shallow-layer proxy scores. However, candidate orders are evaluated on these proxy features but used to predict the full-model residual, creating a proxy–target mismatch: the proxy-preferred order may not minimize residual prediction error. To address this mismatch, we propose \textbf{TORE} (\textbf{T}aylor \textbf{O}rder selection via \textbf{R}esidual \textbf{E}rror estimation), which selects token-wise Taylor orders based on estimated prediction errors for the full-model residual. Yet direct error evaluation requires future ground-truth residuals, which are unavailable during order selection. TORE therefore estimates prediction error by analyzing how errors arise in Taylor extrapolation. Specifically, it decomposes prediction error into truncation error and finite-difference derivative error, then estimates both contributions from cached residuals to preselect token-wise orders for upcoming skip steps. Extensive experiments show that TORE achieves $4.31\times$ speedup on FLUX.1-dev and $4.18\times$ on Wan2.1 while maintaining high generation quality and fidelity.

Abstract: Physically based rendering (PBR) material estimation facilitates relighting, material editing, and 3D content creation. As the reflectance of a surface heavily depends on whether it is a metal or a dielectric, producing any map that determines albedo involves a decision on metalness, which we call the “intended metalness” of the map. Rendering maps with mismatched intended metalness yields physically implausible or misinterpreted materials. The metallic–roughness workflow makes metalness explicit and thus encourages base color to match it, but does not enforce this match, especially for automated methods directly predicting each map. Therefore, we propose ReMeDi, which enforces the match through two components. First, we reparameterize base color as a metalness-weighted blend of its two variants, estimated under the all-metal and all-dielectric hypothesis, respectively. Second, as available supervision cannot teach a single network both hypotheses at every pixel, we restrict each of two separate networks to one hypothesis, so that each variant is ensured to follow its hypothesis. Besides enforcing the match, this also improves each estimated material map. Experiments show that ReMeDi outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both material accuracy and relighting fidelity.

Tianxiang Jiang, Sheng Xia, Yilin Long, Kanghui Tian, Linquan Wu

TL;DR: 论文提出 WebVibe 与 Interactive-SDPO 训练 InteractiveCoder，面向从设计规格生成可交互多页面网站。它通过反向合成（先在浏览器验证跨页/页内交互，再从回放确认状态生成多模态输入）构建 WebVibe-32K 语料，并用基于回放轨迹和状态转移拼图的视觉自蒸馏提供 token 级监督。

Details

Motivation: 现有 MLLM 多将单张截图转为静态 HTML，而从设计规格生成多页路由和有状态组件交互的网站仍困难；前向数据合成会把未实现或改动需求当作监督噪声，强化学习的标量奖励又丢弃回放中的丰富状态转移。

Result: 在 Vision2Web L2 上，one-shot InteractiveCoder-9B 功能分从 20.2 提升到 55.2 FS（近 3 倍），单次生成匹配 1T 参数编码智能体且总参数少 111 倍、token 少 78 倍；在 WebVibeBench 上，4B 和 9B 版本超所有评估基线 23 分以上。

Insight: 创新点是将 X->Y 正向合成反转为经浏览器交互验证的 Y->X 数据构建，并用回放轨迹与 2x2 状态转移拼图条件化权重共享自教师，实现零推理开销的 token 级自蒸馏；客观看，其价值在于用可执行验证减少监督噪声，并把交互状态显式用于训练。

Abstract: Although multimodal large language models translate single screenshots into static HTML, generating interactive functional websites from design specifications still struggles with multi-page routing and stateful widget transitions. Examining existing training pipelines reveals two bottlenecks: (1) in forward data synthesis (X -> Y), writing the specification X before code execution leaves unimplemented or altered requirements in X as supervision noise; and (2) in reinforcement learning, scalar rewards discard the rich state transitions observed during replay. Based on these findings, we introduce WebVibe and Interactive-SDPO to train InteractiveCoder. WebVibe reverses synthesis into Y -> X by verifying cross-page and in-page interactions in the browser before deriving multimodal inputs from replay-confirmed states to build the 31,542-site WebVibe-32K corpus with reusable execution contracts. Interactive-SDPO then conditions a weight-sharing self-teacher on the replay trace and a 2x2 state-transition montage to provide token-level supervision at zero inference-time overhead. On the public Vision2Web L2, one-shot InteractiveCoder-9B nearly 3x its base model’s functional score (20.2 to 55.2 FS) and matches a 1T-parameter coding agent in a single pass with 111x fewer total parameters and 78x fewer tokens. On our WebVibeBench, InteractiveCoder-4B and 9B further outperform all evaluated baselines by over 23 points.

[126] PostMesh: Self-Rewarded Preference Post-Training for Flow-Based Artist Mesh Generation cs.CVPDF

Gihyun Kwon

TL;DR: PostMesh 提出一种面向基于扩散/流模型的艺术家网格生成器的自奖励偏好后训练方法。该方法无需昂贵的人工艺术家网格标注，而是利用生成器自身采样，并通过由稠密输入和输出计算出的艺术家网格奖励进行评分，再以多负样本流偏好优化将奖励迁移到生成器。经过短时间 LoRA 后训练后，模型在多数艺术家网格质量指标和盲测 VLM 偏好测试中可与慢速自回归生成器相当或更优，同时耗时大幅降低。

Details

Motivation: 现有艺术家网格生成多依赖逐 token 解码的自回归模型，生成速度慢；扩散/流模型虽然更快，但生成网格的艺术家质量仍不足。论文旨在提升流式/扩散式艺术家网格生成器的质量，同时避免依赖昂贵的艺术家手工真值。

Result: 经过短时间 LoRA 后训练，PostMesh 在多数艺术家网格质量指标以及盲测 VLM 偏好测试中，与慢速自回归生成器相当或更优，并且生成时间仅为其一小部分。

Insight: 创新点包括：首个面向扩散/流式艺术家网格生成器的后训练方法；无需艺术家真值的自奖励训练框架；结合稠密输入一致性与艺术家网格结构质量的奖励设计；以及多负样本流偏好优化与 LoRA 高效后训练的结合。

Abstract: Artistic mesh generators turn a dense 3D object into an artist mesh: a compact, quad-dominant mesh that is easy to edit. Most of current models are auto-regressive methods which decode a mesh token by token, take tens to hundreds of seconds per mesh. Diffusion- and flow-based generators are much faster, but their meshes still fall short of artist quality. To our knowledge, PostMesh is the first post-training method for a diffusion- or flow-based artist mesh generator, and it needs no expensive artist-made ground truth: the generator is trained on its own samples, scored by a reward computed from the dense input and the output. We propose an artistic mesh reward that keeps the output consistent with the dense input while making the mesh structure into artistic. We also propose a multi-negative flow preference optimisation that transfers the reward to the generator. After a short LoRA post-training, PostMesh is comparable to or better than slow autoregressive generators on most artist-mesh quality metrics and in a blind VLM preference test, at a fraction of their time.

[127] SPARC: Semantic Calibration with Latent Multimodal Knowledge for Robust Image-Text Retrieval cs.CVPDF

Zhongkuan Mao, Wenzhuo Zhao, Haidong Chen, Shangze Li, Yidong Wang

TL;DR: 论文面向图像或文本不完整条件下的鲁棒图文检索，提出SPARC框架，从冻结编码器中提取潜在多模态知识，对不完整查询特征进行语义校准与对齐，并通过匹配关系约束稳定排序。方法包含球面切空间语义校准、鲁棒语义引导和软排序约束三个模块，在Flickr30K和COCO上验证了有效性。

Details

Motivation: 现有鲁棒图文检索方法多侧重适应分布偏移，未充分解决不完整查询下的候选区分能力不足和排序不稳定问题，论文旨在提升不完整图像/文本查询的跨模态匹配准确性与排序鲁棒性。

Result: 在Flickr30K和COCO数据集上，SPARC在多种图像遮挡和词删除设置下优于竞争方法，并在不同预训练骨干上取得一致增益。

Insight: 创新点在于利用冻结编码器的潜在多模态知识，在单位球面切空间校准不完整特征以保持余弦相似度几何，并结合跨模态语义锚点与软排序约束限制排序扰动；客观看，其将特征校准、语义引导和排序稳定性联合建模，为鲁棒检索提供了可借鉴的约束式对齐思路。

Abstract: Robust image-text retrieval aims to maintain accurate cross-modal matching when image or text queries are incomplete, supporting reliable information access in real-world applications. However, existing methods typically focus on adapting to distribution shifts, without adequately addressing candidate discrimination for incomplete queries and ranking instability. To this end, we propose semantic projection and alignment with relational constraints (SPARC), which elicits latent multimodal knowledge from frozen encoders to improve candidate discrimination and ranking stability. Specifically, we first devise a semantic calibration module on a product of spheres that refines incomplete visual and textual features through tangent-space updates, maintaining the unit-sphere structure used for cosine similarity. Second, we introduce a robust semantic guidance module that combines latent multimodal knowledge encoded in complete image-text features with cross-modal semantic anchors to promote semantic alignment of the calibrated features. Finally, we introduce a matching relation constraint module that fuses original query features with their calibrated counterparts and applies a soft ranking constraint to penalize reductions in initially positive matching margins, thereby limiting ranking perturbations. Extensive experiments on Flickr30K and COCO demonstrate that SPARC outperforms competing methods under various image occlusion and word deletion settings, while achieving consistent gains across different pretrained backbones.

[128] AIRIS: Benchmarking Social Embodied Intelligence in Egocentric Streaming Videos cs.CVPDF

Yuchen Zhou, Hongsheng Wu, Jiayu Tang, Yunxuan Mao, Hanjing Ye

TL;DR: 论文提出 AIRIS，一个面向自我中心流视频的社会具身智能因果流式基准，将社交具身智能建模为时间锚定的 Attention-Intention-Reaction-Interaction（A-I-R-I）链。基准包含来自 357 个自我中心视频片段的 8000 个专家标注问题，覆盖 11 类上下文和 36 个能力维度；模型在每个查询时只能看到当时可用的观察前缀。论文评估了 45 个专有与开源模型，揭示当前模型在社会线索理解、意图判断、干预时机和具体反应执行上存在碎片化和时间校准不足的问题。

Details

Motivation: 现有基准通常在孤立能力上评估，或允许模型访问完整视频，难以揭示在线社交决策中“证据是否充分、是否应干预、如何合规响应”的失败点。论文希望弥合被动社会感知与可靠具身交互之间的差距。

Result: AIRIS 包含 8000 个专家问题、357 个自我中心片段、11 类上下文和 36 个能力维度，并采用决策感知指标奖励校准等待与完整反应、惩罚过早承诺。评估 45 个模型发现能力碎片化且时间失准：通用 VLM 擅长被动线索 grounding 和意图识别，具身专家在孤立反应子决策上领先，但所有模型常证据不足时猜测意图，且难以在同一 episode 中联合对齐干预时机、动作域和具体执行。

Insight: 创新点在于将社交具身智能形式化为因果流式 A-I-R-I 时间链，并用仅可见观察前缀和决策感知指标诊断在线社交决策。其可借鉴之处是显式评估“等待/干预时机”和“社会规范约束下的完整反应”，而不仅是静态感知或孤立意图分类。

Abstract: Embodied agents operating around people must not only recognize social cues, but also decide when the available evidence is sufficient, whether to intervene, and how to respond without violating social norms. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate these abilities in isolation or with access to completed videos, obscuring where an online social decision fails. We introduce AIRIS, a causal streaming benchmark that formulates social embodied intelligence as a time-anchored chain of Attention, Intention, Reaction, and Interaction (A-I-R-I). AIRIS contains 8,000 expert-curated questions grounded in 357 egocentric clips across 11 context categories and 36 capability dimensions. At each query, models receive only the observation prefix available at that time; decision-aware metrics reward calibrated waiting and complete reactions while penalizing premature commitment. Evaluating 45 proprietary and open-source general-purpose, embodied, streaming-video, and omni-modal models reveals fragmented and temporally miscalibrated competence. General-purpose VLMs excel at passive cue grounding and intent recognition, whereas embodied specialists lead on isolated reaction sub-decisions; yet across all families, models frequently guess intents before sufficient evidence emerges and fail to jointly align intervention timing, action domain, and concrete execution within the same episode. AIRIS thus provides a diagnostic foundation for bridging the gap between passive social perception and reliable embodied interaction.

[129] One Patch, Many Policies: Object-Associated Physical Patch Attacks on Robotic Manipulation cs.CVPDF

Yiqian Liu, Yuqi Lin, Hui Lu, Jiaming He, Qi Zhu

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向机器人操作的物体关联物理补丁攻击框架OAPA，针对VLA和WAMs等策略模型，通过一次优化得到的通用可迁移补丁，并利用补丁放置位置选择要干扰的任务物体，而无需重新优化补丁。方法结合物体关联放置、考虑视角透视与遮挡的可微物理合成，以及基于动作专家表示散度的补丁优化。

Details

Motivation: VLA和WAMs在机器人操作中容易受到对抗视觉干扰，论文旨在研究通用且可迁移的物理补丁攻击，并进一步实现对攻击者选定任务物体操作的选择性破坏。

Result: 实验表明该方法在不同任务套件和策略之间具有较强攻击迁移性，并能通过补丁放置选择性干扰攻击者指定的任务物体；摘要未给出具体benchmark数值或SOTA比较。

Insight: 创新点在于将物体关联放置与物理补丁优化结合，使单个补丁可跨策略、跨任务迁移，并通过放置位置实现“一个补丁、多种策略/目标”的选择性攻击；同时引入透视与遮挡建模提升物理可实现性，并以动作专家表示散度作为优化信号。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and World Action Models (WAMs) are vulnerable to adversarial visual interference, motivating the study of universal and transferable physical patch attacks. We propose Object-Associated Patch Attack (OAPA), a universal and transferable physical patch framework that enables an attacker to select which task object’s manipulation to disrupt through patch placement, without re-optimizing the patch. Our framework combines object-associated placement, differentiable physical compositing, and final action-expert representation divergence. Physical compositing accounts for perspective and occlusion across camera views, while representation divergence guides patch optimization using either relative RMS or cosine distance. Experimental results demonstrate strong attack transferability across task suites and policies, together with the ability to selectively disrupt manipulation of an attacker-chosen task object through patch placement.

[130] MoCA4D: Monocular 3D Bounding Box Lifting and Tracking with Camera and Object Motion cs.CVPDF

Changwoo Jeon, Jongyoo Kim

TL;DR: 论文提出 MoCA4D，一个从单目视频、关联2D框提示和相机内参中进行3D框提升与跟踪的框架。它通过观测分支估计逐帧几何，并用动态分支显式建模跨对象共享的相机变换和每个对象的独立刚性变换；再用 Star Optimization 结合当前观测与经变换传播的历史框来细化深度和朝向，并指导后续传播。

Details

Motivation: 单目3D框跟踪需同时处理物体运动和相机视角变化；现有逐帧提升加时序滤波将运动隐式化，静态场景融合则假设物体静止，难以准确跟踪动态目标。

Result: 在 Argoverse 2 和 ADT 上，Star Optimization 相对同观测的 Kalman 滤波将中心运动误差分别降低38.1%和38.7%；在 MOVi-F 的八次转移动窗上，动态分支取得最低角点/旋转误差和最高IoU。真实数据上的仅相机控制实验进一步表明预测物体运动有助于动态目标轨迹定位。

Insight: 创新在于将相机运动与物体刚性运动解耦并显式建模，且用 Star Optimization 迭代融合观测与历史传播以细化3D框；客观看，其价值在于为单目动态场景3D跟踪提供可分解运动先验和传播-细化闭环。

Abstract: Monocular 3D bounding box tracking must account for both object motion and changes in camera viewpoint. Framewise box lifting with temporal filtering leaves these motion components implicit, while static-scene fusion assumes stationary objects. We introduce MoCA4D, a framework for 3D box lifting and tracking from monocular video, associated 2D box prompts, and camera intrinsics. An observation branch estimates per-frame geometry, while a dynamic branch predicts a camera transformation shared across objects and a separate rigid transformation for each object. We propose Star Optimization to refine current depth and orientation using the observation and historical boxes propagated through these transformations. The refined box then guides subsequent propagation. Across all evaluated objects in Argoverse 2 and ADT, Star Optimization reduces center motion error by 38.1% and 38.7%, respectively, relative to Kalman filtering of the same observations. On MOVi-F, the dynamic branch achieves the lowest corner and rotation errors and the highest IoU among the evaluated methods over eight-transition motion windows. Real-data camera-only controls further link predicted object motion to improved localization on dynamic-object tracks.

[131] Plausible Is Not Proven: Testing Failure Explanations in Monocular Depth Estimation cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Zeeshan Ali, Md Rayyan, Anshuman Chakravorty, Omer Faraz, Anshi Bajaj

TL;DR: 论文提出 Vision Error Observatory（VEO）审计流程，用于检验单目深度估计模型失败区域的“原因标签”是否可信：先找出在传感器像素上不满足 δ1 准则的区域，为其标注可能失败原因或弃权，再用对象标注一致性、盲评人工评分、定向图像编辑和训练信号四项测试验证。作者在 NYU Depth V2、DIODE、iBims-1 上评估 8 个模型，并在 Hypersim 上检查标签，结果显示许多看似合理的失败解释经不起检验。

Details

Motivation: 现有单目深度模型的失败分析常直接给错误区域贴上遮挡边界、薄物体、反射面等原因标签，但这些标签很少被验证，可能只是看似合理而非被证明。论文关注如何系统检验这些失败解释是否真实有效，避免盲信事后归因。

Result: 遮挡边界标签与标注有一定一致性（NYU AUROC 0.85、Hypersim 0.81、iBims-1 0.69），但单一深度边缘线索表现相当，且最强模型多数边界失败在 2 像素容差下消失；反射标签无法迁移到合成数据，无纹理标签接近随机，四项编辑测试仅一项探索性测试支持其标签。与五名盲评者共识相比，VEO 标签仅达随机水平（Cohen’s κ=0.05），且在微调中提高诊断区域权重反而使目标像素误差上升。

Insight: 创新点在于将失败原因解释视为可证伪假设，设计多维度审计协议并公开协议、代码、评分和结果文件；客观来看，其价值更在于揭示常见失败归因标签的脆弱性和不可迁移性，提醒研究者在信任失败解释前必须进行严格验证。

Abstract: Failure analyses of monocular depth models often name a cause for each error, such as an occluding boundary, a thin object or a reflective surface, but such labels are rarely tested. We present the Vision Error Observatory (VEO), an audit that finds regions failing the $δ_1$ criterion on sensor-measured pixels, labels each with a likely cause or abstains, and then subjects the labels to four tests: agreement with object annotations on data the label model never saw, agreement with blinded human raters, response to targeted image edits, and value as a training signal. We apply it to eight models on NYU Depth V2, DIODE and iBims-1, with labels also checked on Hypersim, and fix most decision rules before their results. The occluding-boundary label agrees with annotations (AUROC 0.85 on NYU, 0.81 on Hypersim, 0.69 on iBims-1), but a single depth-edge cue does as well, and most boundary failures of the strongest model vanish under a 2-pixel tolerance. The reflection label does not transfer to synthetic data, the textureless label is near chance against annotations, and only one of four edit tests, an exploratory one, supports its label. Evaluated against five blinded raters, VEO’s labels matched the human consensus only at chance level (Cohen’s $κ$ = 0.05). In addition, an increase in the weight on the diagnosed regions during fine-tuning led to an increase in the error on targeted pixels at every weight tested. The architecture-specific failure fingerprint claimed by an earlier version of this project also failed to replicate across the datasets. Plausible failure explanations, in short, need to be tested before they are trusted; we release the protocol, code, ratings and every result file.

[132] SPGSR: A Small Pixel-Space Generator for One-Step Super-Resolution cs.CVPDF

Yun Yue, Luming Liang, Yiqi Zhong

TL;DR: 论文提出 SPGSR，一种用于一步超分辨率的小型像素空间生成器。它将低分辨率图像与学习到的 RGB 校正相加，并利用冻结的 DINOv2 提供语义引导，再用轻量模块对校正进行局部细化。该方法在仅 31.8M 参数下取得优于 VOSR 的保真度与感知指标，并显著提升推理速度。

Details

Motivation: 现有一步超分辨率视觉模型虽能直接从低分辨率图像提取空间对齐的语义线索，但生成器参数量可高达 1.4B。论文质疑低分辨率图像已提供大量结构信息，能否用更小的生成器达到可比质量。

Result: 在三个基准上，SPGSR 的 PSNR/SSIM 高于 VOSR，LPIPS/DISTS 低于 VOSR，并与基于 T2I 的超分方法具有竞争力。其生成器仅 31.8M 参数，为 VOSR-0.5B 的 6.5%；训练和蒸馏所用 HR 像素仅为 VOSR 的 0.60%；512×512 输出下相较 VOSR-0.5B/1.4B 分别实现 2.80×/3.35× GPU 流水线加速。

Insight: 创新点在于用学习到的 RGB 残差校正低分辨率图像，而非依赖超大生成器重建全部内容；冻结 DINOv2 提供语义引导，轻量模块仅局部细化校正。客观上看，该设计将强语义先验与像素空间残差生成结合，在保持一步超分和空间对齐的同时显著降低参数与计算成本。

Abstract: In one-step super-resolution (SR), vision-only models extract semantic cues directly from the low-resolution (LR) image, keeping those cues spatially aligned with the structures they help restore. Recent models achieve high restoration quality with generators of up to 1.4 billion parameters. Given how much structure the LR image already provides, could a much smaller generator achieve comparable quality? We introduce SPGSR, a vision-only framework that adds a learned RGB correction to the LR image. A frozen DINOv2 guides prediction, and a lightweight block refines the correction locally. SPGSR achieves higher PSNR/SSIM and lower LPIPS/DISTS than VOSR, a leading vision-only SR method, across three benchmarks. Its $31.8\mathrm{M}$-parameter generator is just $6.5%$ the size of VOSR-0.5B’s. SPGSR also achieves competitive quality against T2I-based SR methods. SPGSR’s generator uses only $0.60%$ of the HR pixels used to train and distill VOSR’s generator. For $512\times512$ outputs, SPGSR achieves $2.80\times$ and $3.35\times$ GPU-pipeline speedups over VOSR-0.5B and VOSR-1.4B, respectively.

[133] DocSEAM: Bidirectional Cross-Page Encoding for Multi-Page Document Understanding cs.CV | cs.CLPDF

Hoang-Nhat Nguyen, Chen Change Loy

TL;DR: 论文提出 DocSEAM，一个插入在预训练视觉编码器与语言模型之间的轻量模块，用于多页文档理解。它通过将每页与相邻两页组成局部三元组，并用中心锚定的掩码自注意力与方向显著性门融合跨页信息，只返回增强后的中心页 token，从而在不增加视觉 token 数的前提下缓解逐页独立编码导致的页边界特征不连续。

Details

Motivation: 现有多页文档视觉语言模型通常独立编码每一页，视觉 token 在生成时无法访问相邻页内容，语言模型也只能基于孤立页表示推理，导致页边界处特征不连续并影响跨页理解。

Result: 在五个多页文档 VQA 基准上，9B 参数模型在评估的 10B 以内开放权重模型中达到 SOTA；受控重新分页实验显示 DocSEAM 降低了对页边界位置的敏感性，并提升了跨页跨度定位能力。

Insight: 创新点在于以轻量、即插即用的方式在视觉编码器与语言模型之间注入局部跨页上下文，通过邻页三元组、中心锚定掩码注意力和方向显著性门实现选择性信息融合，同时保持中心页 token 数量不变，便于集成且不增加视觉 token 开销。

Abstract: Multi-page document vision-language models encode each page independently, creating a feature-level discontinuity at page boundaries: visual tokens are formed without access to content on adjacent pages, and the language model must reason over these page representations formed in isolation. We introduce DocSEAM (Document Selective Enrichment via Anchored Masking), a lightweight module inserted between a pretrained vision encoder and the language model that injects the inductive bias of local cross-page context into visual representations. For each page, DocSEAM forms a local triplet with its two neighbors, applies center-anchored masked self-attention with directional salience gates to fuse information from neighbor pages, and returns only the enriched center-page tokens, leaving the visual token count unchanged. After a supervised cross-page warm-up and multi-page fine-tuning with DocSEAM frozen, our 9B-parameter model achieves state-of-the-art results among the evaluated open-weight models of up to 10B parameters on five multi-page document VQA benchmarks. We further show that under controlled re-pagination, DocSEAM reduces sensitivity to boundary placement and improves cross-page span localization

Felix Tristram, Benjamin D. Killeen, Nassir Navab

Details

Motivation: 机器人操作成功不仅依赖可观测轨迹，还依赖隐藏的交互力；人类具有对该隐藏变量的视觉先验，因此论文追问视觉基础模型的表征是否也编码交互力，以揭示当前模型在力感知上的能力缺口。

Result: 在两个操作数据集上的探测实验显示，大多数图像/视频/世界/策略模型能捕捉接触何时发生，但无法很好跟踪或估计施加力的大小；令人意外的是，机器人后训练并未提升该能力，说明现有模型在力大小感知上仍存在明显不足。

Insight: 创新点在于将“力感知”操作化为接触发生、力变化跟踪和力大小反映三个可探测维度，并系统评测冻结的视觉基础模型表征；其客观价值是揭示了模型对接触时序与力幅度的解耦，以及后训练难以弥补该表征缺陷。

Abstract: World models and vision-language-action policy models are increasingly proposed as foundations for robot manipulation. They predict how scenes evolve and which actions to take, yet successful manipulation depends not solely on following observable trajectories but also on the hidden variable of interaction force. Neurophysiology has shown that humans possess a visual prior for this hidden variable, motivating us to ask the question: Do the representations of visual foundation models also capture the forces involved in an interaction? We define force awareness as the ability of representations to (1) indicate when contact force is applied, (2) track how it changes during an interaction, and (3) reflect how large it is. We probe frozen image, video, world- and policy model representations with a lightweight readout trained on synchronized video and force measurements from two manipulation datasets. Our experiments show that most models capture when contact occurs but not how much force is being applied. Surprisingly, robot post-training does not improve this either, highlighting an important gap in current models capabilities.

Abstract: Modern video generators excel at producing visually plausible futures guided by text and image prompts. Yet their emphasis on semantic plausibility does not guarantee fidelity to the physical consequences of specific actions. Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs), in contrast, aim to simulate how the world responds to robot actions, enabling scalable policy evaluation and improvement without costly real-world rollouts. The challenge is not merely to generate a plausible future, but to predict what follows from a specific physical action. As video priors grow stronger, we ask how little adaptation is truly needed to bridge this gap. We introduce Dianjing, a minimal recipe that preserves and unlocks the capabilities of strong video priors through unified physical action conditioning, training concentrated at the high noise levels where motion and layout, and thus the consequences of actions, are decided, and native history conditioning. Built upon MiniMax H3, a 33B video foundation model, Dianjing freezes the pretrained backbone and trains only 0.34% of its parameters using approximately 65 hours of robot video across two embodiments, without additional robot-specific pretraining or camera calibration. Controlled ablations show that where training is allocated across noise levels matters more than how actions enter the network. On WorldOracle, which evaluates predictions against paired real-world executions, Dianjing achieves the best scores on four of the five evaluation tracks and ranks second on AgiBot-Long. Our findings suggest that strong video priors already bring us most of the way to world modeling; lightweight physical action grounding can unlock their potential for real-world prediction.

Abstract: Instruction-guided image editing has a wide range of applications, yet existing models may inadvertently alter content that should remain unchanged. Reliable consistency evaluation is therefore essential for assessing content preservation and providing reward signals for Reinforcement Learning (RL). However, existing reward models may overlook subtle visual changes or fail to distinguish necessary modifications from unintended alterations, limiting the reliability of consistency assessment. To address this challenge, we propose PreserveReward, a consistency reward model for image editing that integrates visual On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) to enhance fine-grained visual perception and consistency assessment. PreserveReward learns from reference preservation scores through a regression-based scoring loss and employs two complementary teacher views for self-distillation. The local teacher uses matched crops to identify subtle visual changes, while the joint teacher combines local details with global context to determine whether these changes align with the editing intent. To improve the reliability of distillation supervision, we calibrate teacher score distributions via minimum-KL projection under reference-score error constraints and adaptively select teacher process supervision based on gradient alignment with the scoring objective, mitigating scoring bias and optimization conflicts. Abstract: Precise 9-DoF pose control is essential for visual content generation, enabling users to specify object location, size, and orientation in 3D space. However, existing methods typically focus on either text-to-image (T2I) or text-to-video (T2V) generation, with limitations in pose controllability and temporal coherence. We present SceneDesigner++, a unified framework for 9-DoF pose control across both T2I and T2V generation. We construct ObjectPose9D++, a dataset containing high-quality images and videos with accurate pose annotations. Trained on ObjectPose9D++, SceneDesigner++ combines absolute and relative pose encoding strategies to decouple frame-wise spatial grounding from inter-frame motion modeling. Specifically, absolute pose encoding leverages CNOCS map sequences to provide explicit, frame-wise 9-DoF pose guidance. Meanwhile, our relative pose encoding, termed VPRoPE, incorporates relative pose information into rotary position embeddings to explicitly model inter-frame pose transitions, thereby promoting temporally coherent object motion. A learnable orthogonal transformation is further introduced for each attention head, allowing different heads to capture complementary geometric relationships while preserving input feature norms to promote training stability. We further adopt a two-stage training strategy to learn the absolute and relative pose encoding mechanisms separately. Benefiting from this lightweight and decoupled design, SceneDesigner++ also supports integration with an independently trained camera-control module, enabling separate or joint control over object and camera motion. Finally, we introduce T2IVPoseEval, a benchmark for evaluating diverse object poses and motion patterns in both T2I and T2V tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SceneDesigner++ consistently outperforms same-backbone baselines in pose fidelity and motion quality.

Details

Motivation: 现有医学视频语言模型和评测可能让模型忽略视频、依赖位置或常数答案；视频编码器会把相邻帧合并成共享 token；微调模型还会记忆训练问题，导致视觉 grounding 不足。

Abstract: A medical video language model should answer from the visual evidence its question asks about. We find three places where this breaks down and address each with an approach we call MedEvidU. First, benchmarks reward answers that ignore the video: on one dataset, the position of a clip within its recording predicts the surgical step in 37% of questions, and a constant answer beats every trained model on a standard safety-assessment metric. We build MedVidBench-v2, 34 tasks from 18 surgical and clinical video datasets, in which prompts and metrics are audited with answers given without video, and which tests unseen hospitals and windows cut blindly from whole recordings. Second, common video encoders merge consecutive frames into shared tokens, so a question about one frame is answered from a blend of two. Query-slot inputs give each queried frame its own tokens at almost no extra cost. Third, a fine-tuned model has memorized its training questions, which leaves little to learn from. Label-matched question resampling (LQR) adds unseen questions about the same recordings while keeping the answer distribution fixed, in a final supervised epoch and in reinforcement learning. Together, these raise the grounding IoU of a 4B model from 30.4 to 44.4, above a supervised 27B model, and instrument grounding at an unseen hospital from 33.9 to 72.5. On blindly cut windows, event-presence accuracy improves by 5.5 points and localization F1 by 8.1 points. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/MedEvidU/

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向硬标签大视觉语言模型（LVLM）的黑盒对抗补丁攻击方法 HardPatch。该方法仅通过查询模型，将图像划分为均匀图像块并用掩码逐一评估其敏感性，再按敏感度排序选择最脆弱图像块，结合随机噪声估计梯度并迭代优化多个补丁，直到生成满足对抗条件的图像。

Details

Motivation: 现有攻击方法通常依赖LVLM反向传播梯度，要求获取模型梯度或细节，难以适用于真实场景中不提供梯度和模型信息的硬标签LVLM应用。论文旨在研究硬标签黑盒设定下如何利用局部视觉区域脆弱性生成有效对抗补丁。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量指标或benchmark数值，仅说明在多种LVLM模型和数据集上进行了评估，实验表明HardPatch通过合适的补丁替换与优化能够构造有效对抗图像并攻击硬标签LVLM。

Insight: 创新点在于面向硬标签LVLM的模型敏感对抗补丁设计：先通过分块掩码查询评估局部敏感性，再按敏感度选择脆弱补丁，并利用加性随机噪声估计梯度进行优化。客观上看，其可借鉴之处是仅依赖查询即可定位视觉局部脆弱区域，并在无梯度条件下完成补丁式黑盒攻击。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in handling multi-modal downstream tasks, gaining increasing popularity. However, recent studies show that LVLMs are susceptible to both intentional and inadvertent attacks. Existing attackers ideally optimize adversarial perturbations with backpropagated gradients from LVLMs, thus limiting their scalability in practical scenarios as real-world LVLM applications will not provide any LVLM’s gradient or details. Motivated by this research gap and counter-practical phenomenon, we propose a novel hard-label attack method for LVLMs, named HardPatch, to generate visual adversarial patches by solely querying the model. Our method provides deeper insights into how to investigate the vulnerability of LVLMs in local visual regions and generate corresponding adversarial substitution under the practical yet challenging hard-label setting. Specifically, we first split each image into uniform patches and mask each of them to individually assess their sensitivity to the LVLM model. Then, according to the descending order of sensitive scores, we iteratively select the most vulnerable patch to initialize noise and estimate gradients with further additive random noises for optimization. In this manner, multiple patches are perturbed until the altered image satisfies the adversarial condition. Extensive LVLM models and datasets are evaluated to demonstrate the adversarial nature of HardPatch. Our empirical observations suggest that with appropriate patch substitution and optimization, HardPatch can craft effective adversarial images to attack hard-label LVLMs.

Details

Motivation: 传统线性探针需要大量标注数据，且只能恢复视觉表示中“可读出”的信息，未必反映模型实际使用的信息。论文希望更直接地找到 LVLM 视觉表示中的可解释方向，并支持对模型感知的精准干预。

Abstract: It is commonly accepted that high-level concepts are represented linearly in the representation space of large neural networks. For Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs), finding those interpretable directions in visual representations is important for interpretability. To find them, the classical approach is to train a linear classifier on the representations, but it needs a large amount of labels and only recovers what can be read from the representations, not what the model actually uses. In this paper we start from the following insight: The LLM Decoder of an LVLM is also a classifier whose classes are the possible next tokens. So we use its first-order Taylor expansion as a linear classifier. Hence, we introduce Decoder-as-a-Probe (DaP), which significantly outperforms classical probing and steering baselines especially when training data is sparse. (1) It verbalizes what an LVLM perceives in a given image. (2) It can surgically change the perception of the LVLM about specific attributes (e.g. change only the color, the clothes worn or the action done). Due to its simplicity and data efficiency, we believe DaP can serve as a plug-and-play tool for LVLM research, such as interpretability, hallucination mitigation or instruction tuning.

[142] PURA: Video Token Pruning via Unified Spatiotemporal Redundancy Assessment cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Yuyao Sun, Hongli Yuan, Shuang Li, Tao Deng, Deqing Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练的视频视觉token剪枝方法PURA，通过统一的时空冗余评估为每个token确定移除成本，并利用协调剪枝批量删除低成本token，以降低视频大语言模型的推理开销。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频token压缩方法通常预设空间与时间压缩的处理顺序或固定分配比例，难以适应不同视频中时空冗余平衡的变化。论文旨在更灵活地评估和利用时空冗余，降低视频LLM的长视觉token推理成本。

Result: 在三个视频大语言模型和五个视频理解基准上的实验表明，PURA在任务性能与推理效率之间取得了较好的权衡；摘要未给出具体数值或SOTA声明。

Insight: 创新点在于对每个token构建空间与时间参考候选，选择跨两维度表示误差最低者，并结合token重要性确定移除成本；同时提出Coordinated Pruning，将相互兼容的低成本移除分批执行并迭代更新剪枝状态，避免逐个删除token的时间开销。

Abstract: Video large language models incur substantial inference costs from long visual token sequences. Existing video token compression methods mitigate these costs by exploiting spatial redundancy within frames and temporal redundancy across frames. However, many approaches impose predefined processing orders or fixed allocation ratios for spatial and temporal compression, limiting their ability to adapt to the varying balance of spatiotemporal redundancy across videos. To address this limitation, we propose PURA, a training-free method that prunes visual tokens through unified spatiotemporal redundancy assessment. For each token, PURA constructs spatial and temporal reference candidates and selects the reference with the lowest representation error across the two dimensions. The representation error is combined with token importance to determine each token’s removal cost. To avoid the time overhead of removing the lowest-cost token one by one, we introduce Coordinated Pruning, which iteratively groups mutually compatible low-cost removals into batches for simultaneous execution and updates the pruning state after each batch until the target budget is reached. Extensive experiments on three video large language models and five video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that PURA achieves a favorable trade-off between task performance and inference efficiency.

[143] HAS: Hierarchical Adaptive Frame Sampling for Video Understanding cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Yuyao Sun, Daiqi Shi, Shuang Li, Liao Zhang, Yang Cai

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于视频理解的分层自适应帧采样框架 HAS。该方法先用 DPP 选出与查询相关且不冗余的关键帧作为骨干帧，再根据骨干帧的时间分布推断查询需求，并在局部上下文与全局上下文之间自适应分配剩余帧预算。将 HAS 接入多个主流多模态大模型后，在多个长视频理解基准上取得一致更优表现。

Details

Motivation: 多模态大模型受上下文长度限制，无法处理完整长视频，只能采样部分帧作为输入；现有均匀采样或相关性采样通常由单一原则驱动，难以同时满足不同查询对全局时序理解和短时细粒度事件证据的异构需求。

Result: 将 HAS 集成到三个领先 MLLM 中，在 Video-MME、LongVideoBench 和 MLVU 三个视频理解基准上均表现出一致更优的性能，说明其采样策略优于现有方法。

Insight: 创新点在于两阶段分层采样：用 DPP 构建查询相关且非冗余的骨干帧集合，再根据骨干帧时间分布自适应分配局部与全局上下文预算。客观上看，其可借鉴之处是将帧选择从单一相关性扩展为“关键帧骨架+查询感知上下文补全”，兼顾全局理解与细粒度时序证据。

Abstract: Due to context-length constraints, most MLLMs cannot process full-length videos and therefore rely on sampling a subset of frames as input. However, existing sampling methods, ranging from uniform sampling to relevance-based selection, are often driven by a single sampling principle and thus struggle to accommodate the heterogeneous evidential requirements of different queries: some demand a holistic understanding of how the video unfolds over time, while others hinge on fine-grained events within a short temporal window. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{Hierarchical Adaptive Frame Sampling (HAS)}, a two-stage frame sampling framework. In the first stage, \emph{Backbone Frame Construction}, we apply a Determinantal Point Process (DPP) to sample frames that are both query-relevant and non-redundant. These selected frames capture key moments across the video and form the backbone of the sampling set, providing a foundation for subsequent enrichment. In the second stage, \emph{Adaptive Contextual Enrichment}, we analyze the temporal distribution of the backbone frames to infer the query’s requirements and adaptively allocate the remaining frame budget between Local and Global Context. The Local Context enriches the backbone with fine-grained temporal dynamics and short-range causal relations, whereas the Global Context connects temporally isolated evidence and provides a holistic view of the entire video to support global understanding. Through such a hierarchical two-stage process, HAS effectively addresses diverse query requirements. Incorporated into three leading MLLMs, it demonstrates consistently superior performance across the Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU benchmarks.

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in video understanding, yet existing video question answering (VQA) benchmarks mainly evaluate models in offline settings. Real-world streaming scenarios instead require models to answer context-dependent queries by retrieving and reasoning over evidence distributed across the video history. We introduce StreamRecall, a benchmark for context-aware Multi evidence reasoning in streaming video QA. StreamRecall requires models to retrieve, associate, and reason over temporally scattered evidence to answer queries at the appropriate moment. We further propose the Evidence Utilization Cascade to diagnose model reasoning through evidence localization, grounding, chain completion, and answer derivation. Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection (IAD), the task of identifying and localizing deviations from normal product conditions, is essential to automated quality inspection in manufacturing. When such checks are insufficient, models may miss subtle defects or misidentify the defect type. To address this limitation, we introduce ADClaw, an inspection agent that uses executable code to test anomaly hypotheses without task-specific fine-tuning. Abstract: The severe susceptibility of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to hallucinations poses a critical threat to their reliability, making a rigorous evaluation framework indispensable. Abstract: As agents based on vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly interpret social-media content, errors in their analysis may lend credibility to rumors and amplify their spread. In this paper, we study the problem of visual cognitive collusion, in which coordinated posts combine authentic images and locally faithful captions to induce VLM agents to infer and accept attacker-chosen target claims through their own reasoning. To enable systematic evaluation of this cognitive threat, we introduce a new benchmark comprising 544 target cases and 33.2K candidate images across eight application domains. We further propose VisualMontage, a multi-agent framework for simulating such attacks through narrative planning, image selection and ordering, and caption assignment under iterative review. We evaluate this threat through extensive experiments across 12 vision-language models, examining modality effects, production-team components, and downstream defenses. Using identical attack feeds across models without victim-specific optimization, we demonstrate cross-model transferability, achieving mean attack success rates of 61.93% for proprietary models and 68.71% for open-weight models. Moreover, 92.65% of generated feeds pass independent model-based validity auditing. Even after downstream review, residual attack success rates range from 35.3% to 76.7% across the evaluated configurations. Our additional controlled comparisons further reveal that captions can improve model-rated posting naturalness while reducing attack success. Together, these findings reveal a novel cognitive threat in social-media interpretation and motivate safeguards that assess how authentic posts jointly shape agents’ reasoning and conclusions.

Motivation: 现有 VLA 驾驶模型中，语言决策要么是独立输出，要么只是用于从相似候选轨迹中挑选一个的索引，而且其标注并未与轨迹相互校验，导致闭环驾驶时语言决策与轨迹经常不一致，可解释性名不副实。论文旨在从机制上强制语言决策与轨迹规划保持一致。

Result: 在 Bench2Drive 基准上，轻量模型取得 Driving Score 78.50、Success Rate 55.91%，与当前 SOTA 相比具有竞争力，同时大幅降低了语言-动作不一致性；推理可在一张消费级显卡上在线闭环运行。

Insight: 创新点在于：把语言决策重构为可由确定性规则从任意轨迹读出、并基于感知事实的“承诺”（机动类别+连续参数）；用 Maneuver Bank 将其映射为运动学代码；结合一致性损失与反事实执行联合训练决策与轨迹；用规则函数配合 VLM 自动生成、审查并精炼语言标注。由于代码是查表且不依赖语言模型隐藏状态，该框架可无需重训地扩展到新的规则化决策，这是值得借鉴的解耦设计思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models introduce language reasoning into end-to-end autonomous driving. They output a language decision together with a trajectory, which makes the planner more interpretable. However, in existing methods the language decision is either an independent output or an index that selects among similar candidate trajectories, and its annotations are not verified against the trajectory. As a result, the language decision and the trajectory are often inconsistent in closed-loop driving. To address this, we propose Commit2Drive, a framework that imposes the language decision as a consistency constraint on trajectory planning. The decision is a commitment, a maneuver class with a continuous parameter that one deterministic rule reads from any trajectory, and it is grounded in facts reported by the perception of the model itself. A Maneuver Bank maps the commitment to a code with a kinematic meaning, and consistency losses train the planner to execute every admissible decision of a scene, so that the trajectory follows the decision. An automatic data pipeline supplies the supervision. Decisions are labeled from the future motion of the expert, counterfactual executions train the decision and the trajectory together, and rule-based functions with a Vision-Language Model (VLM) generate, review and refine the language annotations. The code is a lookup that involves no hidden state of the language model, so Commit2Drive is extensible to rule-based decisions, which the same planner executes without retraining. Commit2Drive uses a 0.5B language model and runs closed-loop inference online on a single consumer graphics card. On Bench2Drive, Commit2Drive reaches a Driving Score (DS) of 78.50 and a Success Rate (SR) of 55.91% with a lightweight model, which is competitive with the state of the art, and greatly reduces the language-action inconsistency. Code will be released soon.

Details

Motivation: 相机控制视频生成依赖大规模数据隐式学习场景先验，而超声中追踪扫描稀缺，难以隐式学习；同时解剖结构是声谱师依赖的显式先验。仅用探头位姿控制会导致未扫描区域解剖不合理、重访时解剖不一致，因此需要显式解剖先验与3D记忆。

Result: 在甲状腺、肾脏和腰椎三个部位上，方法相较位姿条件生成和记忆增强基线，在视觉保真度、位姿跟随和解剖一致性方面均有提升。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video generation anticipates how a scene appears from new viewpoints by relying on a prior learned implicitly from large-scale data. Ultrasound poses the same problem, with the probe pose as the viewpoint, but under opposite conditions: anatomy offers an explicit prior that every sonographer relies on, whereas tracked scans are too scarce to learn it implicitly. As a result, pose control alone is not enough, and pose-conditioned ultrasound generators produce implausible anatomy in unscanned regions and inconsistent anatomy on revisits. We go beyond pose control and give the generator the two kinds of knowledge that make up a sonographer’s mental model. An anatomical prior, built from a population of subjects, guides generation where nothing has been scanned, and is refined online by every generated chunk towards the scanned subject. A 3D latent memory, designed around the cross-sectional physics of ultrasound, compounds the generated slices into a volume of the patient and reslices it at new poses, keeping revisited anatomy consistent. The fine-grained memory enters the generator at the input, while the coarse anatomy prior guides the layout across transformer blocks. Experiments on thyroid, kidney, and lumbar spine show improved visual fidelity, pose following, and anatomical consistency over pose-conditioned and memory-augmented baselines.

Karl Li

TL;DR: 论文研究不同随机种子训练出的稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征是否在功能上等价：先按方向匹配特征，再分别消融一个特征及其匹配项，比较视觉编码器在留出图像上的输出变化；若变化几乎一致，则称消融可跨种子迁移。作者在 DINOv2/SigLIP 的多个层与特征数设置下评估，并分析用层贡献而非方向来预测可迁移性的效果。

Details

Motivation: 已有工作仅按方向相似性一对一对齐不同种子的 SAE 特征，但方向相似不代表响应相同图像区域或消融效果一致；论文要检验匹配特征是否真正功能等价，避免错误编辑编码器。

Result: 在固定 DINOv2 或 SigLIP 层及 SAE 特征数的三个设置中，12%–70% 的匹配对在容差内可跨种子迁移；随机方向 SAE 和打乱匹配的对照均为 0%。事后预测可迁移性时，层贡献比方向更准，但仍存在少量错误预测，且部分错误在独立图像上持续。

Insight: 创新点是提出以“消融是否跨种子复现输出变化”衡量特征功能等价，而非只看方向匹配；并发现层贡献是更好的预测信号。可借鉴：用功能消融/输出差异做特征对齐验证，结合层贡献筛选与输出测量兜底。

Abstract: A vision encoder turns an image into one vector per patch, refined layer by layer. A sparse autoencoder (SAE) writes each vector of one layer as a sum of learned directions, most with a coefficient of zero. Researchers call each direction a feature and edit the encoder by removing one feature. An SAE trained from another random seed learns different features. Earlier work matches the two SAEs’ features one to one by how closely their directions agree. Matched features can point alike yet respond to different image parts, so removing them may change the encoder differently. We remove a feature and, separately, its match, then compare the changes in the encoder’s output on the same held out images. When the second change nearly reproduces the first, we say the removal carries across seeds. A setting fixes a DINOv2 or SigLIP layer and its SAE feature count. At our tolerance, the removal carries for 12% to 70% of matched pairs across three settings. The share is 0% in two controls: an SAE whose directions stay random and, post hoc, matches shuffled among trained features. Post hoc, predicting which removals carry is more accurate with layer contributions than with directions. Some incorrect predictions remain. One persists on independent images. So pairs can be screened at the layer. Measuring the output detects errors this screen misses.

Motivation: 现有跨模态分类与检索方法多关注样本级对齐，忽视局部判别信息和部分间统计关系，难以充分利用冻结编码器中的潜在多模态知识。

Abstract: Emergent cross-modal classification and retrieval enable transfer between modalities without direct paired supervision. However, existing methods often prioritize sample-level alignment, overlooking local discriminative information and partwise statistical relations. To this end, we propose partwise reasoning across modalities (PRAM), which activates latent multimodal knowledge in frozen encoders by composing partwise statistical relations through a shared pivot modality. Specifically, we first present a multi-granularity covariance regularization module that estimates covariances from global and local vectors in two collections of modality-pivot pairs, stabilizing local estimates through adaptive blending with their cross-part means. Second, we devise a pivot-based spectral relational reasoning module that infers global and partwise statistical relations between target modalities through regularized covariance composition and extracts paired projection directions and their correlation strengths via whitening and spectral decomposition. Finally, we introduce a multi-granularity score fusion module that standardizes and fuses matching scores from global and local relations, incorporating task-specific information on pivot associations and class semantics for class prediction and candidate ranking. Extensive experiments across ten datasets demonstrate that PRAM achieves state-of-the-art performance in 16 classification settings and 19 retrieval settings, while exhibiting highly competitive performance in three sensing scenarios.

Details

Motivation: 视频中的重复动作发生在帧间，模型只采样少量帧，难以直接计数；而编解码器已稠密记录块位移运动，论文希望把这种运动作为计数的直接证据。

Result: 在 MotionBench、TOMATO 和 SVCBench 上相对官方解码器取得一致提升；7B dVLM 在 MotionBench 超过 GPT-5.1 和 Gemini-2.5-Pro，在 TOMATO 超过 GPT-4o 和更大开源模型，在 SVCBench 匹配 GPT-5.4。

Abstract: Counting how many times something happens is among the simplest questions about a video and among the hardest for video-language models, autoregressive and diffusion alike. The difficulty lies between the frames: a model samples only a few of them, while each repetition unfolds as movement in between. Video codecs already record this movement densely as block displacements, and codec-based video models use it to shape their visual input. We are the first to show that this motion provides direct evidence for the count: a candidate count is a testable motion hypothesis, and unwinding the cycles it implies measures how strongly the observed directions support it. We present CoDiCount, a unified training-free framework that turns this test into a count potential and applies it while a frozen diffusion vision-language model (dVLM) denoises its answer. At every denoising step, structured count messages map each option letter, or each grammar-valid edit of an open integer, to the count it expresses and reweight the model’s own probabilities within that set. Consistent gains over the official decoders on MotionBench, TOMATO, and SVCBench demonstrate the broad generalization of CoDiCount across diffusion models, datasets, and diverse counting tasks. With CoDiCount, a 7B dVLM outperforms GPT-5.1 and Gemini-2.5-Pro on MotionBench, surpasses GPT-4o and much larger open models on TOMATO, and matches GPT-5.4 on SVCBench.

Abstract: Generative video models are becoming powerful renderers, yet lack the predictable control interfaces of conventional systems such as HTML/CSS engines or Blender. Their capabilities emerge implicitly from training, making it difficult to determine what they can reliably execute. We present Neural Rendering Compiler (NeurCo), which automatically probes frozen video models to construct model-specific capability manuals describing which semantic requirements they can reliably satisfy. NeurCo represents user intent in a model-agnostic intermediate representation of objects, constraints, events, and cameras, then compiles it into model-specific execution strategies. Reliable requirements remain unchanged, while unreliable ones are selectively rewritten or compiled into alternative execution strategies. We introduce VComBench, a benchmark of 380 compositional, multi-requirement tasks spanning text-to-video, image-to-video, and reference-to-video generation. Abstract: Answering a knowledge-based visual question requires first recognizing the entity in the image and then recalling knowledge about it. Thinking vision-language models (VLMs) reason for thousands of tokens before answering, yet it remains underexplored how well such thinking VLMs perform each of these two steps. We therefore analyze thinking VLMs on this task and make two key observations. (1) Recognition is the bottleneck, since 76.3% of errors across four models on ViQuAE stem from misrecognizing the entity, and 84.9% of these failures are resolved once the entity name is provided in the question. (2) Recognition is driven by entity-recall clues rather than by general descriptions of the image. An entity-recall clue is a distinctive visual detail that helps the model recall the entity, such as keys, a traditional attribute of Saint Peter. Based on these findings, we propose ClueRL, a reinforcement learning approach that extends Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with an advantage localized to the entity-recall clue in the thinking trace. Rather than injecting new entity knowledge, ClueRL changes how VLMs think: it leads them to attend more closely to visual details and spot these clues, helping them use the knowledge they already have. Consequently, it outperforms both GRPO and a perception-reward baseline on three entity recognition benchmarks. Moreover, this clue-seeking behavior generalizes beyond entity recognition, the only task used for training. Since recognition is the bottleneck, the gains carry over to knowledge-based visual question answering. They even extend to general multimodal reasoning, raising Qwen3.5-4B by 4.4 and 4.0 points on the ScienceQA and MMMU subsets of MMStar, respectively.

Motivation: 交互式世界模型需要持续生成视频并及时响应用户动作，但仅有实时吞吐并不能解决输入延迟：粗粒度生成块会延迟动作更新，而输入后再生成又会把额外计算加入响应路径。论文旨在将播放期计算用于提前准备未来，降低交互响应延迟。

Abstract: Interactive world models must generate video continuously while responding promptly to user actions. Real-time throughput alone leaves input latency unresolved: coarse generation blocks delay action updates, and generation after input adds work to the response path. We introduce ShowNow, a fine-grained speculative streaming framework that uses computation available during playback to prepare action-conditioned futures before input arrives. Single-latent commitment enables frequent decisions, while configurable preparation covers one to eight actions. We construct a matched multi-future dataset to train alternative continuations from a common state; shared-history tree execution and fused commitment make their preparation efficient. On a single H100, our 5B model sustains 480p generation at 16 fps with eight prepared futures. These results establish complementary roles for fine-grained commitment and advance preparation, with measured trade-offs in visual fidelity and long-horizon consistency.
