[159] Seeing the Right Context: Grounded On-Policy Distillation for Remote Sensing cs.CVPDF

Yichang Luo, Hourun Zhu, Shixiong Liu, Kan Wei, Shi Liang

TL;DR: 论文针对遥感场景理解中多模态大语言模型难以利用目标周围空间参考和上下文关系的问题，构建了显式包含空间线索、视觉事实和支持区域的GeoContext-5K视觉问答数据集，并提出GeOPD方法，通过带区域标记和视觉事实的在线策略蒸馏，将冻结教师模型的纠正知识传递给学生模型。该方法还利用证据恢复时学生下一词分布偏移与教师纠正的一致性进行筛选，并通过掩码视图对比加权优化。

Details

Motivation: 标准遥感VQA答案和边界框往往隐含目标与邻近物体、周围结构之间的上下文关系，导致MLLM难以正确解释查询目标及其空间参考。论文旨在将这些隐式上下文显式化，并让学生模型在蒸馏中学会关注正确上下文。

Result: 在五个遥感VQA基准上，GeOPD优于所评估的开源MLLM和匹配的后训练基线，相比4B和9B基座模型平均分别提升5.81和2.97个百分点。

Insight: 创新点在于构建带空间线索、视觉事实和支持区域的GeoContext-5K，并将grounded上下文与在线策略蒸馏结合：优先采用与学生分布偏移一致的教师纠正，并用掩码导致的教师对数概率下降来加权双视图对比。客观看，其可借鉴之处是利用证据恢复引起的分布变化筛选蒸馏信号，使上下文监督更贴合学生当前策略。

Abstract: Remote sensing scene understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to interpret queried targets in relation to nearby objects and surrounding structures, which serve as spatial references or define the scope of an answer. However, standard answers and bounding boxes often leave these contextual relationships implicit. Thus, we construct GeoContext-5K to make them explicit in 5,394 training pairs for visual question answering (VQA), with spatial cues, visual facts, and supporting regions. GeOPD uses these annotations for on-policy distillation, guiding a frozen teacher with region marks on the full image and a visual fact while preserving surrounding spatial references. It prioritizes teacher corrections that agree with the shift in the student’s next token distribution when evidence is restored. A complementary objective weights the contrast between the student’s scores on the two views by how much masking lowers the teacher’s log probability for the same sampled token. Experiments on five remote sensing VQA benchmarks show that GeOPD outperforms the evaluated open-source MLLMs and matched post-training baselines, improving over the 4B and 9B base models by 5.81 and 2.97 percentage points on average. The dataset and code will be publicly released at https://github.com/YeeCharm/GeOPD.

[160] Reading, Not Adapting: Unlocking Frozen Visual Knowledge for AI-Generated Image Detection cs.CVPDF

Yuncheng Guo, Ying Dong, Xiaodong Gu

TL;DR: 论文提出ReMoT，一种受Mixture-of-Transformers启发的AI生成图像检测框架，在完全冻结的视觉基础模型上，从空间对齐的RGB与小波视图读取互补知识，而非适配主干网络。方法通过分阶段联合注意力和可学习融合查询实现跨视图交互与逐层知识读出。

Details

Motivation: 现有方法通常适配预训练视觉表示以提升对未见生成器的泛化，但进一步适配可能损害基础模型已有的可迁移检测知识；传统线性探测又仅利用最终层RGB特征，多线索检测器则存在适配、异构编码器和晚融合等权衡。

Result: 在八个基准组上取得91.2%的平均准确率，仅需3,074个可训练参数，并在评估方法中获得最高总体平均表现。

Insight: 核心创新是从主干适配转向结构化知识读出：冻结视觉基础模型，联合利用RGB和小波视图，通过分阶段联合注意力控制局部与全局跨视图交互，并用可学习融合查询沿Transformer深度渐进读取互补证据且无回写。

Abstract: Generalization to unseen image generators has driven efforts to adapt pretrained visual representations for detection. Yet recent findings show that vision foundation models already encode transferable detection knowledge, while further adaptation can impair generalization. This motivates a shift from backbone adaptation to knowledge readout. Conventional linear probing offers a direct way to read this knowledge but is limited to final-layer RGB features. Multi-cue detectors broaden the evidence base, but their designs introduce other tradeoffs: adaptation may compromise pretrained transferability, heterogeneous encoders complicate feature integration, and late fusion precludes intermediate cross-view interaction. To overcome these limitations, we propose ReMoT, a Mixture-of-Transformers-inspired framework that reads complementary knowledge from spatially aligned RGB and wavelet views within a fully frozen visual foundation. ReMoT employs staged joint attention to enable controlled local and global cross-view interaction, while learnable fusion queries progressively read complementary evidence across Transformer depth without writeback. ReMoT achieves 91.2% average accuracy across eight benchmark groups with 3,074 trainable parameters, attaining the highest overall average among the evaluated methods. These results support structured knowledge readout as a promising alternative to backbone adaptation. Code is available at https://github.com/yunncheng/ReMoT.

[161] WorldInteract: Controllable Streaming Video Generation for Multi-Material Interactions cs.CVPDF

Ahmed Hatem, Caoliwen Wang, Robert Hoang, Siyuan Chen, Spencer Szabados

TL;DR: WorldInteract 是一个面向多材料交互的可控流式视频生成框架，将跨材料交互分解为材料相关行为生成与接触响应计算，并通过共享速度增量接口复用无交互视频中学到的材料行为。物理控制器预测接触并计算速度增量，因果生成器执行这些增量，从而在无需多材料配对接触视频监督的情况下实现跨材料交互。

Details

Motivation: 现有可控视频生成对材料相关动力学研究不足，导致不同材料场景中的可靠控制受限，论文旨在实现多材料交互下可物理控制、可流式生成的视频世界模型。

Result: 论文在 Physics-IQ 上显著优于 SOTA，并在真实世界图像上实现更可靠的推动控制；训练使用覆盖 22 类材料的 3 万段模拟无交互视频。

Insight: 核心创新是将跨材料交互拆分为材料行为生成和接触响应计算，并通过统一速度增量接口复用无交互视频学习到的材料行为；结合物理控制器与 Material-Aware RL 的可验证物理奖励，减少对跨材料接触监督视频的依赖。

Abstract: Video world models aim to create interactive physical worlds that capture how diverse materials move and interact. Yet material-dependent dynamics remain underexplored in controllable video generation, limiting reliable control in scenes with different materials. We present WorldInteract, a streaming video generation framework that decomposes cross-material interaction into material-dependent behavior generation and contact response computation. External pushes and contact impulses are expressed through a shared velocity-increment interface, allowing material behaviors learned from interaction-free videos to be reused in interacting scenes. A physics controller predicts contacts and computes velocity increments from estimated masses and restitution coefficients, while a causal generator executes these increments using learned material behaviors. This separation enables cross-material interaction without supervised contact videos spanning diverse material pairings and contact configurations. We train the generator on 30k simulated interaction-free videos spanning 22 material classes and introduce Material-Aware RL, using verifiable physical rewards to refine material behavior and control execution. WorldInteract substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods on Physics-IQ and delivers more reliable push control on real-world images.

Chandan Yeshwanth, Angela Dai

TL;DR: 论文提出SceneDesigner3D，一种基于智能体进化搜索的3D室内场景重排方法。它将场景重排表示为可编辑程序，利用VLM智能体通过进化搜索生成多样、美观且功能合理的布局，并遵循室内设计原则。在SAGE-10k和SceneSmith数据集上，该方法在提示依从性和设计原则分数上优于基线。

Details

Motivation: 3D场景重排对内容创作至关重要，需要生成多样、美观且功能合理的布局。现有方法难以系统探索设计空间并遵循常见室内设计原则，因此需要一种能自动生成高质量重排方案的方法。

Result: 在SAGE-10k和SceneSmith数据集上，SceneDesigner3D相比基线将提示依从性提升了最多5.1%，计算的设计原则分数提升了最多15.2%。

Insight: 创新点在于将重排表示为可编辑程序，结合进化搜索与VLM智能体进行变异和评估；利用VLM评判设计原则和提示依从性；维护结构多样的候选存档以增加多样性。客观分析，该工作将程序表示、进化算法和视觉语言模型结合，为3D场景设计提供了一种新颖的智能体搜索框架。

Abstract: Scene rearrangement is an essential task for 3D content creation applications, requiring generation of diverse, aesthetic and functional scene rearrangements. We present SceneDesigner3D, an agentic evolutionary search method for 3D indoor scene rearrangement that systematically explores the design space of 3D scenes and generates multiple solutions according to common interior design principles. Given an input scene and a text prompt, SceneDesigner3D represents the rearrangement as an editable program and evolves a population of candidate layouts. Starting from distinct design ideas, a VLM agent mutates the programs by refining promising candidates, exploring new rearrangement strategies or redesigning from scratch, while verifying the layouts for physical validity. Proposals are scored by a VLM judge for adherence to the input prompt and satisfaction of design principles such as balance, zoning and emphasis, as well as numerically computed principles such as circulation and clutter. An archive of top-performing, structurally varied candidates further increases diversity of rearrangements. Experiments on the SAGE-10k and SceneSmith datasets show that SceneDesigner3D improves prompt adherence and computed design principle scores over baselines by up to 5.1% and 15.2%, respectively. Code will be made publicly available.

[163] Guidance as Variance Matching cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Ali Backour, Artem Lukoianov, Frank Permenter, Tommi Jaakkola, Justin Solomon

TL;DR: 论文重新解释扩散模型中的 guidance：尽管它会让模型对后验均值的预测变差，却能通过恢复数据方差来提升样本质量。作者提出 variance-matched guidance，用闭式规则将引导预测的方差匹配到数据方差，从而自动选择 guidance scale。该方法无需调参即可复现 11 个常用模型手工选取的尺度，并在 SD 3.5 Medium 上按噪声水平生成更受偏好的调度。

Details

Motivation: Guidance 能显著提升扩散模型采样质量，但其机制长期缺乏清晰解释；论文关注为何预测后验均值变差时样本反而变好，并试图从方差匹配角度解释和自动化选择 guidance scale。

Result: Variance-matched guidance 无需调参即可恢复图像、视频、音频、运动与世界生成等 11 个广泛使用模型的手工 guidance scale；按噪声水平应用时，在 SD 3.5 Medium 上得到的无超参调度优于手工调度。

Insight: 创新点在于提出闭式方差匹配规则来自动确定 guidance scale，并给出机制解释：每个模型对后验均值的预测存在随机误差，guidance 将这些误差之差重缩放到具有后验协方差的样本中，从而改善采样；该视角还支持负 scale 可用以及用自身 checkpoint 引导需更大 scale 的假设。

Abstract: Guidance repels the prediction of a diffusion model away from a weaker reference prediction. It substantially improves sample quality, but its mechanism remains poorly understood. We first find that, at the scales used in practice, guidance does not improve the prediction of the posterior mean (the model’s training target); it makes the prediction worse, while samples improve. Second, posterior-mean predictions have less variance than the data, and we find that guidance restores it. We introduce variance-matched guidance: a closed-form rule that picks the guidance scale by matching the variance of the guided predictions to that of the data. Without tuning, it recovers the hand-picked scales of eleven widely used models across image, video, audio, motion, and world generation; to our knowledge, no previous explanation of guidance predicts them. Applied per noise level, it yields a schedule without tuned hyperparameters that users prefer to hand-crafted ones on SD 3.5 Medium. Third, we offer an interpretation of the mechanism behind guidance: each model makes a random error in predicting the posterior mean, and guidance rescales the difference of these errors into a sample with the posterior’s covariance, which improves sampling. This view also suggests two new hypotheses that our experiments support: guidance can work with negative scales, and guiding a model with its own checkpoint needs a larger scale than guiding it with an independently trained model.

[164] WorldExpand: Building Coherent 3D Worlds via Sparse Joint Panoramic Expansion cs.CVPDF

Yuyang Yin, Chenjie Cao, Xuhui Zuo, Yuning Gong, Pengfei Wang

TL;DR: 论文提出 WorldExpand，将单张全景图的三维世界扩展建模为稀疏、互补且高分辨率全景观测的联合生成，而非稠密序列生成。方法先选择信息量高的目标视角，再联合合成四个位姿对齐、跨视角一致的全景图，并可直接输入现有重建流程构建显式三维表示。

Details

Motivation: 从单张全景图构建完整三维世界时存在大量未观测区域；现有全景视频方法依赖稠密顺序生成，导致相邻帧冗余、长轨迹误差累积以及独立生成方向不一致。

Result: 实验表明方法能恢复遮挡物后方和远处转角内容，在多个数据集上提升新视角合成与下游三维重建指标，并对多样真实场景具有鲁棒泛化性；相比最先进全景视频方法运行显著更快，同时生成更高分辨率且跨视角一致性更强的观测。

Insight: 创新点在于稀疏互补高分辨率全景观测表示、覆盖引导视角选择（场景自适应象限与 warp 覆盖分数）、基于高斯泼溅几何 warp 的联合全景扩散，以及诊断引导的全局-局部注意力调度以降低高分辨率联合生成成本。

Abstract: Constructing complete 3D worlds from a single panorama is challenging because large portions of the scene remain unobserved. Existing panoramic-video methods expand spatial coverage through dense sequential generation, but neighboring frames contain substantial redundancy, long trajectories accumulate errors, and independently generated directions may be inconsistent. We present WorldExpand, which formulates single-panorama world expansion as the generation of sparse, complementary, and high-resolution panoramic observations. This representation concentrates the generation budget on informative scene regions and preserves the fine-grained details required for 3D construction. WorldExpand addresses two key challenges: where to observe and how to synthesize. Our Coverage-Guided View Selection identifies informative target locations using scene-adaptive quadrants and warp-based coverage scores, while our Joint Panoramic Diffusion uses Gaussian-splatting-based geometric warps to jointly synthesize four pose-grounded and cross-view-consistent panoramas. A diagnostic-guided global–local attention schedule retains global attention only in sensitive layers, reducing the training and inference costs of high-resolution joint generation. The resulting posed panoramas can be directly fed into an existing reconstruction pipeline to build an explicit 3D representation. Experiments show that WorldExpand recovers content behind occluders and around distant corners, improves both novel-view synthesis and downstream 3D reconstruction metrics across various datasets, and generalizes robustly to diverse in-the-wild scenes. WorldExpand runs significantly faster than the state-of-the-art panoramic-video method while producing higher-resolution observations with stronger cross-view consistency.

TL;DR: 论文提出 HOG-WAM，从示范图像中提取未来方向梯度直方图（HOG）作为结构监督，并引入辅助 HOG 扩散 Transformer 通过非对称注意力读取 RGB 特征，使该监督目标塑造用于控制的视觉表示。该模块仅在训练时使用、推理时移除，从而保持 FastWAM 的 RGB 到动作架构，并提升视觉干扰下的鲁棒性。

Details

Motivation: 世界-动作模型联合预测未来观测和动作，但视觉损坏会干扰策略。论文希望利用未来 HOG 提供的结构信息作为补充监督，在不改变推理架构的情况下增强视觉表示和控制的视觉鲁棒性。

Result: 在 RoboTwin-Plus 的四种受控视觉损坏下成功率均最高，混合噪声下相比 FastWAM 从 78.86% 提升到 82.86%，干净场景性能相当；在 LIBERO Plus 上整体成功率从 74.49% 提升到 75.26%，传感器噪声下从 74.14% 提升到 78.89%；在四个 Franka 任务上，干净观测平均成功率从 40% 提升到 59%，噪声观测从 17% 提升到 24%。

Insight: 创新点在于将未来 HOG 预测作为训练期辅助结构监督，并通过非对称注意力让该目标影响控制所用的 RGB 表示，同时推理时移除辅助模块以保持效率。消融表明 RGB 观测加辅助 HOG 预测优于用 HOG 替换 RGB，说明方向梯度信息是有益的互补监督，且在物理任务上的绝对增益更明显。

Abstract: World-action models jointly predict future observations and actions, yet visual corruption can disrupt the resulting policies. We introduce HOG-WAM, which adds future Histograms of Oriented Gradients (HOG) as structural supervision derived directly from demonstration images. An auxiliary HOG diffusion transformer (DiT) module reads RGB features through asymmetric attention, allowing its objective to shape the visual representation used for control. The module is used only during training and removed at inference, preserving FastWAM’s RGB-to-action architecture. We evaluate four controlled observation corruptions through a visual-corruption extension of RoboTwin, termed RoboTwin-Plus; HOG-WAM achieves the highest success among the compared policies under all four. Mixed-noise success increases from 78.86% to 82.86% relative to FastWAM, with comparable performance on clean scenes. On LIBERO Plus, overall success increases from 74.49% to 75.26%, with the largest gain under sensor noise, from 74.14% to 78.89%. Across four Franka tasks, average success increases from 40% to 59% under clean observations and from 17% to 24% under noisy observations. A representation ablation favors combining RGB observations with auxiliary HOG prediction over replacing RGB with HOG. These findings support future-gradient prediction as complementary supervision for visually robust control, with larger absolute gains on physical tasks than in simulation. Code is available at https://github.com/iPPLab/HOG-WAM.

[166] Solve Vision with Code: Coding Agents Are Stronger Visual Reasoners Than Video Models cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.LG | cs.MMPDF

Dezhi Luo, Fengyuan Yu, Haotian Zhang, Zehong Zhao, Ran Ji

TL;DR: 论文在相同输入、输出规范和评估器条件下，对比“视频生成模型直接生成答案视频”与“编码智能体编写程序渲染答案视频”两种视觉推理路径。实验表明，在VBVR-Pro-Bench上，闭源编码智能体显著优于最强视频模型，说明对于可由首帧和提示计算求解的任务，程序合成是更可靠的视觉推理方式。

Details

Motivation: 论文质疑视频生成模型作为视觉推理器的“原生视觉”路径是否真的更可靠，并提出用符号化替代方案——让编码智能体写程序生成视频——在同等条件下进行公平对比。

Result: 在VBVR-Pro-Bench的500个实例上，Codex、Claude Code和Gemini CLI分别达到0.923、0.894和0.893，均比最强视频模型高0.22以上；开源模型中GLM-5达到0.572，超过除RL训练视频模型外的所有视频模型。

Insight: 创新点在于将视觉推理任务统一为程序合成问题，并用同一评估器公平比较视频生成与代码生成；论文还区分“描绘式程序”和“命题式程序”，分析智能体程序中视觉推理如何展开及其对推理本质的启示。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly evaluated as visual reasoners: given a first frame and a natural-language prompt, can the model produce the video in which the specified task is carried out? The paradigm is considered advantageous because the reasoning is native to the visual medium, unfolding directly in the frames rather than through language or code. Whether this native route is in fact the more reliable path to visual reasoning has not been tested against a symbolic alternative under the same conditions. We therefore pose the same tasks, with identical inputs, output specification and evaluator, to both media: rather than denoising pixels, a coding agent writes a program that renders the answer video, which an unmodified rule-based evaluator scores. On VBVR-Pro-Bench (Xu et al., 2026), a 500-instance suite spanning five cognitive categories with symbolic ground truth, the agent receives the same first frame and prompt as the video model, with no access to ground truth, metadata or generation models, and one attempt per instance. Three closed-model agents (Codex with gpt-6-astra, Claude Code with Fable 5.1 and Gemini CLI with Gemini 3.1 Pro) score 0.923, 0.894 and 0.893, each more than 0.22 above the strongest video model. Among 23 runnable open-weight models, the best, GLM-5, reaches 0.572, above all video models except the RL-trained one. Together, we show that for tasks whose solutions can be computed from a first frame and a prompt, program synthesis is currently a substantially more reliable route to visual reasoning than video generation. We further distinguish depictive programs, which navigate the image to recover a transferable scheme, from propositional programs, which operate on hard-coded values, revealing how visual reasoning unfolds in agent-written programs and what it implies for its nature of reasoning in general.

[167] BOP-Refer: Benchmark for Monocular 3D and 2D Object Grounding cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.ROPDF

Tomas Hodan, Vineet Bhat, Martin Sundermeyer, Jonathan Tremblay, Yash Patel

TL;DR: 论文提出 BOP-Refer，这是首个面向日常与工业物体的单目 3D 和 2D 物体 grounding 基准，数据来自 9 个 BOP 数据集，并用 VLM 生成、人工校验的指代表达及由 3D 模型和 6DoF 位姿得到的精确 2D/3D 真值框。论文系统评测了 17 个前沿 VLM，发现当前模型在 2D grounding 上表现较好，但在 3D grounding 上仍受深度/度量尺度估计严重制约。

Details

Motivation: 现有评测缺乏统一基准来检验视觉语言模型能否根据指代表达在单目图像中同时定位物体的 2D 区域和 3D 空间位置，尤其是日常与工业物体场景。论文希望系统评估前沿 VLM 的单目 3D/2D object grounding 能力并定位主要失败来源。

Result: 在 BOP-Refer 上评测 17 个来自 Gemini、GPT、Claude、Qwen、Gemma 等家族的前沿 VLM，最佳模型 Gemini 4 Carbon 在 2D 达到 72.6% AP_IoU2D，但 3D 仅 12.4% AP_IoU3D，尽管 3D IoU 阈值更宽松。误差分析显示预测 3D 框通常在图像中居中于目标，但深度偏差常超过物体自身尺寸，说明度量尺度估计是当前主要误差来源。

Insight: 创新点在于构建首个跨多 BOP 数据集的单目 3D/2D object grounding 基准，并利用 3D 模型与 6DoF 位姿生成精确 2D/3D 真值，同时引入 VLM 生成且人工校验的指代表达。客观来看，该工作揭示了当前 VLM 在 2D 定位尚可但 3D 深度/尺度估计严重不足，为后续引入可靠物体尺寸先验和度量尺度估计方法提供了评测基础。

Abstract: We introduce BOP-Refer, the first benchmark for monocular 3D and 2D grounding of everyday and industrial objects sourced from 9 diverse BOP datasets. [168] Focus Only on What’s Important: Training-Free Focus Maps for Frozen Vision Transformers in Long-Video Object Segmentation cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

TL;DR: 论文面向长视频目标分割，为冻结的DINOv3 ViT加Cutie分割头提出免训练聚焦图：在分割器预期目标区域保留全分辨率，在背景区域采用更粗的token合并，以降低ViT编码计算。方法用速度平移与块匹配mask的并集、相机补偿的surprise检查、不确定区域委派和全目标丢失回退，提升长视频快速运动下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision transformers are strong but costly image encoders for video object segmentation. Training-free token merging cuts compute uniformly across the frame, including on tracked objects. We give a frozen DINOv3 ViT inside a trained Cutie head a training-free focus map: full resolution where the segmenter expects its objects and coarser merging elsewhere. Protecting only the previous frame’s mask works on short clips but fails on long videos, where fast motion pushes targets outside the margin and into background merging, locking in loss. Our policy instead takes the union of velocity-shifted and block-matched masks, runs a camera-compensated surprise check, delegates uncertain regions to the compared merger, and falls back to it when all objects are lost. Controls qualify the result: protecting only the previous mask under ALGM matches or exceeds accuracy at higher FLOPs (0.486), so expectation and surprise mainly reduce counted encoder compute. Practically, lower counted encoder compute did not reduce startup-inclusive mean Cutie step time on a 12-video LVOS sample (3%-8% lower in steady state; an equally engineered ALGM is faster at ViT-S and ViT-B). Abstract: Cross-modal place recognition (CMPR) matches natural language descriptions against databases of geo-tagged reference images, enabling localization without visual queries. Despite its potential for language-guided navigation, existing vision-language models lag far behind visual place recognition (VPR) baselines, even after task adaptation. Unlike general-purpose text-to-image retrieval, place recognition relies on subtle local cues to distinguish visually similar locations. We therefore hypothesize that a primary bottleneck in CMPR is capturing fine-grained correspondences between image regions and text details, which requires higher visual token granularity, strong text representation capacity, and local cross-modal matching. To address this challenge, we propose CrossPlace, a two-stage CMPR framework. Through systematic analysis, we isolate the drivers of retrieval accuracy and show that visual token density provides the largest performance improvement, while text encoder capacity and task fine-tuning offer complementary gains. To evaluate robustness under realistic conditions, we also extend CMPR evaluation to city-scale and severe weather and illumination settings. Across all existing and new benchmarks, CrossPlace achieves double-digit Recall@1 improvements over prior cross-modal methods, closes the gap to unimodal text retrieval, and narrows it to unimodal VPR. [170] Stranger Things: When Objects Appear Without Their Typical Neighbors cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Siddhartha Gairola, Jiahao Xie, Anna Kukleva, Francesco Locatello, Bernt Schiele

TL;DR: 论文系统诊断现代视觉模型中的“典型性偏差”：模型在识别物体时过度依赖其常见共现邻居，而非物体自身视觉特征。作者用NPMI量化物体对典型性，构建STRANGE-Bench分层评测基准，覆盖从典型到从未共现的物体对，并在五类模型和三类任务上发现普遍存在5-11个百分点的性能差距。为缓解该问题，提出Anchor-Atypicality Sampling（AAS），通过NPMI信号调整paste类数据增强的共现采样分布，在不牺牲总体性能的情况下显著缩小典型性差距。

Details

Motivation: 视觉上下文有助于识别，但过度依赖上下文会使模型根据典型邻居而非物体本身进行预测，形成典型性偏差。尽管该问题在预Transformer时代已有研究，现代视觉栈缺乏系统诊断，且常被假设会被架构和规模改进自然解决。

Result: 在五类模型家族和三类识别任务上，物体出现在非典型上下文时性能差距达5-11个百分点，且不随模型规模一致缩小。AAS在COCO自然图像划分上最多降低1.6个百分点典型性差距，在合成STRANGE-Bench上最多降低23.0个百分点，且不损害总体性能。

Abstract: Visual context is a powerful cue for object recognition: scenes inform what, where, and at what scale objects are likely to appear. Yet over-reliance on context becomes a failure mode when models predict objects based on their typical neighbors rather than the objects’ own visual features, which we call typicality bias. While extensively studied in the pre-Transformer era, it has often been assumed that architectural and scale improvements would naturally solve it, and a systematic diagnostic across the modern vision stack remains absent. We provide such a diagnostic framework. We measure typicality bias via normalized pointwise mutual information (NPMI) over object pairs, and introduce STRANGE-Bench (Synthetic Typicality-RANked Grounded Evaluation Benchmark): a photorealistic, layout-controlled benchmark spanning 17 sub-tiers from typical to never-co-occurring object pairs. Across five model families (specialist detectors, open-vocabulary detectors, self-supervised, vision-language, and multimodal large language models) and three recognition tasks (supervised detection, open-vocabulary detection, and classification), the bias is prevalent: gaps of 5-11 p.p. persist when objects appear outside their typical company, and do not consistently shrink with scale. To mitigate it, we turn to data augmentation: existing augmentations are designed for visual diversity, not for breaking co-occurrence shortcuts, and sample from the very distribution that produces typicality bias. We propose Anchor-Atypicality Sampling (AAS), a simple sampling rule for paste-based augmentations that uses the NPMI signal to tilt the training-time co-occurrence distribution toward atypical partners. AAS reduces the typicality gap by up to 1.6 p.p. on natural-image COCO splits and 23.0 p.p. on synthetic STRANGE-Bench across three model families, without sacrificing overall performance.

Abstract: How an object deforms when it hits the ground reveals what it is made of. From a single real video of a drop, we estimate a deformable object’s density-normalized stiffness $E/ρ$, and its yield stress $σ_Y/ρ$ when the impact causes plastic deformation, with a feed-forward predictor trained only in simulation. Real videos carry no material labels, so such predictors are developed and validated on synthetic data alone. We show that synthetic validation ranks their geometric inputs in the wrong order. Material acts through 3D deformation, and tracklets lifted to 3D with estimated depth are indeed the most accurate input in simulation. However, on real drops they are the least accurate, and better depth does not repair them: the failure persists with simulator depth in training, LiDAR depth at test time, and exact depth on rendered replays of the real drops. Short 2D optical-flow tracklets do the opposite: they improve nothing in simulation, yet they transfer best. Material is therefore better read from what perception measures consistently in both domains than from what is most informative in simulation. Our predictor, PhysReader, reads the video and 2D tracklets and learns the lift to 3D in simulation by matching a teacher that sees exact 3D deformation. To measure transfer, we introduce PhysReader-Real: 16 everyday objects dropped five times each, with reference materials fit jointly to each object’s drops and checked against tensile tests. On PhysReader-Real, PhysReader is closer to the reference materials than every other input and baseline. The one exception is a per-scene optimizer started from PhysReader’s prediction, which ends slightly closer but takes 49 minutes per video instead of 12 seconds.

Abstract: Processing long videos with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is prohibitively expensive, making informative frame selection essential for accurate video understanding. The default uniform sampling often misses task-relevant keyframes, while recent training-free selectors instead pick frames by how well each one matches the question under an image-text alignment (ITA) score. However, we show that ITA signals correspond poorly with the MLLM’s true needs: their rankings correlate weakly with downstream accuracy and are even negatively correlated on over 40% of questions, whereas the MLLM’s internal yes/no judgment yields a much stronger signal. We present ProbFaS, a training- and label-free probabilistic pipeline that fuses both signals for key frame selection. ProbFaS treats dense ITA scores as cheap, biased measurements and sparse MLLM judgments as accurate, costly ones, unifying them via co-kriging, a multi-fidelity Gaussian process, to calibrate relevance across all frames. Abstract: Real-time autoregressive video generators stream minutes of video and accept new prompts on the fly, but struggle to keep people, objects, and places consistent over a long interactive story. Agent systems provide such memory for offline multi-shot generation, yet they read the whole script in advance, plan each shot before generating it, and regenerate failed shots, which a live stream does not allow. We present Agentic LongLive, an asynchronous agentic memory for long video generation. A memory agent on separate GPUs reads only the prompts revealed so far, never the generated video, and keeps a text state and evolving images for every person, object, and scene. [174] LiveVAE: Causal Window Attention Makes Efficient Streaming Video VAE cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出 LiveVAE，一种基于因果窗口注意力（causal window attention）的流式视频 VAE 编码器-解码器。作者通过分析预训练视频 VAE 的有效感受野，发现每个 patch 的编解码几乎只依赖时空邻域，因此用局部因果窗口注意力替代重卷积和全帧注意力，并保持有界的时序 KV cache 以支持流式推理。通过编码器/解码器解耦蒸馏加联合微调保持原有 latent 空间，可直接替换原 VAE。

Abstract: While video diffusion models have grown faster and increasingly streamable, the video VAE remains a primary latency bottleneck across interactive generation and editing pipelines. Existing VAEs and their acceleration methods rely mainly on heavy convolutions or full frame attention, and most focus only on decoding, leaving encoding costly for streaming conditional generation and editing. This raises a natural question: Do video VAE encoding and decoding actually require such global spatiotemporal modeling? By analyzing the effective receptive fields of pretrained video VAEs, we find that both encoding and decoding a patch rely almost entirely on its spatial and temporal neighbors. To exploit this strong locality, we introduce LiveVAE, a streaming encoder and decoder built on causal window attention, which cuts per frame compute while keeping the temporal KV cache bounded for streaming. Decoupled encoder/decoder distillation followed by joint finetuning preserves the pretrained latent space, making LiveVAE a drop-in replacement for the original VAE. On MiniMax-H3, LiveVAE achieves 5.2-6.2x faster encoding and 3.0-3.3x faster decoding than the official VAE with higher PSNR. Paired with our dedicated inference infrastructure, LiveVAE pushes the speedup to 13.4x with almost no quality loss. Code and models will be released.

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown strong potential for image matting by transferring generative priors learned from large-scale pretraining. However, existing approaches largely rely on latent diffusion models, whose compressed representations and VAE-based reconstruction introduce a mismatch with the high-frequency precision required for matting. We present PixelMatte, a framework that adapts pretrained pixel-space diffusion transformers for high-fidelity image matting. We show that strong image-conditioned matting capabilities can be acquired even from a pure text-to-image model without paired image-to-image pretraining. Our approach reuses the backbone’s native patch embedding to jointly process the input image, trimap, and noisy alpha matte as spatially aligned tokens, enabling direct pixel-space prediction without additional condition encoders or adapters. Two lightweight calibration modules, LayerShift and High-Pass Refiner, further improve regional opacity accuracy and boundary fidelity. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of pixel-space generative priors for precision-sensitive image matting. Project page: https://white-yc.github.io/pixelmatte/.

[176] DynMF2: Rigid Motion Factorization by Expectation-Maximization for Monocular 4D Reconstruction and Motion Segmentation cs.CVPDF

Agelos Kratimenos, Yile Hua, Kostas Daniilidis

TL;DR: 论文提出DynMF2，将单目视频中的场景运动分解为若干条刚体SE(3)轨迹，并约束每个3D点只跟随其中一条运动，从而缓解单目4D重建的欠定性。方法直接从3D点轨迹出发，用EM算法无需渲染地完成刚体运动拟合与点分配，背景被视为相机运动，无需掩码；再用requery、merge、split修正分解，使运动数量自适应场景。

Details

Motivation: 单目视频中一个点在不同视角下可沿相机方向滑动仍匹配所有帧，导致4D重建严重欠定；现有方法常依赖运动掩码，难以处理薄结构、细节和远处小运动物体。论文希望利用运动本身的刚体结构约束重建，并避免预先指定或依赖掩码来划分运动。

Result: 在无掩码条件下，DynMF2在iPhone和NVIDIA数据集的新视角合成上明显优于基于掩码的基线，在iPhone的3D跟踪上也更优；还能恢复掩码遗漏的薄部件、细节和远处小运动物体。在MOVi-F和iTACO上，其运动分割具有竞争力，尽管模型从未针对分割任务训练。

Insight: 创新点在于将场景运动表示为少量刚体SE(3)轨迹，并用EM直接对3D点轨迹做硬分配式运动分解，无需渲染和掩码；背景作为相机运动处理，运动数量由场景自适应确定。客观看，其可借鉴之处是用刚体运动先验约束欠定单目重建，并通过requery/merge/split等工具迭代修正跟踪与运动分解。

Abstract: A single casual video leaves a moving scene under-determined: a point seen once from each place can slide toward or away from the camera and still match every frame. We constrain the reconstruction with the structure of the motion itself. Building on DynMF, we model a scene’s motion as a handful of SE(3) trajectories, each rigid, and let every point follow one of them alone, never a blend. DynMF2 computes this factorization directly from 3D point trajectories, with no rendering, by Expectation-Maximization (EM). Its M-step fits, in closed form, every rigid motion to the points assigned to it, and its E-step reassigns every point to the motion that explains it with the least error. The background is simply one more motion, the camera’s, so no masks are needed. Three tools then revise the factorization: requery adds trajectories where the rendering shows parts the tracker missed, merge joins motions that move together, and split separates those that do not. Thus the number of motions follows the scene. Without masks, DynMF2 clearly outperforms the mask-based baselines in view synthesis on iPhone and NVIDIA and in 3D tracking on iPhone. It recovers what masks leave out, from thin parts and details to small movers far in the background. Its motions segment the moving parts of MOVi-F and iTACO scenes competitively, although DynMF2 is never trained to segment. What moves in a scene is not decided in advance: it emerges from the rigid motions themselves.

Abstract: We present LuxAvatar, a feed-forward framework for real-time head avatar animation and relighting from a single portrait. To achieve both 3D controllability and complex facial light transport, we factorize avatar synthesis into an animatable 3D intrinsic Gaussian proxy and a causal autoregressive neural shader. This proxy disentangles canonical geometry and reflectance attributes, rapidly rasterizing deformed Gaussians into view- and motion-consistent PBR buffers. Conditioned on these buffers, our neural shader formulates forward rendering as a conditional video diffusion process, bypassing explicit PBR computation to synthesize realistic shading effects under target illumination. We achieve real-time streaming through heterogeneous distillation from a pretrained multi-step bidirectional relighting teacher to a two-step causal autoregressive neural shader, inheriting broad synthetic lighting priors while minimizing conditioning and decoding latency. To further specialize these priors to human heads, we construct a hybrid relighting dataset for domain-specific fine-tuning, capturing authentic facial dynamics and physically grounded light transport. Is it possible to transfer advanced image editing capabilities to video editors without new paired videos? We introduce Image-to-Video Transfer (I2VT), which improves pretrained video editors and expands them to new editing tasks using an image editor. It enables efficient post-training with no architectural changes and no new videos, using only individual images. [181] Certify What You Plant: Shortcut-Free Proof Depth in Logical Reasoning Data cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.LOPDF

Baptiste Juran, Dimitri Kachler, Damien Sileo

TL;DR: 论文指出程序生成的逻辑推理数据常通过“植入证明”的深度来设定难度，但该深度可能高估问题实际所需，因为问题可能存在更短证明或无关证明。作者提出计算并验证每个问题的最短证明、必经步骤、最小证明数和不可或缺前提，生成可独立检查的证书；审计八个数据集后，构造每个证明都符合指定结构的无捷径问题，并验证训练数据混合策略。

Details

Motivation: 现有逻辑推理数据用采样植入证明的深度来标定问题难度，但植入证明并非唯一证明，会导致难度标签失真，进而影响模型评估和训练。论文希望认证问题实际所需的最小证明深度和关键推理步骤。

Result: 审计八个数据集发现：ProofWriter和Blocksworld的深度是强制的，FLDx2中标注深度5的问题实际约需两个特定规则应用，ZebraLogic的答案不需要特定步骤。训练实验显示，在等数据规模下，认证问题类型的混合数据（含前向生成不产生的模型检查问题）对Qwen3 0.6B至8B模型的迁移效果优于FLDx2式推导数据。

Insight: 创新点在于用可独立验证的证书审计逻辑问题的真实证明难度，并通过按规则集预证明捷径目录来构造每个证明均满足目标结构的无捷径问题。客观上看，其可借鉴之处是将数据难度认证、最小证明分析和多样化问题类型混合结合，提升推理训练数据的可控性与迁移性。

Abstract: Procedurally generated logic problems are used to both evaluate and train reasoning in language models. Their difficulty is usually set by planting a proof, that is, by sampling a proof of the desired depth and stating its premises as the problem. However, a planted proof is only one possible proof. A problem can also have shorter proofs, or several unrelated ones, and its stated depth then overstates what solving it requires. We measure what a problem actually requires, relative to a stated set of inference rules. For each problem, we compute the steps every proof must make, the shortest proof, the number of minimal proofs and the indispensable premises, and export certificates that an independent checker verifies. Audits of eight datasets find depth that is forced (ProofWriter, Blocksworld), problems labelled depth 5 that require about two specific rule applications (FLDx2), and answers that need no particular step (ZebraLogic). We then construct problems whose every proof has a requested structure, using a catalog of shortcuts proved once per rule set. Every constructed problem is checked in full and comes with its own certificate. In training, the best data mixes depths and problem types. At equal size, such mixtures of certified problem types, including model-checking problems that forward generation does not produce, transfer better than FLDx2-style derivation data for Qwen3 models from 0.6B to 8B.

[182] The Platonic Representation Hypothesis in Jacobian-Space: Language and Vision Models Agree Approximately Up to a Rotation cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Ross Murphy, Gabriel Brostow

TL;DR: 论文研究柏拉图表示假设（PRH）在Jacobian空间（J-space）中的表现，发现视觉和语言模型的J-space近似收敛到一个旋转。实验表明，通过拟合一个旋转矩阵，可以将一个模型的J-space映射到另一个模型，并且该旋转能泛化到未拟合的词和类。旋转后的词向量能改变视觉-语言模型的预测、图像生成的内容以及ImageNet分类器的得分，表明模型不仅在表示上收敛，在如何作用于表示上也收敛。

Details

Motivation: 以往PRH证据主要基于模型激活的相似性，本文探究视觉和语言模型是否在如何利用内部表示影响输出（即动作层面）上也收敛，以更全面地验证PRH。

Result: 在视觉和语言模型的J-space中发现PRH证据，且J-space大多收敛到一个旋转。在部分词上拟合的旋转能泛化到未拟合词和类；旋转词向量到VLM可改变其物体预测（且仅影响添加区域），到图像生成器可改变生成物体，到ImageNet分类器可显著提升对应类得分。

Insight: 创新点在于将PRH从激活空间扩展到Jacobian空间，提出J-space作为分析模型动作收敛的新视角，并发现模型间J-space近似旋转对齐，为表示收敛提供了动作层面的证据。客观分析，该工作揭示了跨模态模型在功能层面的对齐性，为模型可解释性和跨模型知识迁移提供了新思路。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) posits that AI models converge toward a shared representation of the world, even when trained on different data modalities. Previous work has found evidence for the PRH in the activations of vision and language models. In this work, we ask whether vision and language models also converge in how they act on these representations: precisely, in how these internal representations affect the models’ output. At a chosen layer of a transformer, the Jacobian lens gives every word in the vocabulary (or class in an image classifier) a vector in the hidden state: the vector that most increases the model’s tendency to generate that word, at that position or later, or to predict that class. Collectively, these vectors make up what is referred to as the model’s J-space. We find evidence of the PRH in J-space across vision and language models, and furthermore, we find that J-spaces mostly converge up to a rotation. That is, a single rotation, fitted on a subset of words, approximately maps one model’s J-space onto another’s, and so applies to words and classes it was not fitted on. A word’s vector, rotated from a text-only language model’s J-space into the visual patch tokens of a vision-language model, changes the object the vision-language model says is in the image (and only in the image region where it is added). Rotated into an image generator’s text conditioning, a word’s vector changes the object in the generated image. Rotated into an ImageNet classifier (trained without text), it raises that object’s class score above almost all others, and vice versa. This suggests that models converge not only in how they represent the world, but also in how they act on it.

[183] Aligned Machine: A Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Embedding Similarity via Human Comparative Judgements cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Jiechen Li, Hannah Groos, Brinnae Bent

TL;DR: 论文提出 Aligned Machine，一个面向公众的交互式平台，通过让人比较两组文本或图像项目对中哪一对更相似，持续构建人类对齐相似度基准 HASB，用于评估嵌入模型的语义相似度是否符合人类判断。作者用初始数据评估了 12 个常用嵌入模型，发现嵌入相似度与人类判断仅部分对齐，且纯文本模型总体一致性高于多模态模型，多模态模型在不同任务类型上表现差异明显。

Details

Motivation: 现有嵌入相似度评测多依赖静态、专家标注和粗粒度 Likert 标签，难以规模化且难以获得高置信、一致的人类判断；论文希望以可扩展、可审计的方式检验嵌入系统是否真正反映人类对语义相似度的理解。

Result: 初始部署中 115 名参与者贡献 2,875 个标注对，其中 872 个唯一对；基于 HASB 评估 12 个广泛使用的嵌入模型，发现其与人类判断仅部分对齐。纯文本模型总体一致性高于多模态模型；多模态模型在图像-图像比较上对齐最强，图像-文本较弱，文本-文本最低。

Insight: 创新点在于用非专家可参与的强制选择比较任务收集干净偏好数据，并通过稳健聚合构建持续增长的人类对齐相似度基准；客观看，其价值在于将众包比较判断、多模态嵌入审计和纵向/领域/偏差/概念漂移分析结合为可扩展评测框架。

Abstract: Embedding models increasingly inform how people search, recommend, and retrieve information, yet we lack credible ways to test whether these similarity judgments reflect how humans understand meaning. Existing benchmarks typically rely on static, expert-labeled pairs with coarse Likert-style annotations, limiting scalability and making it difficult to obtain consistent, high-confidence annotations. We introduce Aligned Machine, an interactive, public-facing platform that transforms broad participation into a continuously growing Human-Aligned Similarity Benchmark (HASB) for evaluating semantic alignment in embedding-based systems. Participants complete a cognitively-natural, forced-choice task, comparing two pairs of items (text or images) and selecting which pair is more similar. This comparative design is accessible to non-experts, yields clean preference data, and supports robust aggregation. We report results from an initial deployment with 115 participants contributing 2,875 total labeled pairs and 872 unique pairs. Using HASB, we evaluated 12 widely used embedding models and found only partial alignment between embedding similarity and human judgments. Text-only models consistently achieve higher overall agreement than multimodal models, while multimodal performance varies systematically by task type: alignment is strongest for image-image comparisons, weaker for image-text comparisons, and lowest for text-text comparisons. Aligned Machine is designed to evolve over time as participation grows, supporting longitudinal analysis, domain-specific studies, bias detection, and measurement of concept drift across model versions. By combining accessible public participation with rigorous comparative evaluation, HASB provides a scalable and accountable framework for auditing semantic alignment in embedding models.

[184] Learning from Natural Images Untangles Number Sense cs.AI | cs.CG | cs.CV | cs.LG | q-bio.NCPDF

Yechan Cho, Hyeonsu Lee, Emma Deneuville, Jaeson Jang

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉学习如何改变神经网络中的数感表征，提出几何框架追踪“数量（numerosity）”与“比例（proportion）”两个子空间随学习的变化。发现自然图像预训练虽不显著改变单元级数量选择性和基本比较表现，但能增大数量与比例子空间夹角、降低重叠，从而提升二者冲突时的鲁棒读出。进一步表明监督预训练并非必需，自监督学习和合成图像训练也能促成数量与比例的功能分离。

Details

Motivation: 现代视觉语言模型的数值认知仍具挑战，而数感可在未明确数值学习的动物和深度网络中出现；论文关注视觉学习是否仅触发自发数量选择性，还是能进一步塑造更稳健、功能独立的数量与比例表征。

Result: 未训练和预训练网络具有相似单元级数量选择性和基本比较表现；自然图像预训练增大数量与比例子空间夹角并减少重叠，支持冲突条件下更鲁棒的解码。直接操纵这些几何性质可影响解码准确性和功能独立性；摘要未提及具体benchmark或SOTA对比。

Insight: 创新点在于用几何框架刻画数量与比例表征的学习变化，并将子空间夹角/重叠与冲突下读出性能及功能独立性联系起来。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是揭示结构化视觉学习可在无显式数值训练下精炼自发数感，且监督标签或自然图像并非必要条件，自监督与合成图像训练也可促成数量分离。

Abstract: Numerical cognition is a simple yet fundamental ability that remains challenging for modern vision-language models. Meanwhile, number sense is already present in numerically naïve animals, suggesting that it can emerge without explicit numerical learning. Deep neural networks provide a related observation, with numerosity-selective units appearing after training on unrelated natural-image tasks and even before any training. However, these findings leave unresolved whether visual learning contributes beyond the spontaneous emergence of numerical selectivity. Human development provides a clue, as children become better at making judgments in which numerosity and proportion conflict. Here, we introduce a geometric framework to trace how representations of these two quantities change across learning. Although untrained and pretrained networks exhibited similar unit-level numerosity selectivity and basic comparison performance, natural-image pretraining increased the angle between numerosity and proportion subspaces while reducing their overlap, supporting more robust readout under conflict. Direct manipulation of these geometric properties further demonstrated their functional contributions to decoding accuracy and functional independence. Importantly, conventional supervised pretraining was not essential for quantity separation, which also emerged through self-supervised learning without category labels and synthetic-image training without natural-image exposure. Together, our findings suggest that structured visual learning can refine spontaneously emerging number sense into robust and functionally independent quantity representations without explicit numerical training.

Abstract: Diagnosis of congenital heart disease (CHD) requires understanding the spatial relationships between cardiac structures, which clinicians assess by scrolling through CT or cardiac magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) slices and rotating 3D reconstructions. Although automated methods have advanced cardiac segmentation, identifying congenital heart defects from volumetric scans remains an open challenge. The two existing approaches rely on handcrafted diagnostic rules limited to static anatomical abnormalities. We introduce CHD-Bench, the first benchmark for interactive CHD diagnosis, defining a multi-label task and evaluation protocol over 247 CT and MRI scans from three cohorts covering 38 CHD types. We also present CHD-Harness, open-source software that runs a VLM in a 3D Slicer environment through 22 tools, manages each examination, and records the resulting diagnosis and supporting views. We evaluate off-the-shelf medical, open weight, and closed weight VLMs and compare them with a 3D CNN classifier and published rule-based results. [186] Keep or Roll Back? Out-of-Loop Judges for Self-Improving VLA and End-to-End Embodied Policies cs.AI | cs.CV | cs.ROPDF

Zilin Huang, Zhengyang Wan, Kangan Qian, Zihao Sheng, Sikai Chen

TL;DR: 论文提出 SealedLoop，一个用于自改进具身策略的评估框架：在改进闭环之外引入“密封评委”，改进器无法看到或针对其优化；只有密封评委上的序贯检验确认增益时才保留新策略，并以有界概率控制误保留更差策略。作者在 LIBERO 操作、NAVSIM 驾驶和真实车辆实验中验证了闭环内评委可能误判增益，而闭环外独立证据更可靠。

Details

Motivation: 现有具身策略自改进通常由同一闭环内训练或选择的评委决定是否保留新策略，其证据与策略不独立，容易把“评委错误”或“迁移差距”误判为真实提升；论文关注如何可信地判断策略是否真正变好。

Result: 在 LIBERO 操作中，成功分类器在一代内把 196 个失败中的 65 个误判为成功；在 NAVSIM 驾驶中，闭环内涨点的策略在未见城市变差，即使闭环内评委完全准确也会保留 8/15 个坏策略；使用密封检验后驾驶闭环得到比自身 critic 更好的策略（PDMS 0.674 对 0.649，起点 0.638），并在已知密封增益的配对中接受 11/12 个真实增益、拒绝全部 59 个损失；真实车辆盲测中，强化版本在全部 6 对中都比起点驶入弯道更远，但二者均未完成弯道。

Insight: 创新点在于将策略改进闭环与评估证据分离，用闭环外密封评委和序贯检验决定保留或回滚，并区分 judge error 与 transfer gap。客观上看，其价值是把自改进中的“进步判定”从可被优化影响的闭环内信号，转为独立、可统计控制的证据，为可信具身策略自改进提供了评估基础。

Abstract: Self-improvement is now a practical recipe for embodied policies: a policy collects experience, is retrained, and is kept when a judge rates it higher. However, the judge is usually trained or chosen inside the same loop, so its evidence is not independent of the policy. We introduce SealedLoop, an evaluation framework that adds a sealed judge: an evaluator outside the loop that the improver cannot see or optimize against. A new policy is kept only when a sequential test on the sealed judge confirms the gain, with a bounded probability of keeping a worse one. Running the same loop under each judge, we separate two sources of a false gain, which we call judge error (the loop’s judge is wrong on its own data) and transfer gap (a real gain there does not carry over to unseen conditions). We evaluate on LIBERO manipulation, NAVSIM driving, and a full-scale real vehicle. In manipulation, a success classifier accepted 65 of 196 failures in one generation. In driving, policies that gain on the loop’s own frames lose in an unseen city, so even an exact in-loop judge would keep 8/15 of them. When the sealed test decides, a driving loop built to improve ends with a better policy than under its own critic (0.674 against 0.649 PDMS, from 0.638), and on driving pairs with known sealed gain the test accepted 11 of 12 real gains and none of 59 losses. In a real-world field experiment on a published improvement step, the reinforced version drove further into a curve than the version it started from in all 6 blind pairs, though neither completed it. These results show that evidence from inside the loop, however accurate, is not enough to decide whether a policy has improved. SealedLoop offers a foundation for trustworthy self-improvement of embodied policies, in which progress is judged by evidence the improving system cannot influence.

[187] Budget, Readout or Reasoning? Where Small Reasoning Models Lose Value in Auction Bidding cs.AI | cs.CL | econ.GNPDF

Mahbod Khaleti, Seyed Reza Tavakoli, Baran Hoseini, Alireza Noori, Amir Amini

TL;DR: 论文把eBay成交价和AuctionNet模拟广告市场的出价景观（各候选出价的预估获胜概率曲线）交给6个4B–14B参数的开源推理模型，再在留出拍卖上与精确计算器的出价对比打分，从而按来源分解价值损失：模型自身有两类失败，一是即使自己的推理轨迹中已把最优选项排第一仍跳过它，二是错误答案偏向菜单上最高出价；操作者选择也有两类损失，即token上限截断推理、推理块尚未闭合就索取答案。论文还提出审计机制：如果出价与轨迹中的数字不一致，就用计算器的出价覆盖它。

Details

Motivation: 广告平台和交易市场正让基于推理语言模型的智能体根据出价景观直接选价，但小模型在何处、因何损失价值并不清楚；若能区分是模型推理能力不足，还是token预算、答案读取方式等使用选择造成，就能针对性改进。

Result: 在768个强制token、pay-your-bid计价下，错误答案使竞标者损失计算器出价所获盈余的7–17%，算术错误很少；若允许模型在4096 token内自行停止，5个模型能恢复计算器相对无推理模型领先优势的91–100%；先闭合推理块再取答案，最弱模型在eBay从42%升至84%、AuctionNet从24%升至81%；审计并覆盖不一致出价后，768 token下恢复率从42–85%提升到84–100%（嵌套交叉验证），且该设置可从eBay迁移到AuctionNet。

Insight: 创新点在于把端到端性能差距归因分解为“预算（token上限）、读取（readout时机）、推理（模型自身偏差）”三类，并用“与轨迹数字不一致即覆盖”的轻量审计把模型错误与使用错误分开；值得借鉴的是“偏向高出价”的规模性偏差与列表位置无关且采样无法消除，以及闭合推理块这类读取规范能带来巨大提升，说明很多损失源于使用方式而非模型能力。

Abstract: Ad platforms and marketplaces show bidders a bid landscape, the estimated chance of winning at each candidate bid, and increasingly let agents built on reasoning language models choose the bid. We give six open reasoning models of 4B to 14B parameters such landscapes, with win curves from eBay closing prices and from a simulated advertising market (AuctionNet), and score their bids on held-out auctions against an exact calculator. Because the agent is handed the estimates, the lost value can be split by cause. Two failures belong to the models. They pass over the best option even when the values written in their own trace already rank it first, and their wrong answers lean toward the highest bid on the menu. The lean is about the bid’s size, not its place in the list, and it survives sampling. At 768 forced tokens on eBay, under pay-your-bid pricing, the wrong answers cost bidders 7-17% of the surplus the calculator’s bids earn. Arithmetic errors are rare. Two further losses come from operator choices. A token cap cuts traces short: allowed to stop on their own within 4,096 tokens, five models recover 91-100% of the calculator’s lead over a model that does no reasoning. Asking for the answer while the reasoning block is still open misreads it: closing the block first lifts the weakest model from 42% to 84% on eBay and from 24% to 81% on AuctionNet. Finally, an audit that flags bids disagreeing with the trace’s own numbers, and overrides them with the calculator’s bid, raises the share of the calculator’s lead recovered at 768 forced tokens from 42-85% to 84-100% on eBay under nested cross-validation, and the same reader settings transfer from eBay to AuctionNet.

[188] vSFT: Learning to Verbally Tune Agents at Test Time cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Qishuai Wen, Rui Chen, Haiwen Li, Zhiyuan Huang, Qiwen Gu

TL;DR: 论文提出 vSFT（verbally Supervised Fine-Tuning）框架，用于在测试时通过自然语言反馈对 LLM agent 进行权重更新。该框架让 supervisor 根据 agent 的失败轨迹生成语言监督，再由 hypernetwork 将其转换为低秩权重更新，从而形成闭环调优。作者验证了这种测试时参数内演化方法优于上下文内方法及主流强化学习方法。

Details

Motivation: 现有 agentic RL 虽可 on-policy 改进 LLM agent，但 rollout 昂贵且奖励稀疏，难以用于测试时适配。论文希望结合自然语言密集反馈与测试时权重更新，解决该限制。

Result: 使用 Qwen3-4B 时，vSFT 在 ALFWorld 上成功率分别比 GRPO 和 Reflexion 高 8.7% 和 13.9%，在 WebShop 上分别高 4.8% 和 18.2%，优于这些基线方法。

Insight: 创新点在于用 hypernetwork 将 supervisor 的语言监督转化为低秩权重更新，实现测试时 agent 与 supervisor 的闭环自我调优。其核心思想是学习“如何适应模型”，而不仅是得到一个已适配模型，为可自我改进的 agent 提供了新路径。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly improved via agentic reinforcement learning, which can be on-policy but suffers from expensive rollouts and sparse rewards, limiting its application at test time. In this paper, we present a verbally Supervised Fine-Tuning (vSFT) framework that enables the conjunction of dense feedback in natural language and weight updates at test time. Specifically, our vSFT forms a closed loop between an agent and a supervisor via a hypernetwork, in which the supervisor generates verbal supervision from the agent’s failed trajectories, and the hypernetwork converts that supervision into low-rank weight updates to tune the agent. We validate that this test-time in-parameter evolving process outperforms both its in-context counterparts and prevalent reinforcement learning methods. In particular, our vSFT using Qwen3-4B outperforms GRPO and Reflexion by 8.7% and 13.9% in success rate on ALFWorld, and by 4.8% and 18.2% on WebShop, respectively. We believe that learning how to adapt a model, rather than an adapted model, offers a promising path towards more capable agents that improve themselves.

[189] From Shared Patterns to Individual Evidence: Two-Stage Reasoning for Emerging Entities in Temporal Knowledge Graphs cs.AI | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Jinchuan Zhang, Lei Gao, Hongyu Jiang, Chong Mu, Chenang Cai

TL;DR: 论文面向时序知识图谱中新出现实体的链接预测，提出两阶段推理框架 INSPIRE：第一阶段用可用性感知的共享模式预测器，融合全候选目录中事件更新的记忆并进行查询侧与目录侧协同掩码；第二阶段冻结该预测器，训练轻量级个体证据适配器，结合文本语义先验和相关加权长程事件统计。方法还引入支持与角色平衡的共答监督、先验几率去偏和软残差对齐，并按端点角色分解评估、跟踪纵向新兴答案队列。

Details

Motivation: 新实体在时序知识图谱中从零样本首次出现演化为稀疏、突发的小样本轨迹；现有模式迁移方法缺少全目录事件记忆，在线记忆模型存在端点角色不对称并损害未见答案排序，且稀疏早期交互具有长程分散与局部冗余问题。

Result: 在基于 ICEWS 和 GDELT 的四个基准上，INSPIRE 相比最强基线将 Emerging MRR 提升 16.73%-88.83%；相对配对的第一阶段预测器，EA 进一步将 Emerging MRR 提升 8.28%-32.63%、Answer-new MRR 提升 7.81%-41.24%、队列宏平均 MRR 提升 9.64%-39.94%。

Insight: 创新点在于将共享模式学习与个体证据适配解耦的两阶段设计，以及全候选目录事件记忆、角色/支持平衡监督和去偏对齐机制；评估上按端点角色分解并跟踪新兴答案队列，能更细粒度衡量新实体推理能力。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graphs (TKGs) continually introduce emerging entities whose interaction histories evolve from zero-shot debuts into sparse, bursty few-shot trajectories. However, current paradigms are fragmented: pattern-transfer methods lack catalog-wide event memory, whereas online memory models suffer from endpoint-role asymmetry that degrades unseen-answer ranking. Furthermore, sparse early interactions exhibit both long-range dispersion and local redundancy under skewed distribution. To address these challenges, we propose INSPIRE (Integrating Shared Patterns and Individual Evidence for Reasoning), a two-stage forecasting framework over a known catalog. In Stage I, INSPIRE learns an availability-aware shared-pattern predictor that fuses event-updated memory across the full candidate catalog while applying coordinated query- and catalog-side masking. Subsequently, Stage II freezes this predictor and optimizes a lightweight individual-evidence adapter (EA) combining textual semantic priors with correlation-weighted long-range event statistics under support- and role-balanced co-answer supervision with prior-odds debiasing and soft residual alignment. In addition, we decompose evaluation by endpoint roles and track longitudinal emerging-answer cohorts via query-micro and answer-entity macro metrics. Across four benchmarks based on the ICEWS and GDELT event datasets, INSPIRE improves Emerging MRR by 16.73%-88.83% over the strongest baselines. Relative to paired Stage I predictors, EA further increases Emerging MRR by 8.28%-32.63%, Answer-new MRR by 7.81%-41.24%, and cohort macro MRR by 9.64%-39.94%, gains attributed to semantic priors and candidate-level score competition suppressing mismatched established competitors without zero-support prior shortcuts. The official code is publicly available at https://github.com/KcAcoZhang/INSPIRE.

[190] Agentic-Level Adversarial Purification: Revisiting Diffusion Purifiers on Native-Resolution Agentic Inputs cs.CR | cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Yan Lin, Zhongliang Guo, Jingyu Lin, Daizong Liu

TL;DR: 论文首次在智能体层面研究对抗净化：以基于Qwen3-VL-8B的视觉语言智能体为对象，在原生分辨率、多元素页面输入上重新评估扩散净化器，并考虑知道智能体模型但不知道防御的灰盒攻击者。作者发现把智能体输入当作分类器输入来处理会误导评估与设计，并系统分析了原生分辨率净化、干净代价匹配和鲁棒性来源。

Details

Motivation: 视觉语言智能体常处理原生分辨率的多元素页面，单个扰动图片或少量文本笔画即可改变决策，且开放模型使内容提供者可构造此类扰动，因此即插即用净化器是自然防线。现有扩散净化器只在分类器或自然图像的固定分辨率上设计与评估，智能体防御评估也未测试扩散净化器。

Result: 将输入缩放到扩散模型分辨率会使Divide-and-Conquer净化器（DC）在TextVQA上的干净代价从4.0升至35.7点。在网店页面上，DC对网页强化扰动保持85.0%的灰盒鲁棒准确率，而在同等干净代价下廉价输入变换最多只有40.0%；鲁棒性主要来自全局噪声水平，输入估计的掩码和噪声水平没有额外增益，结论在留出集和第二个VLM上仍成立。

Insight: 创新点在于首次将对抗净化提升到智能体层面，在原生分辨率多元素输入、灰盒威胁和匹配干净代价下重新审视扩散净化器，并揭示分类器式评估与设计的误导性。客观看，论文强调了分辨率失配、全局噪声机制和防御评估协议的重要性，并给出面向智能体防御的三项要求。

Abstract: Vision-language agents act on native-resolution renders of multi-element pages, where one perturbed photo or a few altered text strokes can redirect the decision. Any content provider can craft such a perturbation on the open model behind the agent, so a plug-in purifier is the natural first line of defense. Yet diffusion purifiers were designed and evaluated at the fixed resolution of the diffusion model on classifier or natural-image benchmarks, and defense evaluations for agents test JPEG, blur and noise but no diffusion purifier. We present the first study of adversarial purification at the agentic level, for an agent built on Qwen3-VL-8B: purifiers run at native resolution on multi-element inputs, face grey-box attackers who know the agent’s model but not the defense, and are compared at matched clean cost. We show that treating agentic inputs as classifier inputs misleads both evaluation and design. Resizing the input to the resolution of the diffusion model, as existing purifiers do, raises the TextVQA clean cost of the Divide-and-Conquer purifier (DC) from 4.0 to 35.7 points. On web-shop pages, DC keeps 85.0% grey-box robust accuracy against web-hardened perturbations, where cheap input transformations at matched clean cost keep at most 40.0%, and this robustness comes from the global noise level: input-estimated masks and noise levels add none. These findings hold on a disjoint held-out set and on a second VLM and yield three requirements for defenses, we release the protocol.

[193] MulCLIP: A Multi-level Alignment Framework for Enhancing Fine-grained Long-context CLIP cs.IR | cs.CVPDF

Chau Truong, Hieu Ta Quang, Zhenzhen Liu, Dung D. Le

TL;DR: 论文提出MulCLIP，一个端到端的多级对齐框架，用于增强CLIP对长而细粒度图像-文本描述的理解。该方法不依赖region proposals，而是直接利用自然长文本结构，通过全局对比、词-块重建和子描述聚合块对齐等多级训练信号，实现不同文本粒度的视觉-语言对齐。

Motivation: CLIP等视觉-语言模型主要基于短caption训练，难以处理长且细节丰富的描述；现有方法借助region proposals将视觉区域与句子对齐，但部署成本较高。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP excel at image-text alignment but struggle with long, detailed descriptions due to training on short captions. Recent methods address this limitation using region proposals to align visual regions with sentences, but at substantial deployment cost. We present MulCLIP, an end-to-end multi-level alignment framework that directly exploits natural long-text structure without region proposals. Instead of simply stacking objectives, MulCLIP aligns different textual granularities through compatible training signals: global contrastive alignment for long captions and short summaries, Word-Patch Reconstruction over locally calibrated features for within-sample word-patch semantics, and Subcaption-Aggregated Patch alignment for context-rich subcaption grounding. [194] Attributing Agentic Failure in Reasoning Vision-Language Models with Interactive 2D Environments cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Details

Motivation: 现有智能体基准只报告成功率，混淆了读错环境、未理解机制运作、操作顺序错误、无法从错误中恢复等不同失败原因，而这些失败需要不同改进方案；因此需要一个能对失败原因进行归因的受控评测环境。

Result: 在50个迷宫、3个视觉语言模型共150个episode中仅解出6个；50个迷宫中有45个没有任何模型解出；包含switch-gate机制的episode均未被解出，显示当前模型在该类组合机制任务上表现很差。

Insight: 创新点在于将环境机制模块化并可独立操控，同时保持渲染、动作空间和评分函数固定，从而支持受控归因分析；用BFS基于位置、朝向、库存和机制状态联合空间给出精确最优动作数，为难度设定和未完成episode评分提供客观标准。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks report a success rate that confounds distinct causes of failure: misreading the environment, never working out how components of the environment operates, operating the components in the wrong order, and failing to recover from a mistake, each of which calls for a different fix. We present an early preview of MultiNet v2.0-Gridworld - an interactive maze environment built for attribution rather than ranking. Four different types of mechanisms, namely key-door pairs, switch-gate pairs, distractors and decoys, and ordered combinations of these mechanisms, can be added, modified, or removed independently while the renderer, the action space, and the scoring function stay fixed. A breadth-first search over the joint state of position, facing direction, inventory, and mechanism state gives every instance an exact optimal action count, which sets its difficulty and grades unfinished episodes. We fix the evaluation protocol on 15 held-out mazes over 540 episodes, then evaluate three vision-language models on 50 mazes. They solve 6 of 150 episodes, 45 of the 50 mazes were not solved by any model, and no episode containing a switch-gate mechanism was solved.

[195] State Space Attention: Learning Context-Dependent Memory Retention cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Ege Çakar, Cengiz Pehlevan

TL;DR: 论文提出状态空间注意力（SSA），学习保留一组有界的过去表示，并用标准注意力读取；可学习评分器会根据新上下文和记忆其余部分动态重打分记忆块，联合训练评分器与读取器。实验在检索、变量跟踪、多跳推理和语言建模上评估，显示SSA以常数推理内存取得接近全注意力的长上下文能力。

Details

Motivation: 标准注意力的KV缓存随上下文长度线性增长，长上下文推理需要决定序列模型应保留哪些信息。论文认为这种保留策略不应固定，而应学习为上下文相关的有界记忆保留。

Result: 在检索和推理基准上，SSA在多数任务上匹配全注意力或仅差几个点；在每个长上下文检索任务上远超相同可读预算的Transformer，并匹配或超过约4倍预算的Transformer，同时使用常数推理内存。小语言模型规模下与全注意力的最大剩余差距在RULER；论文还预训练和后训练400M参数模型并分析成本缩放。

Insight: 创新点在于将记忆保留建模为可学习选择：评分器动态重打分记忆块，固定上下文Transformer成为总是保留最近token的特例；通过recovery生成监督信号联合训练，并因使用标准注意力读取而可与KV cache eviction方法叠加。客观看，其价值在于用学习型有界记忆兼顾常数推理内存与标准注意力兼容性。

Abstract: Standard attention’s key-value cache grows linearly with context length. Bounding it raises the question of what a sequence model should retain for long-context reasoning. We argue this choice should be learned, and introduce State Space Attention (SSA), which learns to keep a bounded set of past representations and reads them with standard attention. A learned scorer rescores every chunk against newly arriving context and the rest of memory. A fixed-context Transformer is then the special case that always keeps the most recent tokens. We train the scorer and reader jointly, either moving gradually from a differentiable soft weighting of memories to hard retention, or with hard retention from the start. To produce signal for the scorer, we generate supervised samples during training, a technique we call recovery. We compare SSA with recurrent models, bounded-memory attention methods, and full-attention Transformers across retrieval, variable tracking, multi-hop reasoning, and language modeling. On our retrieval and reasoning benchmarks, SSA matches or comes within a few points of full attention on most tasks, far outperforms a Transformer with the same readable budget on every long-context retrieval task we evaluate, and matches or outperforms one with about 4 times that budget, while using constant inference memory. The largest remaining gap to full attention at small language model scale is on RULER. SSA makes a fixed number of tokens useful over a longer context, and since it reads memory with standard attention, we propose that it can be stacked with KV cache eviction methods. We also pretrain and post-train 400M-parameter models and measure how SSA’s training and inference costs scale with context length and model size. Together, these experiments examine the capabilities and limitations of learned memory retention for long-context sequence modeling.

[196] DLaR: Dynamic Stochasticity Control for Latent Reasoning in Large Language Models cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Xiaocheng Luo, Peng Xu, Zaifu Zhan, Xiangyu Duan

TL;DR: 论文提出DLaR框架，用于大语言模型潜在推理中的动态随机性控制，以解决确定性推理缺乏探索、均匀随机性和无约束扰动破坏推理连贯的问题。DLaR在GRPO强化学习框架下设计了token级自适应随机性调制ASM和训练级训练感知随机性调优TAST，形成分层探索-利用策略。实验表明其在多个数学推理基准上取得最高平均性能。

Details

Motivation: 现有潜在推理方法多为确定性推理，难以探索多样推理路径；简单注入随机性又存在所有步骤均匀随机次优、无约束扰动破坏推理连贯两大局限。论文旨在实现更精细、可控的随机性注入，在探索与推理稳定性之间取得平衡。

Result: 在4个分布内和3个分布外数学推理基准上，DLaR在不同模型规模下取得最高平均性能；在Llama-3.2-1B上达到27.9%/50.6%的Pass@1/Pass@8，在Qwen2.5-1.5B上达到46.8%/68.2%。

Insight: 论文宣称创新点在于token级根据置信-熵融合信号动态调节扰动强度，并在训练级按课程计划逐步衰减基础随机性，从而形成双层探索-利用策略。客观看，其创新主要是将随机性控制细粒度化并同时覆盖推理步与训练阶段，以缓解潜在推理中探索不足与连贯性受损的矛盾。

Abstract: Latent reasoning enables LLMs to operate in continuous embedding space, yet existing methods suffer from deterministic inference that precludes exploration of diverse reasoning paths. While recent approaches inject stochasticity to restore exploration, we identify two fundamental limitations: \textit{uniform stochasticity} across all steps is suboptimal, and unconstrained perturbations disrupt reasoning coherence. We propose Dynamic Stochasticity Control for Latent Reasoning (DLaR), a framework with two complementary mechanisms that operate at different temporal granularities within the GRPO reinforcement learning framework. At the token level, Adaptive Stochasticity Modulation (ASM) dynamically adjusts perturbation intensity at each latent step based on a confidence-entropy fusion signal, increasing exploration when the model is uncertain and preserving stability when confident. At the training level, Training-Aware Stochasticity Tuning (TAST) progressively decays the base stochasticity level following a curriculum schedule. Through this dual-level design, DLaR establishes a hierarchical exploration-exploitation strategy that operates at both token and training levels. Extensive experiments on four in-distribution and three out-of-distribution mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that DLaR achieves the highest average performance across model scales, reaching 27.9%/50.6% Pass@1/Pass@8 on Llama-3.2-1B and 46.8%/68.2% on Qwen2.5-1.5B. Code: \href{https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/DLaR}{https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/DLaR}.

[197] Single-Database Rewards Are Hackable: Counterfactual Execution for Trustworthy Text-to-SQL RL cs.LG | cs.CL | cs.DBPDF

Shreshth Rai, Rishii Kumar Singh, Aksh Aggarwal

TL;DR: 论文指出，在text-to-SQL智能体的强化学习训练中，如果只依据查询在单个数据库上运行的结果是否与参考答案匹配来发放奖励，这个奖励机制就容易被“侥幸匹配”欺骗：错误SQL可能因为数据缺少能暴露其bug的行而恰好匹配。为此，论文提出反事实数据库（CF）奖励：在若干隐藏的、由代码生成的数据库副本上执行最终查询，只在全部结果匹配时给奖（严格形式），或按匹配比例给奖（均值形式）。论文在1302条合成审计问题上验证了该方法抑制reward hacking的效果，并在3.35B模型上用GRPO做受控多seed对比，评估其对训练表现的影响。

Details

Motivation: 单数据库的结果匹配只能覆盖部分数据分布，错误查询可能因数据缺少暴露bug的行而侥幸匹配参考答案，导致RL奖励被hack，从而训练出不可靠的text-to-SQL智能体。

Result: 在1302条合成审计问题上，严格形式将获得全额奖励的buggy查询比例从29.5%降至16.8%，将reward hack从54.1%降至1.9%，且未拒绝任何一条2847条正确查询；家族盲生成器生成的副本效果相当。在3.35B模型、每个实验组3个seed的GRPO对照实验中，均值形式未提升域内准确率（8.4% vs 11.7%，因为两种奖励仅在5-7%的rollout上不一致），但在Spider上提升1.6分；单个seed会反转这两个结论，说明单seed的奖励比较具有误导性。

Insight: 创新点在于用代码生成的隐藏数据库副本进行反事实执行，据此构造抗hack的严格或均值奖励信号，并通过多seed受控实验揭示单seed奖励比较不可靠；客观来看，其贡献主要在奖励设计与严谨的实验方法论，准确率收益有限，价值更多体现在可靠性上。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for text-to-SQL agents pays a query when its result matches the reference on one database. However, a wrong query can match by luck when the data lack the rows that expose its bug. We study a counterfactual-database (CF) reward that also runs the final query on a few hidden, code-generated copies of the database and pays only if all results match (strict form) or by the share matched (mean form). On 1,302 synthetic audit questions, the strict form lowers the share of buggy queries paid in full from 29.5% to 16.8% and of reward hacks from 54.1% to 1.9%, rejecting 0 of 2,847 correct queries; copies from a family-blind generator do about as well. In a controlled GRPO comparison on a 3.35B model with three seeds per arm, training on the mean form did not improve in-domain accuracy (8.4% vs 11.7%), because the two rewards disagreed on only 5-7% of rollouts; on Spider it gained 1.6 points. One seed reverses both signs, so single-seed reward comparisons mislead. Code: https://github.com/RishiiGamer2201/counterfactual-sql-rewards ; data, models, demo: https://huggingface.co/counterfactual-sql

[198] Strong Starts, Simple Recipes: Unified Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation from Multimodal Specialists cs.LG | cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出 Uni-MOPD，一种面向多模态专家的统一多教师在线策略蒸馏方法。它将学生模型初始化视为关键设计变量：先对同一基座模型微调得到的各领域专家做免训练均匀合并，再用短程 MOPD（均匀领域采样与全词表反向 KL）进行精炼。在 5 个多模态领域、19 个基准上验证了强初始化与简单蒸馏配方的有效性。

Result: 在 5 个多模态领域、19 个基准上，均匀合并在未训练时已达到基座模型 200 次 MOPD 更新后的分数，短程蒸馏后可与更高分的搜索式初始化相当；短程 MOPD 提升所有领域，且在领域教师仍领先处增益最大。均匀领域采样与全词表反向 KL 匹配或超过所评估的采样策略和散度选择；相同预算下优于 Open-MOPD，训练速度为其发布代码的 3.3 倍。

Insight: 创新点在于把学生初始化作为设计变量，提出免训练的同源专家均匀合并作为强起点，并配合极简的短程 MOPD 配方（均匀领域采样、全词表反向 KL，无需调度、逐 token 加权或截断超参）。客观看，其可借鉴之处是用强初始化减少能力整合负担，并验证简单训练策略在强起点下已足够。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) integrates separately trained domain specialists into a single unified model. Existing methods focus on refining the distillation procedure while initializing the student from a base model or a multi-domain SFT checkpoint, which leaves capability integration largely to distillation itself. Instead, we treat the starting point as a design variable and propose Unified MOPD (Uni-MOPD), which pairs a strong start with a simple recipe: a training-free uniform merge of specialists from the same base model initializes the student, and short MOPD with uniform domain sampling and full-vocabulary reverse KL refines it. Across five multimodal domains and 19 benchmarks, our study establishes three findings. (1) The starting point matters more than subsequent training choices: before any training, the uniform merge already matches the score that the base model reaches after 200 MOPD updates, and after short distillation it matches higher-scoring, search-based starts. (2) Short MOPD recovers most of the capabilities the merge leaves unintegrated; it improves every domain, with the largest gains where the domain teacher still leads. (3) From a strong start, uniform domain sampling and full-vocabulary reverse KL match or exceed every evaluated sampling strategy and divergence, without schedules, per-token weighting, or truncation hyperparameters. Uni-MOPD outperforms Open-MOPD at the same training budget while training 3.3 times faster than its released code. We will release the training framework, specialist checkpoints, and unified student.

Details

Motivation: 历史木建筑中的木材因长期环境作用和解剖区域差异形成复杂化学退化模式，但其形成机制尚不明确；同时不同树种和服务历史的标本难以直接比较，需要建立共同的光谱表示基础。

Result: 仅使用光谱数据时，ChemoMAE在验证标本上的平均LLA高于基线方法；聚类得到的空间结构与木材解剖及表面暴露的预期效应一致，并据此提出了内部和表面退化的机制假设。

Abstract: In historical timber buildings, multiple environmental factors act over centuries on wood whose chemical composition varies across anatomical regions, producing complex patterns of degradation. Understanding how timber members degrade is important for the effective conservation and restoration of historical buildings, yet the mechanisms underlying these patterns remain unclear. To investigate these mechanisms, we used self-supervised learning to establish a common basis for comparing specimens from timber members that differ in wood species and service history. We examined transverse sections of 49 wood specimens from several historical buildings in Japan using near-infrared hyperspectral imaging. ChemoMAE, a self-supervised Transformer, learns pixelwise spectral representations through masked denoising that exploits multicollinearity among spectral variables. Clustering these representations yields spatial maps without predefined chemical-state categories. Because anatomy and degradation can produce spatially continuous regions, we formulated Local Label Agreement (LLA) to quantify agreement among labels assigned to neighboring pixels. Using spectra alone, ChemoMAE yielded higher mean LLA than baseline methods on validation specimens. We then trained ChemoMAE anew and fitted cluster centers across all specimens. Spatial structures consistent with wood anatomy and the expected effects of surface exposure emerged in the resulting maps. By cross-referencing regional spectra and spatial distributions across specimens in light of wood anatomy, service histories, and prior studies, we proposed models of internal and surface degradation as mechanistic hypotheses. This exploratory AI for Science framework gives researchers a basis for linking latent patterns learned through self-supervision with domain knowledge when investigating how material states form.

[200] When Every Rollout Fails: Splicing Learner-Conditioned Options into Long-Horizon Agent RL cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Haowen Hou, Yicheng Liu, Zhen Huang, Jiaqi Wang

TL;DR: 论文关注长程智能体强化学习中基于分组可验证奖励的GRPO等方法在“全失败组”上无法获得有效策略梯度的问题。提出SPLICE：对每个全失败组，在当前策略失败轨迹上在线选择拼接点，由提议模型生成若干有界局部选项，并用当前策略重复续写估计选项价值，将正价值选项转为回报加权局部目标来训练当前策略。方法在SWE-bench Verified和BrowseComp-Plus两类长程任务、两种策略族上验证。

Details

Motivation: 长程智能体任务中稀疏奖励导致采样轨迹全为零奖励，组相对方法缺乏基于奖励的策略梯度；而用强模型提供完整轨迹成本高且引入长程离策略历史。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified和BrowseComp-Plus的四种设置上均优于GRPO和全轨迹指导基线，较GRPO提升2.8–9.8个百分点；在初始失败的SWE任务上将GRPO的“毕业率”提升10.8个百分点，并降低提议模型计算开销。

Insight: 创新点在于从失败轨迹中在线构造局部、学习者条件化的训练目标，用有界选项替代昂贵完整轨迹，并通过当前策略续写估计选项价值。客观上，其价值在于缓解全失败组的稀疏奖励困境，同时减少离策略偏差和强模型调用成本。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards often yields all-fail groups on long-horizon agent tasks: every sampled trajectory receives zero reward, leaving group-relative methods without a reward-based policy gradient. A common remedy supplies complete solutions from a stronger model, but full trajectories are expensive and expose the learner to long off-policy histories. We introduce SPLICE, which constructs local training targets from failed current-policy trajectories online. For each all-fail group, a proposal model selects a splice point in a current-policy trajectory and generates several bounded options from that point. We estimate each option’s value using repeated current-policy continuations, convert positive estimates into a return-weighted local target, and fit the current policy on the options. We evaluate SPLICE across two policy families on two long-horizon agent tasks, Software Engineering with SWE-bench Verified and Deep Research with BrowseComp-Plus. SPLICE outperforms GRPO and full-trajectory guidance baselines in all four settings, improving over GRPO by 2.8–9.8 percentage points. On initially failed SWE tasks, it raises GRPO’s graduation rate by 10.8 percentage points. Further experiments show that SPLICE improves performance while reducing costly proposal-model computation.

Motivation: 探索多模态模型是否能像语言模型用词思考一样用生成图像辅助推理，检验生成辅助图像是否提升视觉问答表现，并弥补该方向缺乏系统实证的问题。

Abstract: Language models think in words; then multimodal models might think in images. We test whether a generated helper improves the answer to a visual question. Danil Kostromin, Andrey Sakhovskiy, Semen Budennyy

TL;DR: 论文提出CypherMAS，一个在固定多智能体图（链、星、二分、环）中隔离通信与路由能力的合成基准。智能体需将加密消息路由给持有匹配解码器的远程节点，由于最优路径已知，可测量冗余LLM调用并追踪路由失败。论文用该基准评估11个4B至753B开放权重模型，发现小模型易在路由和工具使用上失败，大稠密模型随图直径增加而退化，成功运行也常超过最小LLM调用数。

Details

Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统评测通常将通信与开放域任务耦合，难以判断失败究竟来自推理、工具误用、消息丢失还是请求遗忘；同时缺乏对通信执行和相对已知最优路径的路由冗余进行隔离测量。

Result: 在11个4B至753B开放权重模型上，小模型在路由和工具使用上失败；27B以上稠密模型随图直径增大而性能退化；成功运行所需LLM调用数超过理论最小值。论文未报告传统任务SOTA分数，主要给出通信可靠性的诊断性结论。

Insight: 创新点在于用固定多智能体图和加密消息/匹配解码器任务隔离通信与路由，并利用已知最优路径量化冗余LLM调用和路由失败。客观看，其价值是把通信可靠性识别为多智能体系统独立的扩展瓶颈，并提供可控诊断工具。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models (LLMs) distribute reasoning across specialized agents that must exchange messages and invoke tools reliably. Existing evaluations typically couple communication with open domain tasks, making it difficult to determine whether a failure arises from reasoning, malformed tool use, lost messages, or forgotten requests. Prior benchmarks do not isolate communication execution or measure routing redundancy against a known optimal path. We introduce CypherMAS, a synthetic benchmark that isolates communication and routing in fixed multi-agent graphs. Agents in chain, star, bipartite, or ring graphs route cyphered messages to remote peers holding the matching decoders; since the optimal route is known, redundant LLM calls can be measured and routing failures may be easily tracked. Across eleven open-weight models from 4B to 753B, small models fail at routing and tool use, dense models above 27B degrade with diameter, and successful runs exceed minimum LLM calls. These findings identify communication reliability as a distinct scaling bottleneck for multi-agent systems and provide a controlled diagnostic for complex multi-agent systems. The source code is freely available: https://github.com/Andoree/CypherMAS.

[203] Greedy Decoding Hides Good Plans: Sample-and-Verify Planning for Vision-Language Driving Models cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Zhipeng Bao, Wenjie Zhao, Yiping Li, Haohua Que, Qianwen Li

TL;DR: 论文分析了基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的端到端驾驶规划器通常只使用单条贪心解码轨迹进行评估的问题，发现贪心解码会忽略模型输出分布中更优的轨迹（例如应起步时模型却反复选择等待）。为此提出 Sample-and-Verify（SaV）：保持 VLM 规划器冻结，采样多条候选轨迹，并用一个从真实未来轨迹训练的轻量验证器综合轨迹运动学、候选集一致性、似然和自我运动特征来挑选最终轨迹。

Details

Motivation: 现有 VLM 驾驶规划器仅根据单条贪心解码轨迹来评估，但贪心解码可能错过输出分布中更好的轨迹，说明规划误差可能来自“轨迹选择”而非“轨迹生成”；作者希望在不重新训练规划器的前提下改善轨迹选择。

Result: 在 WOD-E2E 验证集上，三个 VLM 规划器的 ADE 和 FDE 均降低约 15%；RFS 变化较小，两个规划器在 val-456 上略有下降，Alpamayo-1.5 在官方测试集上 RFS 从 7.74 提升到 7.81，同时 ADE@5s 下降 11%。在 NAVSIM 上，三个规划器的 ADE@4s 降低 10%～24%，但 PDMS 变化不一致。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于把“轨迹生成”和“轨迹选择”解耦：冻结 VLM 规划器，通过多候选采样以及学习式验证器来挑选轨迹；验证器融合运动学、候选一致性、似然和自我运动等特征。其客观价值在于无需额外训练规划器即可显著提升位移精度，揭示了贪心解码的评估偏差，并为 VLM 规划器提供了一种通用、轻量的后处理选择框架。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly adopted for end-to-end driving planning, yet their performance is typically evaluated based on a single greedily decoded trajectory. However, greedy decoding may overlook better trajectories within the model’s output distribution. Experiments on WOD-E2E and NAVSIM reveal a consistent performance gap between greedily decoded trajectories and the best sampled candidates, showing that planning errors can arise from trajectory selection, rather than trajectory generation alone. On WOD-E2E, when the ego vehicle should start moving, the model often assigns probability to different possible start times, causing greedy decoding to repeatedly favor waiting and produce a stationary plan even though moving trajectories are available. Motivated by these findings, we propose Sample-and-Verify (SaV), which keeps the VLM planner frozen and samples multiple candidate trajectories. A lightweight verifier is trained from ground-truth futures to estimate the relative quality of these candidates. It uses trajectory kinematics, candidate-set agreement and likelihood, and ego-motion features to select the final trajectory. On WOD-E2E, SaV reduces ADE and FDE by approximately 15% across three VLM planners on the validation set. Changes in RFS are smaller: two planners show slight decreases on val-456, while Alpamayo-1.5 improves from 7.74 to 7.81 RFS on the official test set as ADE@5s decreases by 11%. On NAVSIM, SaV reduces ADE@4s by 10-24% across three VLM planners, while PDMS changes are less consistent. These results show that trajectory selection can substantially improve displacement accuracy without additional planner training. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/hzxbzp/sample-and-verify.

Abstract: When the lead robot cannot continue, what should it send the teammate that must finish the route? We replay a frozen receiver at each contact under alternative messages. Across 3,920 autonomous departures in 106 environments, adding the view before the lead’s last action block to its executed actions raised success by 7.51 percentage points (pp) per RxR handoff and 8.54 pp per SAGE handoff. Per departure the gains were 2.23 and 1.44 pp, respectively. The lead continuing in simulation defines the empirical full-history ceiling (Solo). At contact, the lead had accumulated a median of 41 frames. Sending one frame when available recovered 24% of the full-history advantage over actions alone in SAGE and 30% in RxR. Over an estimated progress cue in SAGE, the image rescued 100 routes and reversed 54 successes. Near-goal receivers finished outside less often (63.1% to 51.7%, post hoc). In secondary comparisons the earlier view outperformed the contact view by 6-7 pp per handoff. Exact-route specificity and image gains at controlled starts with mostly unknown progress or outside the goal region remained unresolved. Gains were detectable from about 7 kB per handoff.
