[205] SEEK: Self-Evolution through Execution Knowledge for Agentic Robot Manipulation cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Zeyang Guan, Qihao Zhao, Rui Gong, Zhang Haitian, Yalun Dai

TL;DR: 论文提出 SEEK，一个面向机器人操作的智能体框架，使机器人在不更新底层多模态基础模型的情况下，跨多次尝试保留并复用执行知识。SEEK 用自演化程序化策略 SEPP 将操作编码为可执行程序，并根据当前观测决定动作；同时用持久动作-效果库 PAEB 记录动作实际效果，包括失败尝试的经验，从而在已有程序适用时直接执行、遇到未覆盖条件时借助历史证据修订程序。

Details

Motivation: 现有机器人虽能利用多模态基础模型和执行反馈在部署中适应，但任务结束后会丢失工作上下文中的执行知识，导致下次尝试重复犯错、耗时不变。仅回放过去动作又不足以应对当前环境变化，因此需要同时保留可适应当前场景的程序化策略和动作效果证据。

Result: 在 RoboDojo 的 42 个任务上，经过 10 次尝试保留知识后，SEEK 平均任务分数从 27.0 提升到 54.4，成功率从 19.7% 提升到 48.0%。在 34 个任务上使用 GPT-6.2 Astra 时，SEEK 五次尝试后达到 80.1，而同一模型直接执行仅为 49.6，并减少 77.6% 的模型请求。

Insight: 创新点在于将跨回合执行知识表示为可执行程序，并用持久动作-效果库记录真实动作效果及失败经验，使固定模型也能通过程序修订实现自我进化。客观来看，其可借鉴之处是结合程序化策略复用与动作效果记忆，在减少模型调用的同时提升长程机器人操作的经验积累能力。

Abstract: General-purpose multimodal foundation models enable robots to adapt during deployment from observations and execution feedback, without task-specific demonstrations or training. However, the robot retains what it learns only in the model’s working context. Once the task ends, it loses this execution knowledge, such as how to carry a bowl between two grippers. On the next attempt at the task, the robot may take just as long and repeat the same mistakes. To avoid this repetition, robots must retain execution knowledge across attempts. Yet replaying past actions is insufficient, because their effects depend on the current conditions. Procedures that determine actions from the current scene address this, but when they meet conditions that earlier attempts did not cover, revising them requires the measured effects of earlier actions. We therefore propose SEEK, an agentic framework that retains both, enabling robots to improve across attempts while keeping the underlying model fixed. To adapt retained knowledge to the current scene, SEEK adopts a Self-Evolving Programmatic Policy (SEPP) that encodes manipulation as executable procedures and determines each action from current observations. As the robot executes these procedures, SEEK records the measured effects of its actions, including those from failed attempts, in a Persistent Action-Effect Bank (PAEB). Where a retained procedure applies, it acts without a model request; where it meets a condition it does not cover, this evidence guides its revision. On RoboDojo, knowledge retained over ten attempts raises SEEK’s average task score from 27.0 to 54.4 and its success rate from 19.7% to 48.0% across all 42 tasks. On 34 of these tasks with GPT-6.2 Astra, SEEK reaches 80.1 after five attempts, against 49.6 for the same model acting directly, and uses 77.6% fewer model requests. https://zeyangguan.github.io/SEEK-Agentic-Robot-Manipulation/

Lisiyu Pan, Ting Huang, Zeyu Zhang, Hao Tang

TL;DR: 论文提出 iMFPO，一种面向具身控制的一步生成式策略优化框架。它通过解耦速度学习与漂移感知速度对齐，让共享的区间条件 actor 同时支持瞬时速度监督和区间平均动作生成，并在训练中结合 JVP 校正速度回归、奖励驱动策略目标与冻结 PPO 教师指导。推理时直接通过区间平均速度预测生成动作，实现无需迭代积分的一步控制。

Motivation: 基于流的生成式策略虽然动作建模表达能力强，但在奖励驱动策略优化下实现高效一步生成仍很困难。在线强化学习中动作分布不断变化，会导致瞬时速度学习与区间平均动作生成之间出现不一致。

Result: 在 Go2、G1、H1 和 Spot 上，零潜变量评估的平均回报分别为 35.69、34.44、416.23 和 29.86。在 Go2 上，相比 64 步 RFPO，动作生成延迟从 15.57 ms 降至 0.41 ms。

Abstract: Flow-based generative policies offer expressive action modeling for embodied control, yet achieving efficient one-step generation under reward-driven policy optimization remains challenging. In particular, evolving action distributions during online reinforcement learning may introduce inconsistencies between instantaneous-velocity learning and interval-averaged action generation. To address this challenge, we propose iMFPO, a one-step generative policy optimization framework with decoupled velocity learning and drift-aware velocity alignment. A shared interval-conditioned actor supports instantaneous-velocity supervision and interval-averaged action generation. JVP-corrected velocity regression is jointly optimized with reward-driven policy objectives, with action-level guidance from a frozen PPO teacher during training. At inference, iMFPO directly generates actions through interval-averaged velocity prediction, achieving one-step control without iterative flow integration. Across Go2, G1, H1, and Spot, zero-latent evaluation yields mean returns of 35.69, 34.44, 416.23, and 29.86. On Go2, action-generation latency drops from 15.57 ms to 0.41 ms relative to 64-step RFPO. Details

Motivation: WAM 需要同时判断“在哪里交互”和“机器人与物体如何运动”，但全量微调和 LoRA 等标准适配方法不会显式选择与这些操作能力相关的参数。论文旨在将适配集中在功能特化的注意力头上，以更高效地提升未见任务上的操作性能。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 微调时未见任务上，HeadLight 达到 62.28% 成功率，高于全量微调的 57.89% 和 LoRA 的 58.66%，且仅更新 1.1% 参数。消融显示移除 Motion Heads 后成功率从 57.89% 降至 0.94%，移除 Next-Interaction Heads 后降至 40.61%，均显著大于移除等量随机头，并在多种 WAM 架构和真实机器人设置中有效。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) are a promising new paradigm of robot manipulation policies that jointly generate future video and actions. Successful manipulation requires both identifying where to interact and modeling how the robot and objects move. Yet standard adaptation methods, such as full fine-tuning and LoRA, do not explicitly select parameters aligned with these manipulation-specific capabilities. Motivated by this challenge, we discover two functionally specialized families of attention heads: Next-Interaction Heads, which localize upcoming grasp-and-release targets, and Motion Heads, which track movement across frames. Furthermore, we introduce HeadLight, a parameter-efficient refinement method that jointly adapts Next-Interaction Heads and Motion Heads. On RoboTwin tasks unseen during fine-tuning, HeadLight achieves 62.28% success, compared with 57.89% for full fine-tuning and 58.66% for LoRA, while updating only 1.1% of parameters. Inference-time ablations further validate the functional importance of these heads: ablating Motion Heads reduces manipulation success from 57.89% to 0.94%, while ablating Next-Interaction Heads lowers it to 40.61%, both substantially more than ablating equally many random heads. Abstract: Spatial intelligence concerns both scene structure and its temporal evolution. Yet a semantically correct description can conceal a quantitative error: recognizing that objects are approaching does not determine their closing speed or whether they will meet. Such errors are critical in collision avoidance, trajectory tracking, and reactive grasping, where actions depend on motion magnitude, timing, and relative geometry. We study quantitative kinematic understanding in multimodal large language models (MLLMs): resolving motion magnitude, rate, reference-frame dependence, and future spatial evolution from visual evidence. Abstract: Image-goal navigation asks a robot to reach the viewpoint of a target photograph without a map or goal pose. Abstract: Effective robotic manipulation calls for multimodal memory and rapid adaptation to shifts in contact dynamics. However, many existing policies, which retain either visual or wrench history, may miss key cues from the other modality. Moreover, even with wrench memory, fixed policies can struggle when contact conditions shift at deployment. To this end, we introduce M$^{2}$AdaptVLA, a vision-language-action model that integrates visual and wrench memories with predictive test-time adaptation. For visual memory, the model uses test-time training (TTT) to encode observation history in fast weights. Each entry in the bounded TTT bank combines the corresponding frame’s features with a readout from these weights, preserving cues that predate the oldest retained frame. For wrench memory, a context gate controls how much the action expert draws on wrench history as it jointly denoises future actions and wrenches. Real-world experiments show that the TTT bank outperforms visual memory baselines on progress tracking, scene recall, and demonstration following. On contact-sensitive tasks, the context gate yields higher success rates than direct injection or gated cross-attention. Predictive test-time adaptation improves robustness to shifts in contact dynamics. On eight tasks requiring visual and wrench memory, M$^{2}$AdaptVLA achieves a mean success rate of 82.71%, a 31.46% absolute gain over the strongest baseline. Abstract: Instruction-following robots need navigation policies that reach high accuracy and remain inexpensive to train and to run on a single graphics processing unit (GPU). The most accurate vision-language navigation policies for continuous environments rely on language models with 4 to 8 billion parameters, long observation histories, and large auxiliary corpora. Two properties of the standard training setup limit smaller policies. Imitation labels cover the states that an expert visits, whereas the running policy reaches states that its own errors produce. Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略控制高效但面对陌生环境常需额外人类演示，而推理智能体虽能发现解决方案，却存在反复推理与修复成本高的问题。论文希望借鉴人类“推理+实践”的学习方式，实现无需额外演示的递归自我提升。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 上，独立成功率从 11.0% 提升到 84.1%，混合成功率从 30.4% 提升到 90.5%；在 RoboTwin C2R 上取得类似增益并降低 agentic 成本，在 Unitree G1-D 上混合成功率达 95.0%，还展示了自主任务获取和跨具身 harness 迁移。

Insight: 核心创新是 harness 与策略的双向共同进化闭环：P2H 用策略失败驱动编码智能体搜索并固化 Code、Code+ICL、Code+Policy 方案，H2P 用验证轨迹监督训练并以 CPD 偏好成功修正。客观看，其可借鉴点在于将失败反馈同时用于技能/路由演化和策略蒸馏，从而减少人工演示和重复推理成本。

Abstract: Robots should adapt to unfamiliar environments and acquire new skills through interaction, then execute those skills efficiently with practice. Vision-language-action policies provide efficient control, but often need additional human demonstrations to handle unfamiliar settings; reasoning agents can discover solutions using broad prior knowledge and robot-control APIs, but repeated deliberation and repair are costly. Inspired by human learning through reasoning and practice, we propose RoboEvo$^2$, a framework for recursive harness-policy co-evolution. In Policy-to-Harness (P2H) evolution, policy failures guide a coding agent to search complementary Code, Code+ICL, and Code+Policy routes, consolidating verified solutions into reusable skills, routing strategies, and experience memory. In Harness-to-Policy (H2P) evolution, verified trajectories provide supervised training, while Counterfactual Preference Distillation (CPD) teaches the policy to favor successful corrections over failures from the same starting state. The improved policy provides stronger motor skills for subsequent search, while its remaining failures redirect harness evolution, closing the reciprocal loop. Through recursive co-evolution without additional human demonstrations, RoboEvo$^2$ improves standalone success from 11.0% to 84.1% and hybrid success from 30.4% to 90.5% on LIBERO-PRO, with similar gains and reduced agentic costs on RoboTwin C2R. Further experiments demonstrate autonomous task acquisition, cross-embodiment harness transfer, and 95.0% hybrid success on the Unitree G1-D. Our models and code will be publicly released.

[214] SOLVE: Sparse, Ordered Keyposes Make LLM Agents Versatile and Efficient Robot Manipulator cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Xiang Li, Hongyu Zhou, Ya-Li Li, Tao Li, Rui Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 SOLVE，一种无需训练、通用且高效的 LLM 机器人操作 agent 策略。多模态 LLM 每次调用生成稀疏、有序的 6-DoF 末端执行器关键位姿序列，由运动规划器插值执行；agent 观察执行结果后继续生成下一段，并能利用执行反馈反思和蒸馏任务规则。方法通过稀疏深度查询将关键位姿 grounding 到场景中，兼顾长程快速运动与精细操作。

Details

Motivation: 现有 LLM 机器人操作 agent 存在推理成本高、逐步密集控制效率低以及工具瓶颈等问题。论文希望利用遥操作演示中操作本质稀疏的特点，用少量关键位姿替代密集动作序列，从而提升效率与通用性。

Result: SOLVE 在 RoboDojo 上达到 74.5%、在 RoboLab 上达到 84.5%，取得 SOTA 性能；平均每 episode 仅 1.7 美元，比之前的密集控制 agent 策略少 12 倍。真实机器人上，同一 agent 在 16 个挑战性零样本任务中达到 88% 成功率（85 次中成功 75 次）。

Insight: 创新点在于将稀疏、有序的 6-DoF 关键位姿作为 LLM 与底层运动规划之间的高效接口，并让 agent 自适应决定关键位姿数量和间距。方法无需训练，结合稀疏深度查询抗噪 grounding 与执行反馈规则蒸馏，客观上有助于降低推理和控制成本并提升跨任务适应性。

Abstract: LLM agents have made remarkable progress in robot manipulation, yet they suffer from expensive inference, dense step-by-step control, and tool bottlenecks. We introduce SOLVE, a training-free, versatile and efficient agent policy: a multimodal LLM agent writes a sparse, ordered sequence of 6-DoF end-effector keyposes per call, observes the result, and writes the next, while a motion planner interpolates between them. The agent decides the number and spacing of keyposes for both fast long-range motion and fine-grained manipulation. This granularity matches the sparsity of teleoperated manipulation: most demonstrations in DROID can be compressed into a few keyposes instead of dense actions. SOLVE grounds keyposes with sparse depth queries answered by noisy sources, such as multi-view triangulation, stereo, or monocular depth, and copes with that noise itself. It also improves from execution feedback, reflecting on its own rollouts and distilling a few rules per task. SOLVE reaches state-of-the-art performance of 74.5% on RoboDojo and 84.5% on RoboLab, with only $1.7 per episode on average, 12x less than previous dense-control agent policies. On a real robot, the same agent achieves an 88% success rate (75 of 85 episodes) over 16 challenging zero-shot tasks. Our project page is at https://serenos.github.io/solve-website.

[215] Real2SimLab: A Unified Platform for Real-to-Simulation Transfer cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Yongchang Zhang, Jie Huang, Yunhe Ni, Ningyu Zhou, Guangyu Zhao

TL;DR: 论文提出 Real2SimLab，一个面向真实到仿真迁移（Real-to-Simulation Transfer）的统一平台。该平台通过运行方法生成的仿真程序，并调用各基准原有的评分器对输出仿真进行打分，从而统一评估不同方法；同时提供训练数据与奖励标注生成流程，并通过过滤基准中已有视频来避免数据污染。目前平台支持 6 个基准、47 个 coding agents 和 5 种非 agent 方法，并开源了平台及近 300 篇相关论文的研究地图 Awesome-Real2Sim。

Details

Motivation: 真实到仿真迁移领域已有多个基准，但各基准在输入、交付物和评分流程上定义不一致，导致在 M 个基准上评估 N 个方法需要 N×M 次集成，评估成本高且难以统一比较。

Result: 平台目前已支持 47 个 coding agents 和 5 种非 agent 方法，并在 6 个基准上运行评估；同时开源了 Real2SimLab 平台和 Awesome-Real2Sim 研究地图，未提及具体性能指标或 SOTA 结果。

Insight: 主要创新在于构建统一评估平台，复用各基准原始 scorer 来降低集成成本；并提供可生成训练数据和奖励标注的 pipeline，通过过滤基准中已有视频避免数据污染，形成评估与训练数据构建的闭环。

Abstract: Real-to-simulation transfer, which builds simulations from real-world observations, has long been studied in research and industry. Recently, coding agents have become capable of generating simulation programs from video recordings, and several benchmarks have been proposed to evaluate them. However, each individual benchmark defines its own inputs, deliverables, and scoring pipeline. This leads to $N \times M$ integrations when evaluating $N$ methods on $M$ benchmarks. To this end, we present Real2SimLab, a unified platform for real-to-simulation transfer. It evaluates a method by running its written program and scoring the output simulation with the original scorer of each benchmark. Moreover, Real2SimLab provides a pipeline to create training data and reward annotations for real-to-simulation methods. Our pipeline carefully avoids data contamination by filtering out the video recordings that exist in benchmarks. For each video recording, a program is first produced, and then our pipeline computes its reward with the same code that Real2SimLab uses for evaluation. So far, it supports 47 coding agents and 5 non-agent methods on 6 benchmarks. Both the platform and Awesome-Real2Sim, our research map of nearly 300 real-to-simulation papers, are open source, and we invite the Real2SimHub community to build them together.

[216] Cairn: Object-Centric 3D Trajectory Priors for Steering Frozen VLAs cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Agelos Kratimenos, Kostas Daniilidis, Evangelos Chatzipantazis

TL;DR: 论文提出 Cairn，让视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）保持冻结，在测试时利用夹爪中所持物体的学习到的 3D 轨迹对其进行引导。一个小型先验模型读取场景点云、所持物体及其抓取位姿和指令，采样出到释放点的 SE(3) 轨迹以绕开障碍并通过狭窄间隙。先验与 VLA 构成双向反馈的分层规划器，VLA 的意图运动选择并重塑轨迹，所选轨迹又引导 VLA 的去噪过程，从而在不改动 VLA 权重的情况下将其预测动作拉向下一路点。

Details

Motivation: VLA 是能力较强的底层控制器，但会把物体去向与训练时的布局绑定：当物体被移动或障碍阻挡时，它们会把物体带到错误位置或撞上障碍。在新布局上微调需要针对每个布局的机器人数据，并会侵蚀模型已有技能。

Result: 在 1,941 个含位移物体和障碍的留出 LIBERO 场景上，Cairn 将冻结的 π0.5 成功率从 24.5% 提升到 95.1%，超过在我们训练布局上微调的 π0.5，且不损害标准 LIBERO 表现。在第三方 LIBERO-PRO 基准上，成功率从 27.3% 提升到 38.0%，在其引导的片段上从 54.4% 提升到 81.1%。在真实 Franka 上，仅用仿真训练的先验引导冻结的 DROID π0.5 绕过障碍并到达机械臂可达极限处的放置点。

Insight: 创新点在于冻结 VLA 并在测试时用物体为中心的 3D 轨迹先验进行引导，避免新布局微调的数据需求与技能退化；先验与 VLA 构成双向反馈的分层规划器，兼具轨迹选择与去噪引导；通过抓取映射将预测动作拉向路点，实现无需改动 VLA 权重的即插即用式增强。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are capable low-level controllers, but they tie where objects go to the layouts they were trained on: when objects are moved or obstacles block the way, they carry them to the wrong place or into collisions. Fine-tuning on new layouts needs robot data for each one and erodes skills the model already has. We introduce Cairn, which keeps the VLA frozen and steers it at test time along learned 3D trajectories of the object in its gripper. A small prior reads point clouds of the scene and the held object, along with its pose when grasped and the instruction, and samples SE(3) trajectories up to its release that lead it around obstacles and turn it to fit through gaps. The prior and the VLA form the two levels of a hierarchical planner in which feedback runs both ways: the VLA’s intended motion chooses and reshapes the trajectories, and the chosen one guides the VLA’s denoising. Its predicted actions, mapped to the held object’s poses through the grasp, are pulled toward the next waypoint, while the VLA’s weights stay untouched. Across 1,941 held-out LIBERO scenes with displaced objects and obstacles, Cairn raises the success rate of a frozen π0.5 from 24.5% to 95.1%, above that of a π0.5 fine-tuned on our training layouts, without hurting standard LIBERO. On the third-party LIBERO-PRO benchmark, it raises success from 27.3% to 38.0%, and from 54.4% to 81.1% on the episodes it steers. On a real Franka, with a prior trained purely in simulation, it guides the frozen DROID π0.5 around an obstacle and to a placement at the limit of the arm’s reach, where it otherwise fails. All code, models and data will be made public.

[217] Building a Unified Inline Code Editing Model for GitHub Copilot cs.SE | cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Microsoft CodeAI Team, Microsoft VS Code Team, Julia Gong, Benjamin Liggett, Shengjie Ma

TL;DR: 论文为 GitHub Copilot 提出一个统一的编辑器内联代码编辑模型，用统一的锚定 diff patch 序列表示（含位置、删除行、新增行）在单一输出空间中同时预测光标处补全、附近编辑和远距离编辑三类编辑；模型分两阶段训练，先统一 next edit 与 long-distance edit，再加入代码补全，并通过伪标注与编辑历史回放合成多编辑监督信号。

Details

Motivation: 原有系统需按固定顺序分别调用补全模型、附近编辑模型和远距离编辑模型，三者输出格式各异，无法在单次响应中表达跨远距离位置的编辑意图，且每次建议可能需多次串行模型调用，并总是优先补全而非纠正。论文旨在用单一模型统一三类编辑预测，减少调用次数与延迟。

Result: 在 GitHub Copilot 的在线 A/B 实验中，第一阶段相对三模型基线将显示时间降低 6.77%、输出 token 减少 58.93%；第二阶段 3-in-1 模型配合客户端改造，相对 2-in-1 模型加补全模型的组合，将每用户被忽略建议数减少 10.06%。

Insight: 创新点在于用带锚点的 diff patch 序列作为统一输出空间，使一次响应可包含多处编辑并可缓存后续编辑；同时提出基于伪标注与编辑历史回放从单编辑数据合成多编辑监督，以及共享二进制 LLM 评分奖励、patch 有效性分类体系和 grader boosting 来对齐数据过滤、奖励与评测。

Abstract: AI-assisted coding ranges from fully agentic coding to inline code suggestions in the editor. Inline suggestions require predicting a developer’s most likely next edit from an editing session. The next edit may lie at the cursor, in nearby lines, or farther away, and one editing intent can span several of these. The preceding system queries separate completion, nearby edit, and long-distance edit models, each with its own output format, in a fixed sequence; it thus cannot express intents spanning distant locations in one response, can require several sequential model calls per suggestion, and prioritizes completion even when a correction would serve better. We present a unified model that predicts all three edit types in one output space: a sequence of anchored diff patches specifying locations, deleted lines, and added lines. This representation permits multiple edits per response, so the client can serve later edits from a cache without further model calls. We train this model in two phases, first unifying next edit and long-distance edit prediction, then adding code completion. We synthesize multi-edit supervision from single-edit data via model pseudolabeling and edit history playback. We use a binary LLM-graded reward, a patch validity taxonomy shared by data filtering, reward, and evaluation, and grader boosting for targeted failure modes. Phase 2 also adapts how the client processes and presents model outputs. In controlled online A/B experiments in GitHub Copilot, Phase 1 significantly reduces time to display by 6.77% and output tokens by 58.93% relative to the three-model baseline. In Phase 2, the 3-in-1 model and client adaptations reduce dismissed suggestions per user by 10.06% relative to the 2-in-1 model paired with the completion model. Throughout this report, we also share our methodology and learnings in evaluation, training, and end-to-end model and system design.

[218] Interpreting Speckle Effects Through Neuron Interventions eess.IV | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Shubham K. Singh, Hare Krishna

TL;DR: 论文利用可解释性方法分析合成孔径雷达中的斑点噪声如何影响预训练视觉模型。作者在SARMAE中识别对斑点噪声选择性响应的神经元，并通过激活干预测试其与场景重建的关系。研究发现，最响应斑点噪声的神经元并非模型用于恢复场景的关键神经元，第一MLP中的另一组64个神经元对重建恢复更重要。

Details

Motivation: 斑点噪声是SAR成像固有的乘性噪声，会降低预训练SAR模型性能。论文希望从模型内部神经元层面理解其影响，并检验校正特定神经元激活能否改善重建。

Result: 在SARMAE中，38个斑点选择性神经元恢复干净值并不能改善重建；跨层激活修补显示第一MLP的恢复和注入效应最大，其中另一组64个神经元可恢复留出场景44.1%的相关性损失。保持斑点选择性神经元为噪声值时仍保留96.5%的增益，而保持三个解码器头会移除37.4%的增益；在Sentinel-1和Capella图像上同样64个神经元可改善重建，地物分类中仅恢复整个第一MLP有明显增益。

Insight: 创新点在于用神经元干预和激活修补解释斑点噪声在预训练SAR模型内部的作用机制，而不是仅做外部性能分析。其可借鉴之处是区分“对噪声敏感”与“对恢复有用”的神经元，并通过跨层和模块级干预定位实际功能子网络。

Abstract: Speckle, the multiplicative noise inherent to synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imaging, reduces the performance of pretrained SAR models. We use interpretability methods to examine its effects inside pretrained vision models. We identify neurons that respond selectively to speckle and test whether correcting their activations improves reconstruction. We study SARMAE, an Earth observation model pretrained on a million-scale SAR dataset. Synthetic scenes with known reflectivity allow us to measure recovery precisely. The model contains 38 speckle-selective neurons that track speckle’s value distribution. Restoring these neurons to their clean values does not improve reconstruction. Activation patching across layers shows that the first MLP has the largest restoration and injection effects. Within this layer, a different set of 64 neurons recovers 44.1% of the correlation loss on held-out scenes. When the speckle-selective neurons are held at their noisy values, 96.5% of this gain remains, while holding the values of three decoder heads removes 37.4%. In an ImageNet-pretrained MAE, restoration is most effective in the third MLP block. On manipulated Sentinel-1 and Capella images, the same 64 neurons also improve reconstruction, whereas for land-cover classification, only restoring the entire first MLP yields a clear gain. The neurons that respond most to speckle are therefore not the ones the model uses to recover the scene.
